📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 三分之二的病例有相关信息。对于乙型肝炎病毒,37.7%报告了当前的治疗或管理,而53.6%报告了转诊或正在进行评估;对于丙型肝炎病毒,这些比例分别为33.3%和54.7%。 [10.33321/cdi.2026.50.047]。
  • 该研究提供了特定时期内美国 乙型肝炎病毒 感染 患病率 和 发病率 的最新估计,但所提供的证据中没有详细说明具体的数字结果。 [10.3350/cmh.2026.0817]。
  • 结果表明,区域差距缩小,空间聚集性下降,东部和中部地区标准差椭圆向东南移动,发病率重心向东南移动351.25公里。 [10.1038/s41598-026-53490-8]。
  • 四个 (5%) 样本在两次 ID-NAT 检测中均无反应;所有捐献者的 HBV DNA 均得到证实,通常处于无法量化的水平,并且全部呈抗 HBc 阳性;没有发现一致的分子特征,但在 BCP、核心和 PreS1 中检测到异质的、病例特异性的 突变。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Information was available for two-thirds of cases. For HBV, 37.7% reported current treatment or management, while 53.6% reported referral or assessment in progress; for HCV, these proportions were 33.3% and 54.7%.
  • The study provided updated estimates of hepatitis B virus infection prevalence and incidence in the United States during the specified period, though specific numerical findings were not detailed in the supplied evidence.
  • A total of 102 patients were included (TAF n = 82; ETV n = 20), mean age 72 ± 10 years, 72% male; serum creatinine showed a non-significant increasing trend during TDF therapy (β = +0.008 mg/dL/year, p = 0.24) and after switching (β = +0.009 mg/dL/year, p = 0.07), with no significant difference between groups; serum phosphate declined during TDF in the ETV group (β = -0.175 mg/dL/year, p = 0.002) and showed a significant increase after switching (β = +0.111 mg/dL/year, p = 0.031) while the TAF group remained stable; total cholesterol showed a significant immediate increase at the time of switch (+18.3 mg/dL, 95% CI 11.0-25.6, p < 0.001), with no significant difference between TAF and ETV.
  • Results showed narrowing regional disparities, declining spatial aggregation, and a standard deviation ellipse located in eastern and central regions with a southeastward shift, alongside the incidence gravity center moving 351.25 km southeast.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 非洲CDC推出基于WHO 2024指南的乙肝管理工具包,旨在改善非洲6400万慢性乙肝患者的诊断与治疗;同时,中国、孟加拉国、尼日利亚等国加强消除母婴传播和疫苗接种,而新型疗法与诊断技术取得突破。
  • 非洲CDC发布乙肝操作工具包,覆盖社区至医院五级护理,并配套移动应用,以应对仅6%诊断率和1.6%治疗率的严峻挑战 [news-632d4c14704c1528]。
  • 中国北京启动乙肝消除健康运动,5岁以下儿童感染率降至0.30%,但仍有7500万慢性感染者,占全球三分之一 [news-a1912f67bec17f0e]。
  • 孟加拉国圆桌会议指出,全国有1000万乙肝和丙肝患者,消除肝炎需社会参与,重点解决就业歧视和母婴传播问题 [news-8336271b1c8cab32]。
  • 3期临床试验显示,反义寡核苷酸药物bepirovirsen使约20%慢性乙肝患者实现功能性治愈,低基线HBsAg者治愈率达26% [news-1e18e98cd33aa2ef]。
  • Africa CDC launched a WHO 2024-based operational toolkit to improve diagnosis and treatment for 64 million chronic hepatitis B patients in Africa.
  • Africa CDC released a hepatitis B toolkit covering five levels of care with a mobile app, addressing only 6% diagnosis and 1.6% treatment rates in Africa [news-632d4c14704c1528].
  • Beijing health campaign reported 0.30% HBV prevalence in children under five, but 75 million chronic infections remain, one-third of global cases [news-a1912f67bec17f0e].
  • Bangladesh roundtable highlighted 10 million living with hepatitis B and C, calling for social participation to tackle employment discrimination and mother-to-child transmission [news-8336271b1c8cab32].
  • Phase 3 trials showed bepirovirsen achieved functional cure in ~20% of chronic hepatitis B patients, with 26% cure in those with low baseline HBsAg [news-1e18e98cd33aa2ef].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of Medical Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/jmv.71070  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 98, 8, e71070
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

病毒学受到抑制的 HIV 感染者和既往 HBV 感染者转用替诺福韦保留双重疗法后病毒学失败的风险

Risk of Virological Failure After Switch to a Tenofovir‐Sparing Dual Therapy in Virologically Suppressed People With HIV and Past HBV Infection
作者： A. Borghetti, A. Giacomelli, P. F. Salvo, Adriana Cervo, S. Colombaioni, F. Lagi, Gianmaria Baldin, C. Mussini, L. Oreni, S. Antinori
摘要中文翻译摘要 我们比较了基于替诺福韦 (TFV) 的三联疗法 (TT) 与保留 TFV 的双重疗法 (DT) 在病毒学抑制的 HBsAg−/HBcAb+ HIV 感染者 (PLWH) 中的有效性。我们模拟了一项目标试验,包括来自五所大学医院的具有 HBsAg−/HBcAb+ 血清状态的成人 PLWH,通过加权 Kaplan-Meier 估计器估计,以未抑制的血清 HIV-RNA 开始 TT,并达到 HIV-RNA 50 cp/mL 或单个 HIV-RNA ≥ 200 cp/mL。总体而言,415 名 PLWH 符合研究分析的条件:71 名(17.1%)在随访期间开始了 DT(基于 3TC:78.9%)。他们主要是男性(329 人,79.3%),中位年龄为 48 岁。在加权分析中,5 年时 VF 的估计概率为:DT 为 29.3%,TT 为 29.9%(差异:0.5%;95% CI:-28.7,+21.6)。在最长随访时间(4 年)内未检出 HBsAb 的 PLWH 子集中观察到较高的 VF 风险,独立于 治疗 组:DT 和 TT 分别为 15.5% 和 36.8%(差异:-21.3%;95% CI:-30.3,-13.8)。与继续 TT 相比,改用保留 TFV 的 DT 与 PLWH 和既往 HBV 感染者发生 VF 的风险增加无关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究比较了基于替诺福韦 (TFV) 的三联疗法与保留 TFV 的双重疗法对既往感染过 HBV(HBsAg−/HBcAb+)且病毒学受到抑制的 HIV 感染者的有效性。
设计与对象
该研究模拟了一项目标试验,包括来自五家大学医院的 415 名 HBsAg−/HBcAb+ 血清状态成人,他们开始使用未抑制血清 HIV-RNA 的三联疗法并达到抑制效果。参与者主要为男性(329 名,79.3%),中位年龄为 48 岁,71 名(17.1%)在随访期间开始接受双重治疗。
核心方法
病毒学失败的概率定义为两个连续的 HIV-RNA ≥ 50 cp/mL 或单个 HIV-RNA ≥ 200 cp/mL,通过加权 Kaplan-Meier 估计器进行估计。
主要结果
在加权分析中,双联疗法5年病毒学失败的估计概率为29.3%,三联疗法为29.9%(差异:0.5%;95% CI:-28.7,+21.6)。在 4 年时 HBsAb 检测不到的子集中,双联治疗的发生率为 15.5%,三联治疗的发生率为 36.8%(差异:-21.3%;95% CI:-30.3,-13.8)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,在该人群中,与继续三联疗法相比,改用保留 TFV 的双重疗法与病毒学失败的风险增加无关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,对于病毒学受到抑制的 HIV 感染者和既往 HBV 感染者,尤其是可检测到 HBsAb 的患者,保留 TFV 的双重疗法可能是一种可行的选择。
局限与证据强度
由于目标试验的观察模拟,证据强度中等,尽管进行了加权分析,但可能会受到残留混杂因素的影响。

Risk of Virological Failure After Switch to a Tenofovir‐Sparing Dual Therapy in Virologically Suppressed People With HIV and Past HBV Infection

Authors: A. Borghetti, A. Giacomelli, P. F. Salvo, Adriana Cervo, S. Colombaioni, F. Lagi, Gianmaria Baldin, C. Mussini, L. Oreni, S. Antinori
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT We compared the effectiveness of tenofovir (TFV)‐based triple regimens (TT) to TFV‐sparing dual therapies (DT) in virologically suppressed, HBsAg−/HBcAb+ people living with HIV (PLWH). We emulated a target trial including adults PLWH from five University Hospitals, with HBsAg−/HBcAb+ serostatus, starting a TT with unsuppressed serum HIV‐RNA and reaching HIV‐RNA 50 cp/mL or a single HIV‐RNA ≥ 200 cp/mL) were estimated through a weighted Kaplan–Meier estimator. Overall, 415 PLWH were eligible for study analysis: 71 (17.1%) started a DT (3TC‐based: 78.9%) during follow‐up. They were mainly men (329, 79.3%), with a median age of 48 years. In the weighted analysis, the estimated probabilities of VF were: 29.3% for DT and 29.9% for TT at 5 years (difference: 0.5%; 95% CI: −28.7, +21.6). A higher VF risk was seen in the subset of PLWH with undetectable HBsAb at the longest follow‐up available (4 years), independently of treatment group: 15.5% and 36.8% with DT and TT, respectively (difference: −21.3%; 95% CI: −30.3, −13.8). Switch to a TFV‐sparing DT, compared with continuing a TT, was not associated with an increased risk of VF in PLWH and prior HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study compared the effectiveness of tenofovir (TFV)-based triple regimens to TFV-sparing dual therapies in virologically suppressed people living with HIV who had past HBV infection (HBsAg−/HBcAb+).
Design and population
The study emulated a target trial including 415 adults from five University Hospitals with HBsAg−/HBcAb+ serostatus who started a triple regimen with unsuppressed serum HIV-RNA and reached suppression. Participants were mainly men (329, 79.3%) with a median age of 48 years, and 71 (17.1%) started a dual therapy during follow-up.
Core methods
The probabilities of virological failure, defined as two consecutive HIV-RNA ≥ 50 cp/mL or a single HIV-RNA ≥ 200 cp/mL, were estimated through a weighted Kaplan–Meier estimator.
Main results
In the weighted analysis, the estimated probabilities of virological failure at 5 years were 29.3% for dual therapy and 29.9% for triple therapy (difference: 0.5%; 95% CI: −28.7, +21.6). A higher risk was seen in the subset with undetectable HBsAb at 4 years, with rates of 15.5% for dual therapy and 36.8% for triple therapy (difference: −21.3%; 95% CI: −30.3, −13.8).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that switching to a TFV-sparing dual therapy was not associated with an increased risk of virological failure compared with continuing a triple regimen in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that TFV-sparing dual therapy may be a viable option for virologically suppressed people living with HIV and past HBV infection, particularly for those with detectable HBsAb.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational emulation of a target trial, which may be subject to residual confounding despite weighted analysis.

乌干达 乙型肝炎病毒 意识与疾病特定知识的相关性:对 2018 年至 2025 年发表的研究进行荟萃分析和荟萃回归

Correlates of hepatitis B virus awareness and disease specific knowledge in Uganda: a meta-analysis and meta-regression of studies published between 2018 and 2025
作者： Hussein Mukasa Kafeero, Hakim Sendagire, Dorothy Ndagire, Abdul Walusansa, Ali Kudamba, Fahad Muwanda, Mariam Namusoke, Ponsiano Ocama
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是乌干达的一个主要公共卫生挑战,尽管有国家 疫苗接种 和筛查举措,但 患病率 因地区和人群而异。了解意识和知识水平对于指导乙肝预防和控制策略至关重要。这项研究综合了乌干达不同人群对乙肝病毒的认识和疾病具体知识之间的相关性的现有证据。方法:我们系统地检索了 PubMed、非洲期刊在线 (AJOL) 和 Google Scholar,查找 2018 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的关于乌干达 HBV 认知或特定疾病知识的横断面研究。研究选择、数据提取和质量评估由两名评审员使用 Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) 分析横断面研究批判评估清单独立进行。最终综合报告中纳入了十八项符合资格标准的研究。使用 MedCalc 软件(版本 10.10)中的随机效应模型计算 HBV 认知度和特定疾病知识相关性的汇总估计,并使用 I2 统计量量化异质性。通过亚组分析和敏感性分析探讨异质性的来源。进行元回归以检查按地区、出版年份和研究人群划分的 HBV 认知趋势。使用 Begg 和 Egger 测试评估发表偏倚。统计显着性设置为 p < 0.05,所有估计值均以 95% 置信区间报告。结果:十八项研究符合纳入标准。汇总的 患病率 乙肝病毒知晓率为 75.3%(95% CI:61.5%-86.9%),不同地区和人群之间存在显着异质性(I2=99.24%)。亚组分析显示,与北部、东部和西部地区相比,乌干达中部地区的知晓率显着较高(p < 0.05),与一般人群和产前护理人员相比,医护人员的知晓率显着较高(p < 0.05)。对特定 HBV 领域的了解差异很大:与先前没有 HBV 知识的参与者相比,知识丰富的参与者对病原体、筛查服务和 疫苗接种 可用性的认识显着较高 (p < 0.05),而与至少接受部分 疫苗接种 的参与者相比,对暴露后预防和完成完整 HBV 疫苗接种 时间表的认识仍然较低 (p < 0.05)。对主要 传播 途径(包括性接触、共用针头和母婴 传播)的了解程度中等,但通过感染体液对 传播 的了解仍然很少。与教育水平较低的参与者相比,教育水平较高的参与者更有可能接种 疫苗接种 (p < 0.05),而对 疫苗 有效性持积极态度的参与者比持消极态度的参与者更有可能接种疫苗 (p < 0.05)。荟萃回归分析进一步揭示了研究期间乙肝病毒认知度整体下降的矛盾现象。结论:对乙肝病毒的认识和关键知识领域仍然存在严重差距,这突出表明在传播健康信息时需要针对当代社会动态制定替代策略。临床试验编号:不适用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是乌干达的一项重大公共卫生挑战,尽管有国家 疫苗接种 和筛查举措,但 患病率 仍因地区和人群而异。这项研究综合了乌干达不同人群对乙肝病毒的认识和疾病具体知识之间的相关性的现有证据。
设计与对象
这项荟萃分析和荟萃回归包括 18 项横断面研究,报告了 2018 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的乌干达 HBV 认知或特定疾病知识。这些研究涵盖了不同人群,包括普通人群、医护人员和产前护理人员。
核心方法
两名评审员使用乔安娜·布里格斯研究所批判性评估清单独立进行研究选择、数据提取和质量评估。使用 MedCalc 软件中的随机效应模型计算 HBV 认知度和特定疾病知识相关性的汇总估计,使用 I2 统计量量化异质性,并进行荟萃回归以检查按地区、年份和研究人群划分的趋势。
主要结果
HBV 知晓率的汇总 患病率 为 75.3%(95% CI:61.5%-86.9%),具有显着异质性(I2=99.24%)。与其他地区相比,乌干达中部地区的意识显着较高,与普通人群和产前护理人员相比,医护人员的意识显着较高(p < 0.05)。荟萃回归显示,在研究期间,人们对乙肝病毒的认识出现了矛盾的整体下降。
解释与创新
尽管对 传播 途径的认识中等,但在 HBV 认识和关键知识领域(例如暴露后预防和完整的 疫苗接种 时间表完成)仍然存在严重差距。作者将这些发现解释为强调在传递健康信息时需要针对当代一代的社会动态制定替代策略。
科研与公卫意义
了解意识和知识水平对于指导乙肝预防和控制策略至关重要。研究结果表明,有必要采取有针对性的健康教育干预措施,以解决特定的知识缺陷并提高乌干达不同人群对 疫苗接种 的吸收。
局限与证据强度
由于不同地区和人群之间观察到的显着异质性,证据强度中等。该研究设计依赖于横截面数据,这限制了因果推断,而且随着时间的推移,意识的矛盾性下降值得进一步调查。

Correlates of hepatitis B virus awareness and disease specific knowledge in Uganda: a meta-analysis and meta-regression of studies published between 2018 and 2025

Authors: Hussein Mukasa Kafeero, Hakim Sendagire, Dorothy Ndagire, Abdul Walusansa, Ali Kudamba, Fahad Muwanda, Mariam Namusoke, Ponsiano Ocama
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major public health challenge in Uganda, where prevalence varies by region and population group despite national vaccination and screening initiatives. Understanding awareness and knowledge levels is essential for guiding HBV prevention and control strategies. This study synthesized available evidence on the correlates of HBV awareness and disease specific knowledge among different Ugandan populations. METHODS: We systematically searched PubMed, African Journals Online (AJOL), and Google Scholar for cross-sectional studies reporting on HBV awareness or disease-specific knowledge in Uganda, published between January 2018 and December 2025. Study selection, data extraction, and quality appraisal were conducted independently by two reviewers using the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical Cross-Sectional Studies. Eighteen studies meeting the eligibility criteria were included in the final synthesis. Pooled estimates of HBV awareness and correlates of disease-specific knowledge were computed using a random-effects model in MedCalc software (version 10.10), with heterogeneity quantified using the I2 statistic. Sources of heterogeneity were explored through subgroup analysis and sensitivity analysis. Meta-regression was performed to examine trends in HBV awareness by region, year of publication, and study population. Publication bias was assessed using Begg's and Egger's tests. Statistical significance was set at p < 0.05, and all estimates are reported with 95% confidence intervals. RESULTS: Eighteen studies met the inclusion criteria. The pooled prevalence of HBV awareness was 75.3% (95% CI: 61.5%-86.9%), with substantial heterogeneity observed across regions and population groups (I2=99.24%). Subgroup analysis revealed that awareness was significantly higher in Central Uganda compared to the northern, eastern, and western regions (p < 0.05), and among healthcare workers compared to the general population and antenatal care attendees (p < 0.05). Knowledge of specific HBV domains varied considerably: awareness of the causative agent, screening services, and vaccination availability was significantly higher among knowledgeable participants compared to those with no prior HBV knowledge (p < 0.05), whereas awareness of post-exposure prophylaxis and completion of the full HBV vaccination schedule remained low compared to those who had received at least partial vaccination (p < 0.05). Awareness of key transmission routes including sexual contact, needle sharing, and mother-to-child transmission was moderate, though knowledge of transmission through infected body fluids remained poor. Participants who were of higher education level were more likely to take up vaccination compared to lower education level (p < 0.05), and participants with a positive attitude toward vaccine effectiveness were significantly more likely to be vaccinated compared to those with a negative attitude (p < 0.05). The meta-regression analysis further revealed a paradoxical overall decline in HBV awareness over the study period. CONCLUSION: Critical gaps still persist in HBV awareness and key knowledge areas highlighting the need for alternative strategies tailored to the social dynamics of the current generation during the delivery of health messages. CLINICAL TRIAL NUMBER: Not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major public health challenge in Uganda, where prevalence varies by region and population group despite national vaccination and screening initiatives. This study synthesized available evidence on the correlates of HBV awareness and disease specific knowledge among different Ugandan populations.
Design and population
This meta-analysis and meta-regression included eighteen cross-sectional studies reporting on HBV awareness or disease-specific knowledge in Uganda, published between January 2018 and December 2025. The studies encompassed various population groups, including the general population, healthcare workers, and antenatal care attendees.
Core methods
Two reviewers independently conducted study selection, data extraction, and quality appraisal using the Joanna Briggs Institute Critical Appraisal Checklist. Pooled estimates of HBV awareness and correlates of disease-specific knowledge were computed using a random-effects model in MedCalc software, with heterogeneity quantified using the I2 statistic, and meta-regression was performed to examine trends by region, year, and study population.
Main results
The pooled prevalence of HBV awareness was 75.3% (95% CI: 61.5%-86.9%) with substantial heterogeneity (I2=99.24%). Awareness was significantly higher in Central Uganda compared to other regions and among healthcare workers compared to the general population and antenatal care attendees (p < 0.05). Meta-regression revealed a paradoxical overall decline in HBV awareness over the study period.
Interpretation and novelty
Critical gaps persist in HBV awareness and key knowledge areas, such as post-exposure prophylaxis and full vaccination schedule completion, despite moderate awareness of transmission routes. The authors interpret the findings as highlighting the need for alternative strategies tailored to the social dynamics of the current generation during the delivery of health messages.
Scientific and public-health significance
Understanding awareness and knowledge levels is essential for guiding HBV prevention and control strategies. The findings suggest that targeted health education interventions are necessary to address specific knowledge deficits and improve vaccination uptake among different Ugandan populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the substantial heterogeneity observed across regions and population groups. The study design relied on cross-sectional data, which limits causal inference, and the paradoxical decline in awareness over time warrants further investigation.

针对乙型和丙型肝炎的增强型 监测:诊断 后临床护理的见解

Enhanced surveillance for hepatitis B and C: insights into clinical care after diagnosis
作者： Jennifer MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Nasra Higgens, Amelia. Savage, Irene Guan, Mielle Abbott, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C. Cowie
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 感染的护理服务存在已知差距,公共卫生 监测 提供了支持病例卫生服务参与的机会;然而,诊断 后的随访缺乏具体数据。
设计与对象
对 2023 年至 2024 年期间维多利亚州报告的 HBV 和 HCV 病例进行了分析,以确定 诊断 后的随访情况,包括人口统计和环境的变化。
核心方法
该研究通过分析通知病例和报告的缺乏护理联系的原因,评估了维多利亚州一项旨在提高数据可用性和支持护理联系的举措的成果。
主要结果
三分之二的病例有相关信息。对于乙型肝炎病毒,37.7%报告了当前的治疗或管理,而53.6%报告了转诊或正在进行评估;对于丙型肝炎病毒,这些比例分别为33.3%和54.7%。
解释与创新
可以通过公共卫生 监测 有效收集级联护理信息,这些系统为支持公共卫生后续行动的优先顺序提供了机会。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,增强的 监测 系统可用于支持公共卫生随访的优先顺序,并改善与病毒性肝炎病例护理的联系。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体的设计限制、偏见、混杂因素或证据强度的详细评估。

Enhanced surveillance for hepatitis B and C: insights into clinical care after diagnosis

Authors: Jennifer MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Nasra Higgens, Amelia. Savage, Irene Guan, Mielle Abbott, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C. Cowie
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: There are known gaps in care uptake for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) infection, and public health surveillance represents an opportunity to support health service engagement for cases; however, specific data are lacking regarding follow-up after diagnosis. We assessed outcomes of a Victorian initiative to enhance data availability and to support linkage to care. METHODS: Notified cases of HBV and HCV within Victoria during 2023-2024 were analysed to determine post-diagnosis follow-up, including variation by demographics and setting, and reported reasons for lack of linkage to care. RESULTS: Information was available for two-thirds of cases. For HBV, 37.7% reported current treatment or management, while 53.6% reported referral or assessment in progress; for HCV, these proportions were 33.3% and 54.7%. Reasons for gaps included loss to follow-up, cost barriers, and misapplication of HBV treatment criteria. DISCUSSION: Cascade of care information can be effectively collected via public health surveillance, and these systems represent an opportunity to support prioritisation of public health follow-up.

Question and background
There are known gaps in care uptake for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) infection, and public health surveillance represents an opportunity to support health service engagement for cases; however, specific data are lacking regarding follow-up after diagnosis.
Design and population
Notified cases of HBV and HCV within Victoria during 2023-2024 were analysed to determine post-diagnosis follow-up, including variation by demographics and setting.
Core methods
The study assessed outcomes of a Victorian initiative to enhance data availability and to support linkage to care by analysing notified cases and reported reasons for lack of linkage to care.
Main results
Information was available for two-thirds of cases. For HBV, 37.7% reported current treatment or management, while 53.6% reported referral or assessment in progress; for HCV, these proportions were 33.3% and 54.7%.
Interpretation and novelty
Cascade of care information can be effectively collected via public health surveillance, and these systems represent an opportunity to support prioritisation of public health follow-up.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that enhanced surveillance systems can be utilised to support the prioritisation of public health follow-up and improve linkage to care for viral hepatitis cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, bias, confounding, or a detailed assessment of evidence strength.

未经治疗的献血者中出现高 HBsAg 和检测不到 DNA 水平的 乙型肝炎病毒 感染:病毒机制和输血安全影响

Hepatitis B virus infection with high HBsAg and undetectable DNA levels in untreated blood donors: Viral mechanisms and transfusion safety implications
作者： Maelenn Païline Delcourt, Marion Vermeulen, Aneta Kopacz, Manuela Miletic, Andrew Saville, Christoph Niederhauser, Jean‐Michel Pawlotsky, Syria Laperche, Daniel Candotti, Manuela Miletić
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管 HBV DNA 检测不到或极低,但献血者筛查偶尔会发现 HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 水平较高的个体。这种不一致特征的分子基础和输血风险仍不清楚。研究设计和方法:对 75 名 HBsAg >100 IU/mL 且 HBV DNA 检测不到或极低 (<6 IU/mL) 的献血者进行了调查,其中包括 39 名 ID-NAT 无反应性的献血者。使用第二次 ID-NAT 测定、病毒粒子浓缩后的验证性 PCR 和测序进一步评估 HBV DNA。将序列与基因型匹配的高病毒血症对照的序列进行比较。将选定的基本核心启动子 (BCP) 和核心 突变 引入具有复制能力的 1.18 聚体 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 质粒中,以评估对细胞外病毒粒子相关 DNA 产生的影响。结果:两个 ID-NAT 检测中有四个 (5%) 样品没有反应。所有捐献者的 HBV DNA 均得到证实,通常处于无法量化的水平,并且全部呈抗 HBc 阳性。没有发现一致的分子特征。在 BCP、核心和 PreS1 中检测到异质的、病例特异性的 突变,包括预测会影响前基因组 RNA 转录、衣壳化或病毒颗粒组装的变体,没有证据表明聚合酶活性受损。在体外,选定的 BCP 突变 显着减少细胞外 HBV DNA,而不影响 HBsAg 的产生。尽管携带 cN92T/cM93T 或 cS170F 的两种菌株显示病毒体相关 DNA 减少,但大多数核心 突变 的影响很小。一些构建体表现出病毒颗粒分泌受损或裸衣壳产量增加。结论:HBsAg 阳性/低 DNA 谱很大程度上反映了慢性非活动性感染,病毒血症极少。多样化的监管和结构 突变 可能以多因素的方式做出贡献。持续性病毒 基因组 和残留复制支持持续的 HBsAg 筛查,其中 NAT 敏感性次优且无法实施抗 HBc 检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
献血者筛查有时会发现乙肝表面抗原 (HBsAg) 水平较高的个体,尽管乙肝病毒 DNA 无法检测到或极低,但这种不一致特征的分子基础和输血风险仍不清楚。
设计与对象
对 75 名 HBsAg >100 IU/mL 且 HBV DNA 检测不到或极低 (<6 IU/mL) 的献血者进行了调查,其中包括 39 名 ID-NAT 无反应的献血者。
核心方法
使用第二次 ID-NAT 测定、病毒粒子浓缩后的验证性 PCR 和测序进一步评估 HBV DNA;将选定的基本核心启动子 (BCP) 和核心 突变 引入具有复制能力的 HBV 质粒中,以评估对细胞外病毒粒子相关 DNA 产生的影响。
主要结果
四个 (5%) 样本在两次 ID-NAT 检测中均无反应;所有捐献者的 HBV DNA 均得到证实,通常处于无法量化的水平,并且全部呈抗 HBc 阳性;没有发现一致的分子特征,但在 BCP、核心和 PreS1 中检测到异质的、病例特异性的 突变。
解释与创新
HBsAg 阳性/低 DNA 谱在很大程度上反映了具有最小病毒血症的慢性非活动性感染,并且不同的调节和结构 突变 可能以多因素的方式起作用。
科研与公卫意义
持续性病毒 基因组 和残留复制支持持续的 HBsAg 筛查,其中 NAT 敏感性次优且无法实施抗 HBc 检测。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究没有发现一致的分子特征,并依赖体外测定来确认 突变 效应,尽管样本量足以满足观察到的结果。

Hepatitis B virus infection with high HBsAg and undetectable DNA levels in untreated blood donors: Viral mechanisms and transfusion safety implications

Authors: Maelenn Païline Delcourt, Marion Vermeulen, Aneta Kopacz, Manuela Miletic, Andrew Saville, Christoph Niederhauser, Jean‐Michel Pawlotsky, Syria Laperche, Daniel Candotti, Manuela Miletić
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Blood donor screening occasionally identifies individuals with high HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) levels despite undetectable or extremely low HBV DNA. Molecular basis and transfusion risk of this discordant profile remain unclear. STUDY DESIGN AND METHODS: Seventy-five blood donors with HBsAg >100 IU/mL and undetectable or very low HBV DNA (<6 IU/mL), including 39 ID-NAT-nonreactive, were investigated. HBV DNA was further assessed using a second ID-NAT assay, confirmatory PCR following virion concentration, and sequencing. Sequences were compared with those from genotype-matched high-viremia controls. Selected basic core promoter (BCP) and core mutations were introduced into 1.18-mer replication-competent Hepatitis B virus (HBV) plasmids to assess effects on extracellular virion-associated DNA production. RESULTS: Four (5%) samples were nonreactive by the two ID-NAT assays. HBV DNA was confirmed in all donors, often at unquantifiable levels, and all were anti-HBc positive. No consistent molecular signature was identified. Heterogeneous, case-specific mutations were detected in BCP, core, and PreS1, including variants predicted to affect pregenomic RNA transcription, encapsidation, or virion assembly, with no evidence of impaired polymerase activity. In vitro, selected BCP mutations significantly reduced extracellular HBV DNA without affecting HBsAg production. Most core mutations had minimal impact, although two strains carrying cN92T/cM93T or cS170F showed reduced virion-associated DNA. Some constructs exhibited impaired virion secretion or increased naked capsid production. CONCLUSION: HBsAg-positive/low-DNA profile largely reflects chronic inactive infection with minimal viremia. Diverse regulatory and structural mutations may contribute in a multifactorial manner. Persistent viral genomes and residual replication support continued HBsAg screening where NAT sensitivity is suboptimal and anti-HBc testing cannot be implemented.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Blood donor screening occasionally identifies individuals with high HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) levels despite undetectable or extremely low HBV DNA, but the molecular basis and transfusion risk of this discordant profile remain unclear.
Design and population
Seventy-five blood donors with HBsAg >100 IU/mL and undetectable or very low HBV DNA (<6 IU/mL), including 39 ID-NAT-nonreactive, were investigated.
Core methods
HBV DNA was further assessed using a second ID-NAT assay, confirmatory PCR following virion concentration, and sequencing; selected basic core promoter (BCP) and core mutations were introduced into replication-competent HBV plasmids to assess effects on extracellular virion-associated DNA production.
Main results
Four (5%) samples were nonreactive by the two ID-NAT assays; HBV DNA was confirmed in all donors, often at unquantifiable levels, and all were anti-HBc positive; no consistent molecular signature was identified, but heterogeneous, case-specific mutations were detected in BCP, core, and PreS1.
Interpretation and novelty
The HBsAg-positive/low-DNA profile largely reflects chronic inactive infection with minimal viremia, and diverse regulatory and structural mutations may contribute in a multifactorial manner.
Scientific and public-health significance
Persistent viral genomes and residual replication support continued HBsAg screening where NAT sensitivity is suboptimal and anti-HBc testing cannot be implemented.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study identified no consistent molecular signature and relied on in vitro assays to confirm mutation effects, though the sample size was adequate for the observed findings.

结构性障碍导致中国山东省流动人口乙型肝炎预防的意向与行动差距:一项血清学、空间和行为数据的三角研究。

Structural barriers drive the intention-action gap in hepatitis B prevention among migrants in Shandong Province, China: a triangulated study of serological, spatial, and behavioral data.
作者： Yuan Sun, Bingyang Li, Na Li, Feng Hao, Peng Chen, Hao Feng
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是中国的一个重大公共卫生挑战。由于地域流动性大和职业脆弱性,农民工构成高度弱势群体。尽管做出了公共卫生教育努力,但明显的意图与行动差距扰乱了乙肝护理级联。这项研究整合了血清学、空间和行为数据,以调查这种差距是否主要是由认知缺陷造成的,还是被潜在的结构性障碍所中断。方法:在中国烟台进行了一项三角横断面研究,包括结构化问卷调查(N = 1,184)和血清学检测(N = 2,364)。使用地理信息系统 (GIS) 绘制区域抗 HBs 阳性率的空间分布。使用结构方程模型 (SEM) 和鲁棒最大似然估计器对改编后的健康信念模型进行测试,以评估客观结构约束(特别是时间贫困、经济约束和系统性缺乏医疗保险)的破坏性影响。结果:血清学确诊的抗-HBs阳性率为51.10%(1,208/2,364),远低于世界卫生组织推荐的70%阈值。空间分析显示,地理异质性显着(χ2 = 74.38,P < 0.001),免疫力较高的地区集中在规模较大的工业带(67.33%),免疫力较低的地区集中在分散的服务业区(36.81%)。SEM 结果表明,虽然外部线索强烈预测 HBV 知识(β = 0.894),但知识对预防意图只有边际影响(β = 0.192)。预防意图显着预测实际行为(β = 0.655)。然而,这种激励过程受到结构性障碍的严重破坏,主要是时间贫困(β = -0.563,P < 0.001),其次是经济约束(β = -0.278,P < 0.001)和缺乏医疗保险(β = -0.056,P = 0.030)。结论:农民工乙肝预防的意向与行动差距主要是由社会经济结构性障碍(尤其是时间贫困)造成的,而基本的乙肝知识仍然是预防行动的基础但不充分的条件。实现消除病毒性肝炎的目标需要从个人认知说服转向结构性干预,强调基于工作场所的服务提供、有针对性的空间资源分配以及保险改革,以减轻自付费用和机会成本。

问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是中国的一个重大公共卫生挑战。由于地域流动性大和职业脆弱性,农民工构成高度弱势群体。

在中国烟台进行了一项三角横断面研究,包括结构化问卷调查(N = 1,184)和血清学检测(N = 2,364)。

解释与创新
农民工乙肝预防的意向与行动差距主要是由社会经济结构性障碍造成的——最明显的是时间匮乏,而基本的乙肝知识仍然是预防行动的基础但不充分的条件。
科研与公卫意义
实现消除病毒性肝炎的目标需要从个人认知说服转向结构性干预,强调基于工作场所的服务提供、有针对性的空间资源分配以及保险改革,以减轻自付费用和机会成本。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果受到其横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,并且可能存在未测量的混杂因素。由于依赖自我报告数据且缺乏纵向随访,证据强度中等。

Structural barriers drive the intention-action gap in hepatitis B prevention among migrants in Shandong Province, China: a triangulated study of serological, spatial, and behavioral data.

Authors: Yuan Sun, Bingyang Li, Na Li, Feng Hao, Peng Chen, Hao Feng
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health challenge in China. Migrant workers constitute a highly vulnerable population due to high geographic mobility and occupational vulnerability. Despite public health education efforts, a notable intention-action gap disrupts the HBV care cascade. This study integrates serological, spatial, and behavioral data to investigate whether this gap is primarily driven by cognitive deficits or interrupted by underlying structural barriers. METHODS: A triangulated cross-sectional study was conducted in Yantai, China, involving structured questionnaire surveys (N = 1,184) and serological testing (N = 2,364). The spatial distribution of regional anti-HBs positive rates was mapped using Geographic Information Systems (GIS). An adapted Health Belief Model was tested using Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) with a Robust Maximum Likelihood estimator to evaluate the disruptive effects of objective structural constraints-specifically, time poverty, economic constraints, and systemic lack of medical insurance. RESULTS: The serologically confirmed anti-HBs positive rate was 51.10% (1,208/2,364), substantially lower than the 70% threshold recommended by the World Health Organization. Spatial analysis revealed significant geographic heterogeneity (χ2 = 74.38, P < 0.001), with higher immunity concentrated in large-scale industrial belts (67.33%) and lower immunity clustered in dispersed service-sector districts (36.81%). SEM results indicated that while external cues strongly predicted HBV knowledge (β = 0.894), knowledge had only a marginal effect on preventive intention (β = 0.192). Preventive intention significantly predicted actual behavior (β = 0.655). However, this motivational process was severely undermined by structural barriers, predominantly time poverty (β = -0.563, P < 0.001), followed by economic constraints (β = -0.278, P < 0.001) and lack of medical insurance (β = -0.056, P = 0.030). CONCLUSIONS: The intention-action gap in HBV prevention among migrant workers is primarily driven by socioeconomic structural barriers-most notably time poverty-while basic HBV knowledge remains a foundational but insufficient condition for preventive action. Achieving the goal of viral hepatitis elimination requires a shift from individual cognitive persuasion to structural interventions, emphasizing workplace-based service delivery, spatially targeted resource allocation, and insurance reforms to alleviate out-of-pocket and opportunity costs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health challenge in China. Migrant workers constitute a highly vulnerable population due to high geographic mobility and occupational vulnerability.

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and by the potential for unmeasured confounding factors. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on self-reported data and the absence of longitudinal follow-up.

老年 慢性乙型肝炎 患者从富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯转换为替诺福韦艾拉酚胺或恩替卡韦的影响

Impact of Switching from Tenofovir Disoproxil Fumarate to Tenofovir Alafenamide or Entecavir in Older Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B
作者： Federico Cesanelli, Ottavia Nozza, Federica Gottardi, Francesca Mosti, Serena Zaltron, Giorgio Tiecco, Irene Scarvaglieri, Angiola Spinetti, Francesco Castelli, Eugenia Quiros-Roldan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 对 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 非常有效,但可能引起进行性肾功能损害和近端肾小管功能障碍;改用替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 或恩替卡韦 (ETV) 可能会减少 TDF 相关的肾毒性。
设计与对象
这项回顾性纵向 队列研究 研究纳入了在三级中心 (2012-2023) 从 TDF 转为 TAF 或 ETV 的慢性乙型肝炎成人患者,符合条件的患者接受了 ≥ 2 年的 TDF 治疗 治疗,并且进行了 ≥ 2 年的转后随访。
核心方法
从转换前2年到转换后4年,每6个月收集一次临床和实验室数据;使用线性混合效应模型对生物标志物轨迹进行分析,其中时间以开关为中心,比较 治疗,并对关键混杂因素进行多变量调整。
主要结果
A total of 102 patients were included (TAF n = 82; ETV n = 20), mean age 72 ± 10 years, 72% male; serum creatinine showed a non-significant increasing trend during TDF therapy (β = +0.008 mg/dL/year, p = 0.24) and after switching (β = +0.009 mg/dL/year, p = 0.07), with no significant difference between groups; serum phosphate declined during TDF in the ETV group (β = -0.175 mg/dL/year, p = 0.002) and showed a significant increase after switching (β = +0.111 mg/dL/year, p = 0.031) while the TAF group remained stable; total cholesterol showed a significant immediate increase at the time of switch (+18.3 mg/dL, 95% CI 11.0-25.6, p < 0.001), with no significant difference between TAF and ETV.
解释与创新
与之前报告从 TDF 转换后肌酐明显改善的研究不同,队列研究 并没有显示转换后肌酐水平有统计学上的显着降低,尽管观察到肌酐进展减弱和磷酸盐水平恢复,表明 TDF 相关的肾脏和肾小管毒性得到缓解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为临床实践提供了信息,表明老年患者从 TDF 转换为 TAF 或 ETV 可能会减轻肾和肾小管毒性,尽管肌酐没有显着降低,需要仔细监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和小样本量而受到限制,特别是对于 ETV 组(n = 20),这可能会降低普遍性;然而,多变量调整的纵向分析提供了中等的证据强度。

Impact of Switching from Tenofovir Disoproxil Fumarate to Tenofovir Alafenamide or Entecavir in Older Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B

Authors: Federico Cesanelli, Ottavia Nozza, Federica Gottardi, Francesca Mosti, Serena Zaltron, Giorgio Tiecco, Irene Scarvaglieri, Angiola Spinetti, Francesco Castelli, Eugenia Quiros-Roldan
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) is highly effective for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) but may induce progressive renal impairment and proximal tubular dysfunction. Switching to tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) or entecavir (ETV) may reduce TDF-related nephrotoxicity. This study evaluated renal and metabolic changes before and after switching from TDF to TAF or ETV in a real-world cohort of older patients. METHODS: This retrospective longitudinal cohort study included adults with CHB who switched from TDF to TAF or ETV at a tertiary center (2012-2023). Eligible patients had ≥ 2 years of TDF treatment and ≥ 2 years of post-switch follow-up. Those with virological failure, major coinfections, or non-HBV-related kidney disease were excluded. Clinical and laboratory data were collected at 6-month intervals from 2 years before to 4 years after switching. Biomarker trajectories were analyzed using linear mixed-effects models with time centered at switch comparing treatments and with multivariable adjustment for key confounders. RESULTS: A total of 102 patients were included (TAF n = 82; ETV n = 20), mean age 72 ± 10 years, 72% male. All patients had undetectable HBV-DNA at switch. During TDF therapy, serum creatinine showed a non-significant increasing trend (β = + 0.008 mg/dL/year, p = 0.24), which persisted after switching (β = + 0.009 mg/dL/year, p = 0.07), with no significant difference between groups. Serum phosphate declined during TDF in the ETV group (β = - 0.175 mg/dL/year, p = 0.002) and showed a significant increase after switching (β = + 0.111 mg/dL/year, p = 0.031) while the TAF group remained stable throughout. Total cholesterol ishowed a significant immediate increase at the time of switch (+ 18.3 mg/dL, 95% CI 11.0-25.6, p < 0.001), with no significant difference between TAF and ETV. CONCLUSIONS: Unlike previous studies reporting clear creatinine improvement after switching from TDF, this cohort did not show a statistically significant reduction in creatinine levels after the switch although an attenuation of creatinine progression and recovery of phosphate levels were observed, suggesting mitigation of TDF-related renal and tubular toxicity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) is highly effective for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) but may induce progressive renal impairment and proximal tubular dysfunction; switching to tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) or entecavir (ETV) may reduce TDF-related nephrotoxicity.
Design and population
This retrospective longitudinal cohort study included adults with CHB who switched from TDF to TAF or ETV at a tertiary center (2012-2023), with eligible patients having ≥ 2 years of TDF treatment and ≥ 2 years of post-switch follow-up.
Core methods
Clinical and laboratory data were collected at 6-month intervals from 2 years before to 4 years after switching; biomarker trajectories were analyzed using linear mixed-effects models with time centered at switch comparing treatments and with multivariable adjustment for key confounders.
Main results
A total of 102 patients were included (TAF n = 82; ETV n = 20), mean age 72 ± 10 years, 72% male; serum creatinine showed a non-significant increasing trend during TDF therapy (β = +0.008 mg/dL/year, p = 0.24) and after switching (β = +0.009 mg/dL/year, p = 0.07), with no significant difference between groups; serum phosphate declined during TDF in the ETV group (β = -0.175 mg/dL/year, p = 0.002) and showed a significant increase after switching (β = +0.111 mg/dL/year, p = 0.031) while the TAF group remained stable; total cholesterol showed a significant immediate increase at the time of switch (+18.3 mg/dL, 95% CI 11.0-25.6, p < 0.001), with no significant difference between TAF and ETV.
Interpretation and novelty
Unlike previous studies reporting clear creatinine improvement after switching from TDF, this cohort did not show a statistically significant reduction in creatinine levels after the switch although an attenuation of creatinine progression and recovery of phosphate levels were observed, suggesting mitigation of TDF-related renal and tubular toxicity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform clinical practice by suggesting that switching from TDF to TAF or ETV in older patients may mitigate renal and tubular toxicity, though the absence of significant creatinine reduction warrants careful monitoring.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and small sample size, particularly for the ETV group (n = 20), which may reduce generalizability; however, the longitudinal analysis with multivariable adjustment provides moderate evidence strength.

西班牙 队列研究 患者中与表面抗原 (HBsAg) 清除相关的因素:对长期预后的影响

Factors Associated With Surface Antigen (HBsAg) Clearance in a Spanish Cohort of Chronic Hepatitis B Patients: Influence on the Long‐Term Prognosis
作者： Paula Gavilán, Juan‐Carlos Gavilán, Alberto Piserra‐Lopez, Alberto Piserra-Lopez, Alberto Piserra López-Fernández De Heredia
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的是在西班牙南部未经选择的长期随访乙型肝炎开放 队列研究 中,确定有助于 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染患者乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 清除的因素。一项前瞻性开放 队列研究 研究于 1991 年至 2022 年间在马拉加(西班牙)两家医院内科肝脏病房招募了 453 名 CHB 感染患者,以评估随访期间乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)清除率的 发病率,确定与其相关的因素,并确定清除率对长期预后的影响。随访时间为0.5至36.08年。进行多变量 Cox 回归分析以确定与 HBsAg 清除相关的因素。使用联合预后终点评估清除率对长期预后的影响:需要肝移植、肝脏并发症或肝脏相关的 死亡率。采用 Kaplan-Meir 方法构建时间-事件曲线,随后通过对数秩检验对组间事件发生情况进行比较。在随后的随访期间,96/453 名患者(21.2%)失去表面抗原(每年 1.96/100 名患者)。多变量 Cox 回归分析确定为与 HBsAg 清除率相关的独立因素:首次评估年龄超过 40 岁:HR 2.21 (1.38–3.52);基线 DNA HBV 峰值低于 3.3log 10 (< 2000 IU/mL):HR 3.44 (2.01–5.89);随访期间乙型肝炎 e 抗原 (HBeAg) 丢失:HR 7.23 (3.87–13.50)。HBsAg 清除率的 发病率 为 1.96 例/100 患者年。这种情况在 40 岁以上、基线 DNA-HBV 低于 3.3 log 10 (< 2000 IU/mL) 的患者以及随访期间 HBeAg 消失的慢性 HBeAg+ 患者中更为常见。HBsAg 清除率与更好的长期肝脏预后相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染患者乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 清除的影响因素,并评估其对长期预后的影响。
设计与对象
1991 年至 2022 年间,西班牙马拉加两家医院的肝脏病房对 453 名 CHB 感染患者进行了一项前瞻性开放 队列研究 研究。发病率 HBsAg 清除率为每 100 患者年 1.96 例。
核心方法
使用多变量 Cox 回归分析来确定与 HBsAg 清除率相关的因素。使用联合预后终点评估清除率对长期预后的影响:需要肝移植、肝脏并发症或肝脏相关的 死亡率。
主要结果
随访期间,96/453 名患者 (21.2%) 失去 HBsAg,每年 发病率 率为 1.96/100 名患者。
解释与创新
HBsAg 清除率与更好的长期肝脏预后相关,凸显了其在疾病结果中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 监测 和 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的管理策略提供了信息,强调了 HBsAg 清除率在预测有利的长期结果方面的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于样本量大和随访期长,证据有力。

Factors Associated With Surface Antigen (HBsAg) Clearance in a Spanish Cohort of Chronic Hepatitis B Patients: Influence on the Long‐Term Prognosis

Authors: Paula Gavilán, Juan‐Carlos Gavilán, Alberto Piserra‐Lopez, Alberto Piserra-Lopez, Alberto Piserra López-Fernández De Heredia
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The objective is to identify factors that contribute to hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) clearance in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection in an unselected, long‐term follow‐up hepatitis B open cohort in southern Spain. A prospective open cohort study of 453 patients with CHB infection was recruited in the Liver Unit of the Internal Department of two hospitals in Málaga (Spain) between 1991 and 2022 to assess the incidence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) clearance during follow‐up, identify factors related to it, and determine the influence that clearance had on the long‐term prognosis. The duration of follow‐up ranged from 0.5 to 36.08 years. A multivariate Cox regression analysis was conducted to identify factors associated with HBsAg clearance. The impact of clearance on long‐term prognosis was evaluated using a combined prognostic end point: need for liver transplantation, liver complications, or liver‐related mortality. The construction of time‐to‐event curves was achieved by employing the Kaplan‐Meir method, with subsequent comparison of event occurrence between groups conducted via log‐rank test. During the subsequent follow‐up period, 96/453 patients (21.2%) lost surface antigen (1.96/100 patients per year). Multivariate Cox regression analysis identified as independent factors associated with HBsAg clearance: age at first assessment over 40 years: HR 2.21 (1.38–3.52); a baseline DNA HBV peak below 3.3log 10 (< 2000 IU/mL): HR 3.44 (2.01–5.89); loss of hepatitis B e‐antigen (HBeAg) during follow‐up: HR 7.23 (3.87–13.50). The incidence of HBsAg clearance was 1.96 cases/100 patient‐years. It was more frequent in patients older than 40 years, with baseline DNA‐HBV below 3.3 log 10 (< 2000 IU/mL), and in chronic HBeAg+ patients who lost HBeAg during follow‐up. HBsAg clearance was associated with a better long‐term hepatic prognosis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify factors contributing to hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) clearance in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection and assess its influence on long-term prognosis.
Design and population
A prospective open cohort study of 453 patients with CHB infection was conducted in the Liver Unit of two hospitals in Málaga, Spain, between 1991 and 2022. The incidence of HBsAg clearance was 1.96 cases per 100 patient-years.
Core methods
A multivariate Cox regression analysis was used to identify factors associated with HBsAg clearance. The impact of clearance on long-term prognosis was evaluated using a combined prognostic endpoint: need for liver transplantation, liver complications, or liver-related mortality.
Main results
During follow-up, 96/453 patients (21.2%) lost HBsAg, with an incidence rate of 1.96/100 patients per year.
Interpretation and novelty
HBsAg clearance was associated with a better long-term hepatic prognosis, highlighting its significance in disease outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance and management strategies for chronic hepatitis B patients, emphasizing the importance of HBsAg clearance in predicting favorable long-term outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and long follow-up period.

慢性 HBV 感染会破坏 CCL5 分泌性 cNK 细胞,并减弱肝脏中 DC 和 HBV 特异性 T 细胞的积聚和激活

Chronic HBV Infection Disrupts CCL5‐Secreting cNK Cells and Attenuates Liver Accumulation and Activation of DCs and HBV‐Specific T Cells
作者： Ailu Yang, Yucan Wang, Yating Yu, Jing Wu, Cuiping Bao, Zixuan Wang, Qiuju Han, Zhigang Tian, Jian Zhang, Huajun Zhao
摘要中文翻译摘要 了解 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染诱导的免疫耐受的免疫学机制对于制定有效的治疗策略至关重要,但自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞亚群在慢性 HBV 感染期间调节树突状细胞 (DC) 介导的 抗病毒药物 免疫的机制仍不清楚。在这里,我们证明慢性 HBV 感染通过抑制肝 NK 细胞产生 CC 趋化因子配体 5 (CCL5) 来破坏肝内 DC 的积累和激活。重要的是,慢性 HBV 感染减少了肝脏常规 NK (cNK) 细胞(肝脏 CCL5 的主要来源)的数量,而肝脏驻留 NK (LrNK) 细胞增加并表现出较低的 CCL5 表达和免疫抑制特征。CCL5 活性肝 cNK 细胞的过继转移促进了 DC 的积累和激活,增强了 HBV 特异性 CD8 + T 细胞反应,并促进了病毒清除。从机制上讲,慢性HBV感染促进肝NK细胞中腺苷A2a受体(ADORA2A)/核因子κB(NF-κB)通路的激活,从而拮抗NF-κB激活,降低cNK细胞比例,抑制CCL5产生,最终损害肝脏聚集和DCs激活,导致抗HBV 免疫应答缺陷。这项研究揭示了慢性 HBV 感染诱导的免疫耐受的新机制,并表明靶向 cNK 细胞中的 ADORA2A/NFκB/CCL5 轴可能代表慢性 HBV 感染的潜在治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
了解 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染诱导的免疫耐受的免疫学机制对于制定有效的治疗策略至关重要,但自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞亚群在慢性 HBV 感染期间调节树突状细胞 (DC) 介导的 抗病毒药物 免疫的机制仍不清楚。
设计与对象
研究表明,慢性 HBV 感染通过抑制肝 NK 细胞产生 CC 趋化因子配体 5 (CCL5),从而破坏肝内 DC 的积累和激活。
核心方法
该研究涉及过继转移具有 CCL5 活性的肝脏常规 NK (cNK) 细胞,以促进 DC 积累和激活,增强 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞反应并促进病毒清除。
主要结果
慢性HBV感染减少了肝脏cNK细胞(肝脏CCL5的主要来源)的数量,而肝脏驻留NK(LrNK)细胞增加并表现出较低的CCL5表达和免疫抑制特征。
解释与创新
慢性HBV感染促进肝NK细胞中腺苷A2a受体(ADORA2A)/核因子κ-B(NF-κB)通路的激活,从而拮抗NF-κB激活,降低cNK细胞比例,抑制CCL5产生,最终损害肝脏聚集和DCs活化,导致抗HBV 免疫应答缺陷。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究揭示了慢性 HBV 感染诱导免疫耐受的新机制,并表明靶向 cNK 细胞中的 ADORA2A/NFκB/CCL5 轴可能代表慢性 HBV 感染的潜在治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究为慢性 HBV 感染期间 cNK 细胞在调节 DC 介导的 抗病毒药物 免疫中的作用提供了强有力的证据,但需要进一步的研究来在临床环境中验证这些发现。

Chronic HBV Infection Disrupts CCL5‐Secreting cNK Cells and Attenuates Liver Accumulation and Activation of DCs and HBV‐Specific T Cells

Authors: Ailu Yang, Yucan Wang, Yating Yu, Jing Wu, Cuiping Bao, Zixuan Wang, Qiuju Han, Zhigang Tian, Jian Zhang, Huajun Zhao
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Understanding the immunological mechanisms underlying chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection‐induced immune tolerance is critical for developing effective therapeutic strategies, yet the mechanisms by which natural killer (NK)‐cell subsets regulate dendritic cell (DC)‐mediated antiviral immunity during chronic HBV infection remain unclear. Here, we demonstrated that chronic HBV infection disrupted intrahepatic DCs accumulation and activation by suppressing CC chemokine ligand 5 (CCL5) production from hepatic NK cells. Importantly, chronic HBV infection reduced the number of hepatic conventional NK (cNK) cells, the main source of hepatic CCL5, whereas liver‐resident NK (LrNK) cells increased and exhibited lower CCL5 expression and immunosuppressive features. Adoptive transfer of CCL5‐competent hepatic cNK cells promoted DCs accumulation and activation, enhanced HBV‐specific CD8 + T‐cell responses, and facilitated viral clearance. Mechanistically, chronic HBV infection promoted the activation of adenosine A2a receptor (ADORA2A)/nuclear factor kappa‐B (NF‐κB) pathway in hepatic NK cells, thereby antagonizing NF‐κB activation, reducing the proportion of cNK cells, suppressing CCL5 production, and ultimately impairing hepatic aggregation and activation of DCs, leading to defective anti‐HBV immune responses. This study revealed a novel mechanism involved in chronic HBV infection‐induced immune tolerance and indicated that targeting the ADORA2A/NFκB/CCL5 axis in cNK cells may represent a potential therapeutic strategy for chronic HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Understanding the immunological mechanisms underlying chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection-induced immune tolerance is critical for developing effective therapeutic strategies, yet the mechanisms by which natural killer (NK)-cell subsets regulate dendritic cell (DC)-mediated antiviral immunity during chronic HBV infection remain unclear.
Design and population
The study demonstrated that chronic HBV infection disrupted intrahepatic DCs accumulation and activation by suppressing CC chemokine ligand 5 (CCL5) production from hepatic NK cells.
Core methods
The study involved the adoptive transfer of CCL5-competent hepatic conventional NK (cNK) cells to promote DCs accumulation and activation, enhancing HBV-specific CD8+ T-cell responses and facilitating viral clearance.
Main results
Chronic HBV infection reduced the number of hepatic cNK cells, the main source of hepatic CCL5, whereas liver-resident NK (LrNK) cells increased and exhibited lower CCL5 expression and immunosuppressive features.
Interpretation and novelty
Chronic HBV infection promoted the activation of adenosine A2a receptor (ADORA2A)/nuclear factor kappa-B (NF-κB) pathway in hepatic NK cells, thereby antagonizing NF-κB activation, reducing the proportion of cNK cells, suppressing CCL5 production, and ultimately impairing hepatic aggregation and activation of DCs, leading to defective anti-HBV immune responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study revealed a novel mechanism involved in chronic HBV infection-induced immune tolerance and indicated that targeting the ADORA2A/NFκB/CCL5 axis in cNK cells may represent a potential therapeutic strategy for chronic HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study provides strong evidence for the role of cNK cells in regulating DC-mediated antiviral immunity during chronic HBV infection, but further research is needed to validate these findings in clinical settings.

基于计划行为理论的中国成人慢性乙型肝炎的药物依从性

Medication adherence in Chinese adults with chronic hepatitis B based on theory of planned behavior
作者： Yumei WANG, Yiqing HUANG, Bing GE, Chen CHEN, Jie XU
摘要中文翻译目的 基于计划行为理论(TPB)探讨慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎,CHB)感染患者药物依从性的相关因素。方法 在这项横断面研究中,收集了中国CHB门诊患者的药物依从性信息。采用Pearson相关分析评估药物依从性与TPB项目之间的关联。建立结构方程模型以进一步评估潜在TPB变量对药物依从性的影响。结果 共纳入从青岛市公共卫生临床中心门诊部招募的225例CHB患者,根据Morisky依从性评估量表评估,其中67.6%报告了中等药物依从性。药物依从性与行为意向(r = 0.51,P < 0.001)、感知行为控制(r = 0.63,P < 0.001)和主观规范(r = 0.52,P < 0.001)呈显著正相关。感知行为控制直接和间接影响药物依从性,相关系数分别为0.14和0.32。结构方程模型显示行为态度和主观规范是药物依从性的最强预测因子,两者均间接影响行为(相关系数分别为:0.13 × 0.66 = 0.09 和 0.39 × 0.66 = 0.25)。TPB变量的最佳拟合模型解释了药物依从性58.5%的方差。结论 发现中国CHB成人患者具有中等药物依从性。行为态度、主观规范和感知行为控制间接影响药物依从性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在利用计划行为理论(TPB)探讨慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)感染患者服药依从性的相关因素。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究纳入了从中国青岛公共卫生临床中心门诊部招募的 225 名慢性乙型肝炎患者。
核心方法
采用 Pearson 相关分析评估药物依从性与 TPB 项目之间的关联,并建立结构方程模型来评估潜在 TPB 变量对药物依从性的影响。
主要结果
在225例CHB患者中,67.6%经Morisky依从性评估量表评估报告了中等药物依从性。药物依从性与行为意向(r = 0.51,P < 0.001)、感知行为控制(r = 0.63,P < 0.001)和主观规范(r = 0.52,P < 0.001)呈显著正相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,行为态度、主观规范和感知行为控制间接影响药物依从性,结构方程模型解释了药物依从性差异的 58.5%。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为干预措施提供信息,通过基于 TPB 框架的行为态度、主观规范和感知行为控制来提高慢性乙型肝炎患者的药物依从性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了因果推断,但大样本量和经过验证的措施的使用提供了中等的证据强度。

Medication adherence in Chinese adults with chronic hepatitis B based on theory of planned behavior

Authors: Yumei WANG, Yiqing HUANG, Bing GE, Chen CHEN, Jie XU
Original abstract (English)
Purpose To investigate the factors related to medication adherence in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection by considering the theory of planned behavior (TPB). Methods In this cross-sectional study, medication adherence information was collected from outpatients with CHB in China. Pearson correlation analysis was performed to evaluate the association between medication adherence and TPB items. A structural equation model was established to further assess the effects of the latent TPB variables on medication adherence. Results A total of 225 patients with CHB recruited from the outpatient department of Qingdao Public Health Clinical Center were included, 67.6% of whom reported moderate medication adherence, as assessed using Morisky Adherence Assessment Score. Medication adherence was significantly positively correlated with behavioral intention ( r = 0.51, P < 0.001), perceived behavioral control ( r = 0.63, P < 0.001), and subjective norms ( r = 0.52, P < 0.001). Perceived behavioral control both directly and indirectly affected medication adherence, with the correlation coefficients being 0.14 and 0.32, respectively. The structural equation model revealed behavioral attitude and subjective norms as the strongest predictors of medication adherence, both of which indirectly affected behavior (correlation coefficients: 0.13 × 0.66 = 0.09 and 0.39 × 0.66 = 0.25, respectively). The optimal fitted model of TPB variables explained 58.5% of the variance in medication adherence. Conclusions Moderate medication adherence was discovered among Chinese adults with CHB. Behavioral attitude, subjective norms, and perceived behavioral control indirectly affected medication adherence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate factors related to medication adherence in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection using the theory of planned behavior (TPB).
Design and population
This cross-sectional study included 225 patients with CHB recruited from the outpatient department of Qingdao Public Health Clinical Center in China.
Core methods
Pearson correlation analysis was used to evaluate associations between medication adherence and TPB items, and a structural equation model was established to assess the effects of latent TPB variables on medication adherence.
Main results
Among 225 patients with CHB, 67.6% reported moderate medication adherence as assessed by the Morisky Adherence Assessment Score. Medication adherence was significantly positively correlated with behavioral intention (r = 0.51, P < 0.001), perceived behavioral control (r = 0.63, P < 0.001), and subjective norms (r = 0.52, P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that behavioral attitude, subjective norms, and perceived behavioral control indirectly affected medication adherence, with the structural equation model explaining 58.5% of the variance in medication adherence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform interventions to improve medication adherence in CHB patients by targeting behavioral attitude, subjective norms, and perceived behavioral control based on the TPB framework.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was cross-sectional, limiting causal inference, but the large sample size and use of validated measures provide moderate evidence strength.

荷兰乙型肝炎、丙型肝炎和 HIV 血清流行率 的趋势:二十年全国 PIENTER 血清调查的见解。

Trends in Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and HIV Seroprevalence in the Netherlands: Insights From Two Decades of Nationwide PIENTER Serosurveys.
作者： van Straten CGJI, Birgit H B van Benthem, Fiona R M van der Klis, Gaby Smits, Eric R A Vos, Tom Woudenberg, Marc van der Valk, Eline L M Op de Coul, Christine G J I van Straten
摘要中文翻译尽管采取了有效的 治疗 和预防措施,乙型肝炎 (HBV)、丙型肝炎 (HCV) 和艾滋病毒仍然是公共卫生挑战。感染通常是无症状的,导致未确诊病例、传播 以及可预防的发病率和 传播。在荷兰,疾病负担集中在特定人群,有许多感染未被发现。我们使用来自三项全国性横断面血清调查(PIENTER:1995-1996、2006-2007 和 2016-2017)的 14,444 名 15-79 岁参与者的数据估计了 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率,确定了决定因素并评估了 20 年来的趋势。参与者完成了有关人口统计、行为和社会经济因素的问卷调查,并在 2017 年对所有轮次的 HBV 和 HCV 以及 HIV 样本进行了血清样本检测。血清流行率 估计值根据荷兰人口进行加权,使用加权泊松回归分析趋势,并使用多变量泊松回归评估决定因素。加权 HBV 血清流行率 在整个调查中保持稳定(3%-5% 过去或现在的感染;< 0.4% 活动感染)。乙型肝炎病毒 患病率 在第一代流动人口中最高,年龄、流动背景和性传播感染史是关键决定因素。HCV 血清流行率 仍然较低(< 0.4%),并且在非西方移民中最高。HIV 患病率 为 0.1%,在非西方移民和男男性行为者中感染率最高。只有 9% 的活动性 HBV 病例知道自己的状况。总体而言,荷兰的 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率 仍然处于较低水平,但集中在意识有限的特定群体中。这些发现支持有针对性的预防、筛查和与护理挂钩的战略,以实现世卫组织 2030 年消除目标,并强调持续进行基于人群的(血清)监测以指导公共卫生政策的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管有有效的 传播 和预防措施,乙型肝炎 (HBV)、丙型肝炎 (HCV) 和 HIV 仍然是公共卫生挑战,感染通常无症状,导致未确诊病例和进一步的 传播。在荷兰,疾病负担集中在特定人群中,有许多未发现的感染,因此需要对 血清流行率 随着时间的推移的趋势进行评估。
设计与对象
该研究利用了三项全国性横断面血清调查(PIENTER:1995-1996、2006-2007 和 2016-2017)的数据,包括 14,444 名 15-79 岁的参与者,以估计 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率、确定决定因素并评估 20 年来的趋势。
核心方法
参与者完成了有关人口统计、行为和社会经济因素的问卷调查,并在 2017 年对所有轮次的 HBV 和 HCV 以及 HIV 样本进行了血清样本检测。血清流行率 估计值根据荷兰人口进行加权,使用加权泊松回归分析趋势,并使用多变量泊松回归评估决定因素。
主要结果
加权 HBV 血清流行率 在整个调查中保持稳定(3%-5% 过去或现在感染;< 0.4% 活动性感染),在第一代移民中最高,只有 9% 的活动性 HBV 病例知道自己的状况。HCV 血清流行率 仍然较低(< 0.4%),在非西方移民中最高,而 HIV 患病率 为 0.1%,在非西方移民和男男性行为者中最高。
解释与创新
总体而言,荷兰的HBV、HCV和HIV 血清流行率仍然较低,但集中在认知有限的特定群体中,这表明虽然一般人群患病率稳定,但在这些亚群中存在持续的传播风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持有针对性的预防、筛查和与护理挂钩的战略,以实现世卫组织 2030 年消除目标,并强调持续进行基于人群的(血清)监测以指导公共卫生政策的重要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告具体的设计限制、偏差或混杂因素,但使用二十年来三项大规模全国性调查的加权数据为评估人口趋势提供了中等水平的证据。

Trends in Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and HIV Seroprevalence in the Netherlands: Insights From Two Decades of Nationwide PIENTER Serosurveys.

Authors: van Straten CGJI, Birgit H B van Benthem, Fiona R M van der Klis, Gaby Smits, Eric R A Vos, Tom Woudenberg, Marc van der Valk, Eline L M Op de Coul, Christine G J I van Straten
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B (HBV), hepatitis C (HCV), and HIV remain public health challenges despite effective treatments and preventive options. Infections are often asymptomatic, leading to undiagnosed cases, onward transmission, and preventable morbidity and mortality. In the Netherlands, disease burden is concentrated in specific populations, with many undetected infections. We estimated HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence, identified determinants, and assessed trends over 20 years using data from 14,444 participants aged 15-79 years from three nationwide cross-sectional serosurveys (PIENTER: 1995-1996, 2006-2007, and 2016-2017). Participants completed questionnaires on demographics, behaviour, and socio-economic factors, and serum samples were tested for HBV and HCV in all rounds and HIV in 2017. Seroprevalence estimates were weighted to the Dutch population, trends were analysed using weighted Poisson regression, and determinants were evaluated using multivariable Poisson regression. Weighted HBV seroprevalence remained stable across surveys (3%-5% past or present infection; < 0.4% active infection). HBV prevalence was highest among first-generation migrants, with older age, migration background, and STI history as key determinants. HCV seroprevalence remained low (< 0.4%) and was highest among non-Western migrants. HIV prevalence was 0.1% and highest among non-Western migrants and men who have sex with men. Only 9% of active HBV cases were aware of their status. Overall, HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence remain low in the Netherlands but are concentrated in specific groups with limited awareness. These findings support targeted prevention, screening, and linkage-to-care strategies to achieve the 2030 WHO elimination goals and highlight the importance of continued population-based (sero-)monitoring to guide public health policy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B (HBV), hepatitis C (HCV), and HIV remain public health challenges despite effective treatments and preventive options, with infections often asymptomatic leading to undiagnosed cases and onward transmission. In the Netherlands, the disease burden is concentrated in specific populations with many undetected infections, necessitating an assessment of seroprevalence trends over time.
Design and population
The study utilized data from three nationwide cross-sectional serosurveys (PIENTER: 1995-1996, 2006-2007, and 2016-2017) including 14,444 participants aged 15-79 years to estimate HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence, identify determinants, and assess trends over 20 years.
Core methods
Participants completed questionnaires on demographics, behaviour, and socio-economic factors, and serum samples were tested for HBV and HCV in all rounds and HIV in 2017. Seroprevalence estimates were weighted to the Dutch population, trends were analysed using weighted Poisson regression, and determinants were evaluated using multivariable Poisson regression.
Main results
Weighted HBV seroprevalence remained stable across surveys (3%-5% past or present infection; < 0.4% active infection), was highest among first-generation migrants, and only 9% of active HBV cases were aware of their status. HCV seroprevalence remained low (< 0.4%) and was highest among non-Western migrants, while HIV prevalence was 0.1% and highest among non-Western migrants and men who have sex with men.
Interpretation and novelty
Overall, HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence remain low in the Netherlands but are concentrated in specific groups with limited awareness, indicating that while general population prevalence is stable, persistent transmission risks exist within these subpopulations.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support targeted prevention, screening, and linkage-to-care strategies to achieve the 2030 WHO elimination goals and highlight the importance of continued population-based (sero-)monitoring to guide public health policy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, bias, or confounding factors, but the use of weighted data from three large-scale nationwide surveys over two decades provides a moderate level of evidence for assessing population trends.

中国乙型肝炎发病率发病时空特征及影响因素

Spatiotemporal evolution characteristics and influencing factors of hepatitis B incidence rate in China
作者： Ke Sun, Siqi Zhang, Fang Wang, Liqing Li
摘要中文翻译发病率(HBV)感染仍然是中国持续存在的公共卫生挑战,发病率 具有显着的时空异质性。本研究旨在利用2008年至2023年的发病率数据,考察乙型肝炎发病率的区域差异,探讨其时空进化模式,并分析中国相关因素的时空异质性。Dagum基尼系数分解方法评估区域差异来源,标准差椭圆探索时空方向和全球特征,探索性空间数据分析(ESDA)检查空间相关性和聚集性,使用地理和时间加权回归(GTWR)模型检查影响因素的时空异质性。结果表明,区域差距缩小,空间聚集性下降,东部和中部地区标准差椭圆向东南移动,发病率重心向东南移动351.25公里。模式从聚集转变为分散,可能是由于 疫苗接种 和医疗保健分配的改善。六个社会经济因素产生了显着但时空异质的影响,根据经济和政策背景,产生了多样化的、区域性的差异性影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用2008年至2023年的发病率数据,探讨乙型肝炎发病率的区域差异,探讨其时空发病率模式,并分析中国各地相关因素的时空异质性。
设计与对象
该研究利用2008年至2023年中国各地的发病率数据来检验区域差异、时空进化模式以及影响因素的异质性。
核心方法
达古姆基尼系数分解法评估区域差异来源,标准差椭圆探讨时空方向和全球特征,探索性空间数据分析(ESDA)考察空间相关性和聚集性,地理和时间加权回归(GTWR)模型考察影响因素的时空异质性。
主要结果
结果表明,区域差距缩小,空间聚集性下降,东部和中部地区标准差椭圆向东南移动,发病率重心向东南移动351.25公里。
解释与创新
模式从聚集转变为分散,可能是由于 疫苗接种 和医疗保健分配的改善。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提供了对中国乙型肝炎发病率的时空动态的深入了解,可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施和资源分配提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。

Spatiotemporal evolution characteristics and influencing factors of hepatitis B incidence rate in China

Authors: Ke Sun, Siqi Zhang, Fang Wang, Liqing Li
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a persistent public health challenge in China, with significant spatiotemporal heterogeneity in incidence. This study aimed to examine regional disparities in hepatitis B incidence, explore its spatiotemporal evolution patterns, and analyze the spatiotemporal heterogeneity of associated factors across China, using incidence data from 2008 to 2023. The Dagum Gini coefficient decomposition method assessed regional disparity sources, the standard deviation ellipse explored spatiotemporal direction and global characteristics, exploratory spatial data analysis (ESDA) examined spatial correlation and aggregation, and the geographically and temporally weighted regression (GTWR) model was used to examine spatiotemporal heterogeneity of influencing factors. Results showed narrowing regional disparities, declining spatial aggregation, and a standard deviation ellipse located in eastern and central regions with a southeastward shift, alongside the incidence gravity center moving 351.25 km southeast. Patterns shifted from clustered to dispersed, likely due to vaccination and improved healthcare allocation. Six socioeconomic factors exerted significant yet spatiotemporally heterogeneous impacts, with diverse, regionally varying effects based on economic and policy contexts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to examine regional disparities in hepatitis B incidence, explore its spatiotemporal evolution patterns, and analyze the spatiotemporal heterogeneity of associated factors across China, using incidence data from 2008 to 2023.
Design and population
The study used incidence data from 2008 to 2023 across China to examine regional disparities, spatiotemporal evolution patterns, and the heterogeneity of influencing factors.
Core methods
The Dagum Gini coefficient decomposition method assessed regional disparity sources, the standard deviation ellipse explored spatiotemporal direction and global characteristics, exploratory spatial data analysis (ESDA) examined spatial correlation and aggregation, and the geographically and temporally weighted regression (GTWR) model was used to examine spatiotemporal heterogeneity of influencing factors.
Main results
Results showed narrowing regional disparities, declining spatial aggregation, and a standard deviation ellipse located in eastern and central regions with a southeastward shift, alongside the incidence gravity center moving 351.25 km southeast.
Interpretation and novelty
Patterns shifted from clustered to dispersed, likely due to vaccination and improved healthcare allocation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the spatiotemporal dynamics of hepatitis B incidence in China, which can inform targeted public health interventions and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study.

恩替卡韦治疗 治疗 初治患者的长期结果

Long-Term Outcomes of Entecavir Therapy in Treatment-Naive Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B
作者： Esma Eroğlu
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 需要长期 抗病毒药物 治疗以预防疾病进展。本研究评估了恩替卡韦 (ETV) 治疗的长期疗效和安全性,并研究了 治疗 初治 CHB 患者部分病毒学缓解 (PVR) 的预测因素。方法 这项回顾性研究纳入了 2020 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间接受恩替卡韦治疗的 230 名初治核苷的慢性乙型肝炎患者。病毒学应答 (VR) 定义为 HBV-DNA 减少 1 log10 IU/mL,尽管在治疗至少 12 个月后可检测到病毒 DNA。随访期间分析临床、生化和病毒学参数。结果 平均年龄为 44 ± 18 岁,其中 54.7% 为男性。基线 HBV-DNA 为 6.8 log10 IU/mL。VR 率在第 3 个月达到 66%,第 6 个月达到 86%,第 1 年达到 89%,第 4-5 年达到 100%。ALT 正常化逐渐改善,而 HBeAg 血清转化在第 2-3 年达到最高。10.2% 的患者发生 PVR。PVR 与较高的基线 HBV-DNA 水平和第 24 周未能达到病毒学应答 VR 显着相关。未观察到严重不良事件。结论 ETV 疗法为 治疗 初治 CHB 患者提供了有效、持久的病毒抑制和出色的安全性。PVR 患者继续使用 治疗 仍可能实现完全病毒学抑制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究需要评估恩替卡韦治疗的长期疗效和安全性,并研究未接受过 治疗 治疗的 治疗 患者的部分病毒学缓解的预测因子,解决对有效的长期 抗病毒药物 治疗以预防疾病进展的需求。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了 2020 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间接受恩替卡韦治疗的 230 名未接受过核苷治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者。
核心方法
病毒学应答定义为治疗至少 12 个月后 HBV-DNA 减少至少 1 log10 IU/mL,且可检测到病毒 DNA,并在随访期间分析临床、生化和病毒学参数。
主要结果
病毒学应答率在第 3 个月达到 66%,第 6 个月达到 86%,第 1 年达到 89%,第 4-5 年达到 100%;10.2% 的患者出现部分病毒学缓解,并且与较高的基线 HBV-DNA 水平和第 24 周未能实现病毒学缓解显着相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,恩替卡韦治疗为首次使用 治疗 的患者提供了有效、持久的病毒抑制和出色的安全性,而在部分病毒学缓解的患者中继续使用 治疗 仍可能实现完全的病毒学抑制。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用恩替卡韦作为 慢性乙型肝炎 的一线治疗,对长期病毒抑制和减少疾病进展具有影响,并表明即使具有部分病毒学应答的患者也可能受益于持续的 治疗。
局限与证据强度
由于回顾性研究设计和单中心人群,证据强度中等,这可能限制普遍性,尽管大样本量和长随访期提供了恩替卡韦疗效和安全性的可靠数据。

Long-Term Outcomes of Entecavir Therapy in Treatment-Naive Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B

Authors: Esma Eroğlu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) requires long-term antiviral therapy to prevent disease progression. This study evaluated the long-term efficacy and safety of entecavir (ETV) therapy and investigated predictors of partial virological response (PVR) in treatment-naive CHB patients. Methods This retrospective study included 230 nucleoside-naive CHB patients treated with entecavir between January 2020 and December 2024. Virological response (VR) was defined as HBV-DNA 1 log10 IU/mL reduction in HBV-DNA despite detectable viral DNA after at least 12 months of therapy. Clinical, biochemical, and virological parameters were analyzed during follow-up. Results The mean age was 44 ± 18 years, and 54.7% were male. Baseline HBV-DNA was 6.8 log10 IU/mL. VR rates reached 66% at month 3, 86% at month 6, 89% at year 1, and 100% by years 4–5. ALT normalization improved progressively, while HBeAg seroconversion was highest during years 2–3. PVR occurred in 10.2% of patients. PVR was significantly associated with higher baseline HBV-DNA levels and failure to achieve virological response VR at week 24. No serious adverse events were observed. Conclusions ETV therapy provides effective, durable viral suppression and excellent safety in treatment-naive CHB patients. Continued treatment in patients with PVR may still achieve complete virological suppression.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to evaluate the long-term efficacy and safety of entecavir therapy and investigate predictors of partial virological response in treatment-naive chronic hepatitis B patients, addressing the need for effective long-term antiviral therapy to prevent disease progression.
Design and population
This retrospective study included 230 nucleoside-naive chronic hepatitis B patients treated with entecavir between January 2020 and December 2024.
Core methods
Virological response was defined as HBV-DNA reduction of at least 1 log10 IU/mL with detectable viral DNA after at least 12 months of therapy, and clinical, biochemical, and virological parameters were analyzed during follow-up.
Main results
Virological response rates reached 66% at month 3, 86% at month 6, 89% at year 1, and 100% by years 4–5; partial virological response occurred in 10.2% of patients and was significantly associated with higher baseline HBV-DNA levels and failure to achieve virological response at week 24.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that entecavir therapy provides effective, durable viral suppression and excellent safety in treatment-naive chronic hepatitis B patients, and continued treatment in patients with partial virological response may still achieve complete virological suppression.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of entecavir as a first-line therapy for chronic hepatitis B, with implications for long-term viral suppression and reduced disease progression, and suggest that even patients with partial virological response may benefit from continued treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the retrospective study design and single-center population, which may limit generalizability, though the large sample size and long follow-up period provide robust data on entecavir's efficacy and safety.

澳大利亚 慢性乙型肝炎 护理和 治疗 依从性的纵向模式:国家关联数据 队列研究 研究。

Longitudinal Patterns of Chronic Hepatitis B Care and Treatment Adherence in Australia: A National Linked Data Cohort Study.
作者： Jennifer H MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Stacey L Rowe, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C Cowie
摘要中文翻译目前澳大利亚对 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 治疗 的吸收和护理远低于目标水平,导致可预防的肝病和癌症的持续负担。了解个人层面的护理参与轨迹和社会人口因素的变化对于指导改善护理获取和保留的努力至关重要。这项研究分析了通过澳大利亚国家健康保险系统提供的乙型肝炎病毒载量 (VL) 检测和 Z​​XQTERMNQXZ 治疗 记录,这些记录与来自各个政府数据集的移民、死亡和社会人口统计数据相关。对人群的 治疗 和 VL 测试进行了评估,并使用慢性乙型肝炎患者人数的模型估计来计算吸收率。还评估了基于分发脚本的 VL 测试频率和 治疗 遵守情况。2023 年澳大利亚估计有 219,800 名慢性乙型肝炎患者,其中 118,334 人 (53.8%) 在 2014-2023 年期间接受了 VL 检测或 治疗。原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民对 VL 测试和 治疗 的估计采用率较低,并且因出生地区而异。在接受任何 VL 测试的 81,360 人中,只有 17,352 人 (21.3%) 定期接受测试,而 31,715 人 (39.0%) 有先前监测的证据,但自 2019 年以来没有进行 VL 测试。大多数开始 治疗 的人继续治疗并获得足够的供应以维持依从性。这项研究表明,澳大利亚在慢性乙型肝炎护理方面存在巨大差距,特别是在对未参加 治疗 的人群进行持续监测方面。优先人群的吸收差异很明显。应优先考虑重新参与那些失去随访的人。

Longitudinal Patterns of Chronic Hepatitis B Care and Treatment Adherence in Australia: A National Linked Data Cohort Study.

Authors: Jennifer H MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Stacey L Rowe, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C Cowie
Original abstract (English)
Current uptake of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) treatment and care in Australia is well below target levels, leading to an ongoing burden of preventable liver disease and cancer. Understanding individual-level trajectories of care engagement and variation by sociodemographic factors is essential to guide efforts to improve access to and retention in care. This study analysed records of hepatitis B viral load (VL) testing and antiviral treatment provided through Australia's national health insurance system, which were linked to migration, death, and sociodemographic data from various government datasets. Treatment and VL testing by population group were assessed, and uptake was calculated using modelled estimates of the number living with CHB. VL testing frequency and treatment adherence based on scripts dispensed were also assessed. Of the estimated 219,800 people living with CHB in Australia in 2023, 118,334 (53.8%) received either VL testing or treatment during 2014-2023. Estimated uptake of VL testing and treatment uptake were lower among Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander people and also varied by region of birth. Of the 81,360 who received any VL testing, only 17,352 (21.3%) received it regularly, while 31,715 (39.0%) had evidence of prior monitoring but no VL testing since 2019. The majority of those who initiated treatment continued therapy and received supply sufficient for adherence. This study shows that substantial gaps exist in CHB care in Australia, particularly in continued monitoring among those not on treatment. Disparities in uptake are evident by priority population. Re-engaging those lost to follow-up should be prioritised.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Current uptake of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) treatment and care in Australia is well below target levels, leading to an ongoing burden of preventable liver disease and cancer.
Design and population
This study analysed records of hepatitis B viral load (VL) testing and antiviral treatment provided through Australia's national health insurance system, which were linked to migration, death, and sociodemographic data from various government datasets.
Core methods
Treatment and VL testing by population group were assessed, and uptake was calculated using modelled estimates of the number living with CHB.
Main results
Of the estimated 219,800 people living with CHB in Australia in 2023, 118,334 (53.8%) received either VL testing or treatment during 2014-2023. Of the 81,360 who received any VL testing, only 17,352 (21.3%) received it regularly, while 31,715 (39.0%) had evidence of prior monitoring but no VL testing since 2019.
Interpretation and novelty
This study shows that substantial gaps exist in CHB care in Australia, particularly in continued monitoring among those not on treatment.
Scientific and public-health significance
Estimated uptake of VL testing and treatment uptake were lower among Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander people and also varied by region of birth.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design.

未经治疗的免疫耐受者与免疫耐受者之间的肝细胞癌风险接受治疗的免疫活跃 慢性乙型肝炎 患者。

Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Between Untreated Immune Tolerant Vs. Treated Immune Active Chronic Hepatitis B Patients.
作者： Robert J Wong, Zeyuan Yang, Ramsey Cheung
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者在未经治疗的免疫耐受 (U-IT) 与治疗的免疫活跃 (T-IA) 阶段发生肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的风险尚不清楚,并且大多数数据来自亚洲人群。
设计与对象
我们对 2010 年 1 月 1 日至 2021 年 12 月 31 日期间符合 U-IT 或 T-IA 标准的慢性乙型肝炎退伍军人进行了倾向评分加权分析(随访至 2024 年 12 月 31 日)。总共确定了 591 名 U-IT 和 555 名 T-IA CHB 患者。
核心方法
使用倾向评分加权分析比较 U-IT 组和 T-IA 组之间的肝硬化、HCC 或死亡的 发病率。
主要结果
Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients.
解释与创新
在主要为非亚裔的慢性乙型肝炎成人 队列研究 中,我们观察到与 T-IA 患者相比,U-IT 患者的 HCC 风险较低。虽然这与亚洲国家的一些研究形成鲜明对比,但这些数据确实强调了在 IA 阶段优先考虑并确保及时治疗 CHB 患者的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在免疫活跃期及时治疗慢性乙型肝炎患者的重要性,尽管免疫耐受患者的常规 治疗 仍存在争议,并且需要更多数据来更好地了解长期结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在主要非亚洲人 队列研究 中进行的,这可能会限制对亚洲人群的普遍适用性。尽管有倾向评分加权,但由于观察设计和潜在的残留混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Between Untreated Immune Tolerant Vs. Treated Immune Active Chronic Hepatitis B Patients.

Authors: Robert J Wong, Zeyuan Yang, Ramsey Cheung
Original abstract (English)
The risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) between chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients in the untreated immune tolerant (U-IT) vs. treated immune active (T-IA) phases is not clear, and most data are in Asian populations. We aim to evaluate long-term risks of cirrhosis, HCC, and overall mortality between U-IT vs. T-IA CHB patients among a national cohort of U.S. We conducted a propensity score weighted analysis of Veterans with CHB who met criteria for U-IT or T-IA from 1/1/2010 to 12/31/2021 (with follow-up through 12/31/2024). Incidence of cirrhosis, HCC, or death were compared between U-IT and T-IA groups. A total of 591 U-IT and 555 T-IA CHB patients were identified (mean age 56.4-57.5 years, 45%-50% non-Hispanic white, 37%-40% African American, 9%-10% Asian, 25%-29% concurrent diabetes, 35%-39% with FIB-4 > 3.25). Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients. Among a predominantly non-Asian cohort of adults with CHB, we observed that HCC risk was lower in U-IT patients compared to T-IA patients. While this contrasts with some studies in Asian countries, these data do emphasize the importance of prioritizing and ensuring timely therapy for CHB patients in the IA phase. However, routine treatment in IT patients remains controversial and more data are needed to better understand long-term outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) between chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients in the untreated immune tolerant (U-IT) vs. treated immune active (T-IA) phases is not clear, and most data are in Asian populations.
Design and population
We conducted a propensity score weighted analysis of Veterans with CHB who met criteria for U-IT or T-IA from 1/1/2010 to 12/31/2021 (with follow-up through 12/31/2024). A total of 591 U-IT and 555 T-IA CHB patients were identified.
Core methods
Incidence of cirrhosis, HCC, or death were compared between U-IT and T-IA groups using a propensity score weighted analysis.
Main results
Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients.
Interpretation and novelty
Among a predominantly non-Asian cohort of adults with CHB, we observed that HCC risk was lower in U-IT patients compared to T-IA patients. While this contrasts with some studies in Asian countries, these data do emphasize the importance of prioritizing and ensuring timely therapy for CHB patients in the IA phase.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of timely therapy for CHB patients in the immune active phase, though routine treatment in immune tolerant patients remains controversial and more data are needed to better understand long-term outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a predominantly non-Asian cohort, which may limit generalizability to Asian populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and potential residual confounding despite propensity score weighting.

Prevalence of hepatitis B infection and its associated factors in rural South India: A valuable investigation.
作者： Jing Zhang, Lin-Jing Wang, Jin-Feng Chi, Zhang J, Wang LJ, Chi JF., Linjing Wang, Junjie Chi

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定印度南部农村地区乙型肝炎感染的 患病率 并确定相关因素,解决区域 流行病学 的知识差距。
设计与对象
在印度南部农村地区进行了一项横断面研究,调查当地人群的乙型肝炎感染 患病率 及相关因素。
核心方法
该研究采用系统的数据收集和分析来评估南印度农村人口的乙型肝炎感染状况并确定潜在的危险因素。
主要结果
该研究报告了乙型肝炎感染的 患病率,并确定了与南印度农村地区乙型肝炎感染发生相关的具体因素。

局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度受到横截面设计和潜在抽样偏差的限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。
