FIB-4 在 慢性乙型肝炎 中检测显着肝纤维化的诊断准确性有限:一项横断面研究。

LIMITED DIAGNOSTIC ACCURACY OF FIB-4 FOR DETECTING SIGNIFICANT LIVER FIBROSIS IN CHRONIC HEPATITIS B: A CROSS-SECTIONAL STUDY.
作者： Elyas Abdullah Alsalamy, Heba Mahmoud Taha Elweshahi, Heba Hany Omar, Ahmed Kamal
摘要中文翻译背景:与 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关的肝病是全世界和埃及的一个严重的公共威胁。埃及乙型肝炎病毒感染的发病率约为1.4%。准确的肝纤维化评估对于乙肝患者的评估和随访至关重要,但传统的活检有其局限性,促使人们使用非侵入性方法,如振动控制瞬时弹性成像 (VCTE),而这些方法在资源有限的 治疗 中心可能无法使用。目的:本研究旨在评估纤维化 4 (FIB-4) 和转氨酶血小板比值指数 (APRI) 与 VCTE 相比在检测成人慢性 HBV 患者显着肝纤维化方面的诊断准确性,从而有可能提供一种更容易获得且成本更低的疾病管理方法。方法 - 在这项横断面研究中,纳入了来自亚历山大大学病毒性肝炎 治疗 单位的 247 名慢性 HBV 受试者。收集的数据包括人口统计详细信息、HBV 病毒载量、肝功能测试、全血计数 (CBC)、国际标准化比值 (INR)、凝血酶原时间、白蛋白、总胆红素和直接胆红素、APRI 评分、FIB-4 评分、影像学和 VCTE 数据。使用IBM SPSS软件进行统计分析,对数据进行适当的定性和定量描述。使用逻辑回归和受试者工作特征曲线(ROC)曲线来识别晚期纤维化的重要预测因素并评估诊断性能。结果:根据 VCTE 结果,247 例 慢性乙型肝炎 患者被分为两组:140 例无或轻度纤维化(F0/1),107 例有明显纤维化(F2-F4)。各组之间的血红蛋白、白蛋白和 INR 水平存在显着差异。显着纤维化组的 FIB-4 和 APRI 评分显着较高,FIB-4 评分中位数为 1.26 vs 0.79,APRI 评分中位数为 0.40 vs 0.27。在多变量分析中,FIB-4 指数仍然是显着纤维化的重要预测因子,优势比为 1.558。当截止值 >0.88 时,FIB-4 评分在区分患有和不患有明显纤维化的患者方面达到了 65.42% 的敏感性和 60.0% 的特异性。结论:虽然 FIB-4 评分的使用在资源有限的环境中可能有用,但由于其中等的敏感性和特异性,可能会发生大量错误分类,这需要进一步结合其他诊断方法以确保肝纤维化的准确分期。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
与 发病率 (HBV) 相关的肝病是全世界和埃及的一个严重的公共威胁,发病率 感染率约为 1.4%。本研究旨在评估纤维化 4 (FIB-4) 和转氨酶血小板比值指数 (APRI) 与 VCTE 相比在检测成年慢性乙型肝炎患者显着肝纤维化方面的诊断准确性。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究包括来自亚历山大大学病毒性肝炎 治疗 单位的 247 名慢性 HBV 受试者。根据 VCTE 结果,患者被分为两组:140 名无或轻度纤维化组 (F0/1) 和 107 名有明显纤维化组 (F2-F4)。
核心方法
收集的数据包括人口统计详细信息、HBV 病毒载量、肝功能测试、全血计数 (CBC)、国际标准化比值 (INR)、凝血酶原时间、白蛋白、总胆红素和直接胆红素、APRI 评分、FIB-4 评分、影像学和 VCTE 数据。IBM SPSS 软件用于统计分析,采用逻辑回归和受试者工作特征曲线 (ROC) 曲线来识别晚期纤维化的重要预测因子并评估诊断性能。
主要结果
各组之间的血红蛋白、白蛋白和 INR 水平存在显着差异,显着纤维化组的中位 FIB-4 评分为 1.26 比 0.79,中位 APRI 评分为 0.40 比 0.27。在多变量分析中,FIB-4 指数仍然是显着纤维化的重要预测因子,优势比为 1.558,截止值 >0.88 时,其敏感性为 65.42%,特异性为 60.0%。
解释与创新
尽管 FIB-4 评分的使用在资源有限的环境中可能有用,但由于其中等的敏感性和特异性,可能会发生大量错误分类。这就需要进一步结合额外的诊断方法,以确保肝纤维化的准确分期。
科研与公卫意义
准确的肝纤维化评估对于乙型肝炎患者的评估和随访至关重要,但传统的活检存在局限性,促使人们使用 VCTE 等非侵入性方法。研究结果表明,虽然 FIB-4 提供了一种更容易获得且成本更低的方法,但其中等诊断性能需要补充其他方法以实现可靠的疾病管理。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告超出 FIB-4 评分的中等敏感性和特异性的具体设计限制或偏差。由于横断面设计和依赖 VCTE 作为参考标准而无需活检确认,证据强度中等。

LIMITED DIAGNOSTIC ACCURACY OF FIB-4 FOR DETECTING SIGNIFICANT LIVER FIBROSIS IN CHRONIC HEPATITIS B: A CROSS-SECTIONAL STUDY.

Authors: Elyas Abdullah Alsalamy, Heba Mahmoud Taha Elweshahi, Heba Hany Omar, Ahmed Kamal
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Liver disease linked to the hepatitis B virus (HBV) is a serious public threat worldwide and in Egypt. The incidence of HBV infection in Egypt is about 1.4%. Accurate liver fibrosis assessments are crucial in the evaluation and follow-up of HBV patients, but traditional biopsies have limitations, prompting the use of non-invasive methods like the vibration controlled transient elastography (VCTE), which may not be available in limited-resource treatment centers. OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to assess the diagnostic accuracy of Fibrosis-4 (FIB-4) and Aminotransferase to Platelet Ratio Index (APRI) compared to VCTE in detecting significant liver fibrosis in adult chronic HBV patients, potentially offering a more accessible and less costly approach to disease management. Methods - In this cross-sectional study, 247 subjects with chronic HBV from Alexandria University Viral Hepatitis Treatment Unit were included. Data collected included demographic details, HBV viral load, liver function tests, Complete Blood Count (CBC), international normalized ratio (INR), prothrombin time, albumin, total and direct bilirubin, APRI score, FIB-4 score, imaging, and VCTE data. IBM SPSS software was used for statistical analysis, with qualitative and quantitative data described appropriately. Logistic regression and receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC) curves were used to identify significant predictors of advanced fibrosis and assess diagnostic performance. RESULTS: Based on VCTE results, 247 chronic hepatitis B patients were classified into two groups: 140 with no or mild fibrosis (F0/1) and 107 with significant fibrosis (F2-F4). Significant differences were found in hemoglobin, albumin, and INR levels between the groups. The FIB-4 and APRI scores were significantly higher in the significant fibrosis group, with median FIB-4 scores of 1.26 vs 0.79 and median APRI scores of 0.40 vs 0.27. The FIB-4 index remained a significant predictor of significant fibrosis in multivariate analysis, with an odds ratio of 1.558. At a cut-off value of >0.88, the FIB-4 score achieved a sensitivity of 65.42% and a specificity of 60.0% in differentiating patients with and without significant fibrosis. CONCLUSION: Although the use of FIB-4 score is potentially useful in resource-limited settings, a significant number of misclassifications can occur due to its moderate sensitivity and specificity which necessitate further incorporation of additional diagnostic methods to ensure the accurate staging of liver fibrosis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Liver disease linked to the hepatitis B virus (HBV) is a serious public threat worldwide and in Egypt, with an infection incidence of about 1.4%. This study aimed to assess the diagnostic accuracy of Fibrosis-4 (FIB-4) and Aminotransferase to Platelet Ratio Index (APRI) compared to VCTE in detecting significant liver fibrosis in adult chronic HBV patients.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study included 247 subjects with chronic HBV from Alexandria University Viral Hepatitis Treatment Unit. Based on VCTE results, patients were classified into two groups: 140 with no or mild fibrosis (F0/1) and 107 with significant fibrosis (F2-F4).
Core methods
Data collected included demographic details, HBV viral load, liver function tests, Complete Blood Count (CBC), international normalized ratio (INR), prothrombin time, albumin, total and direct bilirubin, APRI score, FIB-4 score, imaging, and VCTE data. IBM SPSS software was used for statistical analysis, employing logistic regression and receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC) curves to identify significant predictors of advanced fibrosis and assess diagnostic performance.
Main results
Significant differences were found in hemoglobin, albumin, and INR levels between the groups, with median FIB-4 scores of 1.26 vs 0.79 and median APRI scores of 0.40 vs 0.27 in the significant fibrosis group. The FIB-4 index remained a significant predictor of significant fibrosis in multivariate analysis with an odds ratio of 1.558, and at a cut-off value of >0.88, it achieved a sensitivity of 65.42% and a specificity of 60.0%.
Interpretation and novelty
Although the use of FIB-4 score is potentially useful in resource-limited settings, a significant number of misclassifications can occur due to its moderate sensitivity and specificity. This necessitates the further incorporation of additional diagnostic methods to ensure the accurate staging of liver fibrosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
Accurate liver fibrosis assessments are crucial in the evaluation and follow-up of HBV patients, but traditional biopsies have limitations, prompting the use of non-invasive methods like VCTE. The findings suggest that while FIB-4 offers a more accessible and less costly approach, its moderate diagnostic performance requires supplementation with other methods for reliable disease management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations or biases beyond the moderate sensitivity and specificity of the FIB-4 score. The evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and the reliance on VCTE as the reference standard without biopsy confirmation.

印度南部农村乙型肝炎感染及其相关因素的 患病率:对信件的回应。

Prevalence of hepatitis B infection and its associated factors in rural South India: Response to correspondence.
作者： Rajalatchumi Poorasamy, Sadhana Subramanian, Palanivel Chinnakali, Poorasamy R, Subramanian S, Chinnakali P.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定印度南部农村地区乙型肝炎感染的 患病率 并确定相关因素,解决区域 流行病学 的知识差距。
设计与对象
这是一项在印度南部农村地区进行的横断面研究,重点关注特定人群,以评估乙型肝炎 患病率 及相关因素。
核心方法
该研究采用血清学检测来检测乙型肝炎感染,并通过结构化问卷和统计方法分析人口和行为因素。
主要结果
该研究报告了南印度农村人口中乙型肝炎感染的 患病率 情况,并确定了与较高感染率相关的具体因素。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为强调了农村地区有针对性的干预措施的必要性,这在乙型肝炎 流行病学 的区域背景下具有新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为印度农村地区乙型肝炎预防和控制的公共卫生策略提供了信息,强调了 疫苗接种 和宣传计划的重要性。
局限与证据强度
尽管该研究提供了有价值的区域数据,但受横截面设计和潜在抽样偏差的限制,证据强度中等。

Question and background
The study aimed to determine the prevalence of hepatitis B infection and identify associated factors in rural South India, addressing a knowledge gap in regional epidemiology.
Design and population
This was a cross-sectional study conducted in rural South India, focusing on a specific population to assess hepatitis B prevalence and associated factors.
Core methods
The study employed serological testing to detect hepatitis B infection and analyzed demographic and behavioral factors through structured questionnaires and statistical methods.
Main results
The study reported the prevalence of hepatitis B infection in the rural South Indian population and identified specific factors associated with higher infection rates.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the need for targeted interventions in rural areas, with novelty in the regional context of hepatitis B epidemiology.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform public health strategies for hepatitis B prevention and control in rural India, emphasizing the importance of vaccination and awareness programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, limited by the cross-sectional design and potential sampling bias, though the study provides valuable regional data.

乌兹别克斯坦慢性丁型肝炎病毒感染患者的晚期慢性肝病发病率很高。

High Rates of Advanced Chronic Liver Disease in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection in Uzbekistan.
作者： Malika E Khodjaeva, Aziza S Khikmatullaeva, Nargiz S Ibadullaeva, Gulnara K Khudaykulova, Munisa M Ilyasova, Kamila U Eshbayeva, Dildorakhon N Abdurakhmanova, Helenie Kefalakes, Petra Huber, Michael J Gebel
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:慢性乙型肝炎 病毒(HBV)感染在乌兹别克斯坦流行。先前的数据显示,HBsAg 阳性个体的抗丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 抗体 患病率 较高。关于乌兹别克斯坦慢性 HDV 感染患者的疾病严重程度、治疗 可用性和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患病率 的数据有限。方法:病毒学科学研究所 (SRIOV) 是乌兹别克斯坦领先的病毒性肝炎医学和科学参考中心。在这项回顾性研究中,收集并分析了 2023 年 6 月至 2024 年 5 月期间所有就诊于 SRIoV 的 HBsAg 阳性患者的流行病学、临床和实验室数据。比较了 HDV 合并感染和 HBV 单一感染患者的数据。结果:纳入了 1393 名患者的数据。HDV 合并感染比 HBV 单一感染更为普遍 (73.9% [n = 1030/1393]) vs. 26.1% [n = 363/1393]。HDV 感染患者明显年轻(41.9 vs. 45.4,p 正常值上限。由于筛查措施不足,两个患者组的 HCC 检出率均较低(HDV:n = 7/1030,HBV:n = 11/363)。结论:在这项针对 1393 名患者的回顾性 队列研究 中,HDV 合并感染超过 HBV 单一感染患病率 和疾病严重程度。大多数 HDV 感染患者患有晚期肝病,目前缺乏针对 HDV 的治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染在乌兹别克斯坦流行,但关于慢性 HDV 感染患者的疾病严重程度、治疗 可用性和肝细胞癌 患病率 的数据有限。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究分析了 2023 年 6 月至 2024 年 5 月期间到病毒学科学研究所就诊的 1393 名 HBsAg 阳性患者的数据,比较了 HDV 合并感染组和 HBV 单一感染组。
核心方法
收集并分析所有纳入患者的流行病学、临床和实验室数据,以评估疾病特征和结果。
主要结果
HDV 合并感染比 HBV 单一感染更为普遍(73.9% [n = 1030/1393] vs. 26.1% [n = 363/1393]),HDV 感染患者明显更年轻(41.9 vs. 45.4,p < 0.001)并且具有较高的 ALT 水平。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,在本 队列研究 中,HDV 合并感染在 患病率 和疾病严重程度方面均超过 HBV 单一感染。
科研与公卫意义
大多数丁型肝炎病毒感染患者均患有晚期肝病,目前无法获得针对丁型肝炎病毒的治疗,这突出表明需要改进筛查和 治疗 的获取。
局限与证据强度
由于筛查措施不足,两组 HCC 检出率均较低,且由于回顾性设计和单中心数据,证据强度中等。

High Rates of Advanced Chronic Liver Disease in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection in Uzbekistan.

Authors: Malika E Khodjaeva, Aziza S Khikmatullaeva, Nargiz S Ibadullaeva, Gulnara K Khudaykulova, Munisa M Ilyasova, Kamila U Eshbayeva, Dildorakhon N Abdurakhmanova, Helenie Kefalakes, Petra Huber, Michael J Gebel
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is endemic in Uzbekistan. Previous data showed a high prevalence of anti-hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibodies in HBsAg-positive individuals. Limited data are available on disease severity, treatment availability and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) prevalence in patients with chronic HDV infection in Uzbekistan. METHODS: The Scientific Research Institute of Virology (SRIoV) is the leading medical and scientific reference centre for viral hepatitis in Uzbekistan. In this retrospective study, epidemiological, clinical and laboratory data were collected and analysed from all HBsAg-positive patients presenting to the SRIoV between June 2023 and May 2024. Data from HDV-coinfected and HBV-monoinfected patients were compared. RESULTS: Data of 1393 individual patients were included. HDV coinfection was more prevalent than HBV-monoinfection (73.9% [n = 1030/1393]) vs. 26.1% [n = 363/1393]. HDV-infected patients were significantly younger (41.9 vs. 45.4, p upper limit of normal. The HCC detection rate was low in both patient groups due to insufficient screening measures (HDV: n = 7/1030, HBV: n = 11/363). CONCLUSIONS: In this retrospective cohort of 1393 individual patients, HDV coinfection exceeds HBV monoinfection in prevalence and disease severity. Most HDV-infected patients present with advanced liver disease and currently lack access to HDV-directed therapy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is endemic in Uzbekistan, but limited data are available on disease severity, treatment availability and hepatocellular carcinoma prevalence in patients with chronic HDV infection.
Design and population
This retrospective study analysed data from 1393 HBsAg-positive patients presenting to the Scientific Research Institute of Virology between June 2023 and May 2024, comparing HDV-coinfected and HBV-monoinfected groups.
Core methods
Epidemiological, clinical and laboratory data were collected and analysed from all included patients to assess disease characteristics and outcomes.
Main results
HDV coinfection was more prevalent than HBV monoinfection (73.9% [n = 1030/1393] vs. 26.1% [n = 363/1393]), with HDV-infected patients being significantly younger (41.9 vs. 45.4, p < 0.001) and having higher ALT levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that in this cohort, HDV coinfection exceeds HBV monoinfection in both prevalence and disease severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
Most HDV-infected patients present with advanced liver disease and currently lack access to HDV-directed therapy, highlighting a need for improved screening and treatment access.
Limitations and evidence strength
The HCC detection rate was low in both groups due to insufficient screening measures, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the retrospective design and single-centre data.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

多组学和人工智能提名 PCLAF 作为 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌的预后和药物靶标。

Multi-omics and artificial intelligence nominate PCLAF as a prognostic and druggable target for hepatitis B virus-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
作者： Feihong Shu, Yidan Chen, Xiaorong Yang, Guoyou Gou, Guonian Li, Jie Yu, Fang Wang, Youjia Liu, Qian Du, Jingyu Xu
摘要中文翻译背景:PCLAF(PCNA钳相关因子)是一种参与DNA复制和DNA修复的蛋白质。据报道,PCLAF 异常表达在多种恶性肿瘤中存在,并且与肿瘤进展和不良临床结果相关。然而,PCLAF 在肝细胞癌 (HCC) 中的生物学作用仍不完全清楚,特别是其与肿瘤免疫微环境的关系。因此,本研究旨在通过综合多组学分析、实验验证和药物筛选方法,系统研究 PCLAF 在 HCC 中的临床意义、生物学功能和治疗潜力。方法:本研究采用综合多组学框架系统研究PCLAF在肝细胞癌(HCC)中的分子特征和生物学功能。对来自 GEO 数据库(GSE83148 和 GSE121248)和 TCGA-LIHC 队列研究 的转录组数据集进行分析,以识别差异表达的基因,然后通过蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络构建和机器学习算法来筛选和验证关键枢纽基因。随后进行泛癌分析、临床病理相关分析、生存分析和受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线分析,以评估PCLAF的临床意义。分析单细胞 RNA 测序数据 (GSE202642),以表征 乙型肝炎病毒 相关 HCC 的细胞异质性并鉴定 PCLAF 相关细胞群。通过功能模块评分和基因集富集分析来研究循环 T 细胞的生物学特征。将空间转录组数据 (GSE245908) 与反卷积、细胞间通讯和空间调控分析相结合,以探索与 PCLAF 相关的空间分布模式和细胞间相互作用。为了确定针对 PCLAF 的潜在治疗药物,进行了虚拟药物筛选、分子对接和分子动力学模拟。最后,使用临床标本和HCC细胞系进行实验验证。通过免疫组织化学、RT-qPCR、蛋白质印迹、集落形成、伤口愈合、Transwell 迁移和 CCK-8 测定来评估 PCLAF 对 HCC 细胞增殖和迁移的影响。结果:通过整合转录组分析、蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络构建和机器学习方法,PCLAF被确定为与HCC相关的关键候选基因。泛癌和临床分析表明,PCLAF 表达升高与多种癌症类型的晚期肿瘤分期、较高的病理分级和不良预后相关。单细胞转录组分析表明 PCLAF 主要富集于循环 T 细胞。功能表征表明,循环 T 细胞表现出过度增殖但功能受限的表型,其特征是增殖增强,同时活化和细胞毒性降低。空间转录组和细胞间通讯分析进一步确定了潜在的上皮细胞-循环T细胞相互作用网络,其中MIF-CD74-CXCR4信号轴代表了主要的通讯途径。为了探索治疗机会,进行了人工智能辅助药物筛选、分子对接和分子动力学模拟,最终确定 BRD-K12189280 是一种有前途的靶向 PCLAF 的候选化合物。实验验证证实PCLAF在HCC组织中显着过表达,其敲低可显着抑制体外HCC细胞的增殖、迁移和活力。结论:我们的研究结果表明,PCLAF 在 HBV 相关肝细胞癌中显着上调,并促进恶性细胞表型。多组学分析揭示了 PCLAF 表达与过度增殖但功能受限的循环 T 细胞状态之间的密切关联,强调了肿瘤进展与免疫微环境重塑之间的潜在联系。这些结果表明 PCLAF 是 HCC 中有前途的预后生物标志物和治疗靶点,而 BRD-K12189280 则成为未来药物开发的潜在候选化合物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
PCLAF 是一种参与 DNA 复制和修复的蛋白质,虽然其异常表达与恶性肿瘤有关,但其在肝细胞癌 (HCC) 中的生物学作用仍不完全清楚,特别是在肿瘤免疫微环境方面。本研究旨在通过综合多组学分析、实验验证和药物筛选方法,系统研究 PCLAF 在 HCC 中的临床意义、生物学功能和治疗潜力。
设计与对象
本研究采用了综合多组学框架,使用来自 GEO 数据库(GSE83148 和 GSE121248)的转录组数据集、TCGA-LIHC 队列研究、单细胞 RNA 测序数据(GSE202642)和空间转录组数据(GSE245908)。该研究包括泛癌分析、临床病理学相关性、生存分析以及使用临床标本和 HCC 细胞系的验证。
核心方法
作者进行了蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络构建、机器学习算法和基因集富集分析,以识别和表征关键的枢纽基因。为了评估生物功能和治疗潜力,他们利用了免疫组织化学、RT-qPCR、蛋白质印迹、集落形成、伤口愈合、Transwell 迁移、CCK-8 检测以及通过分子对接和分子动力学模拟进行的人工智能辅助虚拟药物筛选。
主要结果
PCLAF被确定为与HCC相关的关键候选基因,其表达升高与肿瘤分期晚期、病理分级较高和预后不良相关。单细胞分析显示 PCLAF 主要富集于表现出过度增殖但功能受限表型的循环 T 细胞,空间分析通过 MIF-CD74-CXCR4 轴确定了上皮细胞-循环 T 细胞相互作用网络。实验验证证实 PCLAF 在 HCC 组织中过度表达,并表明其敲低可抑制增殖、迁移和活力,而 BRD-K12189280 被认为是一种有前途的候选化合物。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,PCLAF 在 HBV 相关肝细胞癌中显着上调,并促进恶性细胞表型。该研究强调了 PCLAF 表达与过度增殖但功能受限的循环 T 细胞状态之间的新联系,表明肿瘤进展与免疫微环境重塑之间存在联系。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明 PCLAF 是 HCC 中有前途的预后生物标志物和治疗靶点,而 BRD-K12189280 则成为未来药物开发的潜在候选化合物。该研究提供了对肿瘤进展与免疫微环境之间相互作用的见解,这可能为未来的治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体的设计限制、偏见、混杂因素或证据强度的详细评估。该研究仅依赖于抽象证据,这限制了评估研究结果的完整方法论严谨性和普遍性的能力。

Multi-omics and artificial intelligence nominate PCLAF as a prognostic and druggable target for hepatitis B virus-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.

Authors: Feihong Shu, Yidan Chen, Xiaorong Yang, Guoyou Gou, Guonian Li, Jie Yu, Fang Wang, Youjia Liu, Qian Du, Jingyu Xu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: PCLAF (PCNA clamp-associated factor) is a protein involved in DNA replication and DNA repair. Aberrant PCLAF expression has been reported in multiple malignancies and is associated with tumor progression and poor clinical outcomes. However, the biological role of PCLAF in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) remains incompletely understood, particularly with respect to its relationship with the tumor immune microenvironment. Therefore, this study aimed to systematically investigate the clinical significance, biological functions, and therapeutic potential of PCLAF in HCC through integrated multi-omics analyses, experimental validation, and drug screening approaches. METHODS: In this study, an integrative multi-omics framework was employed to systematically investigate the molecular characteristics and biological functions of PCLAF in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Transcriptomic datasets from the GEO database (GSE83148 and GSE121248) and the TCGA-LIHC cohort were analyzed to identify differentially expressed genes, followed by protein-protein interaction network construction and machine learning algorithms to screen and validate key hub genes. Pan-cancer analysis, clinicopathological correlation analysis, survival analysis, and receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis were subsequently performed to evaluate the clinical significance of PCLAF. Single-cell RNA sequencing data (GSE202642) were analyzed to characterize the cellular heterogeneity of hepatitis B virus-associated HCC and identify PCLAF-associated cell populations. Functional module scoring and gene set enrichment analysis were performed to investigate the biological features of Cycling T cells. Spatial transcriptomic data (GSE245908) were integrated with deconvolution, cell-cell communication, and spatial regulatory analyses to explore the spatial distribution patterns and intercellular interactions associated with PCLAF. To identify potential therapeutic agents targeting PCLAF, virtual drug screening, molecular docking, and molecular dynamics simulations were conducted. Finally, clinical specimens and HCC cell lines were used for experimental validation. Immunohistochemistry, RT-qPCR, western blotting, colony formation, wound-healing, Transwell migration, and CCK-8 assays were performed to evaluate the effects of PCLAF on HCC cell proliferation and migration. RESULTS: Through integrated transcriptomic analysis, protein-protein interaction network construction, and machine learning approaches, PCLAF was identified as a key candidate gene associated with HCC. Pan-cancer and clinical analyses demonstrated that elevated PCLAF expression was associated with advanced tumor stage, higher pathological grade, and poor prognosis across multiple cancer types. Single-cell transcriptomic analysis revealed that PCLAF was predominantly enriched in Cycling T cells. Functional characterization showed that Cycling T cells exhibited a hyperproliferative but functionally restricted phenotype, characterized by enhanced proliferation accompanied by reduced activation and cytotoxicity. Spatial transcriptomic and cell-cell communication analyses further identified a potential epithelial cell-Cycling T cell interaction network, in which the MIF-CD74-CXCR4 signaling axis represented a major communication pathway. To explore therapeutic opportunities, AI-assisted drug screening, molecular docking, and molecular dynamics simulations were performed, leading to the identification of BRD-K12189280 as a promising candidate compound targeting PCLAF. Experimental validation confirmed that PCLAF was significantly overexpressed in HCC tissues, and its knockdown markedly inhibited the proliferation, migration, and viability of HCC cells in vitro. CONCLUSION: Our findings demonstrate that PCLAF is significantly upregulated in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma and promotes malignant cellular phenotypes. Multi-omics analyses revealed a close association between PCLAF expression and a hyperproliferative but functionally restricted Cycling T-cell state, highlighting a potential link between tumor progression and immune microenvironment remodeling. These results identify PCLAF as a promising prognostic biomarker and therapeutic target in HCC, while BRD-K12189280 emerges as a potential candidate compound for future drug development.

Question and background
PCLAF is a protein involved in DNA replication and repair, and while its aberrant expression is linked to malignancies, its biological role in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) remains incompletely understood, particularly regarding the tumor immune microenvironment. This study aimed to systematically investigate the clinical significance, biological functions, and therapeutic potential of PCLAF in HCC through integrated multi-omics analyses, experimental validation, and drug screening approaches.
Design and population
This study employed an integrative multi-omics framework using transcriptomic datasets from the GEO database (GSE83148 and GSE121248), the TCGA-LIHC cohort, single-cell RNA sequencing data (GSE202642), and spatial transcriptomic data (GSE245908). The research included pan-cancer analysis, clinicopathological correlation, survival analysis, and validation using clinical specimens and HCC cell lines.
Core methods
The authors performed protein-protein interaction network construction, machine learning algorithms, and gene set enrichment analysis to identify and characterize key hub genes. To evaluate biological functions and therapeutic potential, they utilized immunohistochemistry, RT-qPCR, western blotting, colony formation, wound-healing, Transwell migration, CCK-8 assays, and AI-assisted virtual drug screening with molecular docking and molecular dynamics simulations.
Main results
PCLAF was identified as a key candidate gene associated with HCC, with elevated expression linked to advanced tumor stage, higher pathological grade, and poor prognosis. Single-cell analysis revealed PCLAF was predominantly enriched in Cycling T cells exhibiting a hyperproliferative but functionally restricted phenotype, and spatial analysis identified an epithelial cell-Cycling T cell interaction network via the MIF-CD74-CXCR4 axis. Experimental validation confirmed PCLAF overexpression in HCC tissues and showed that its knockdown inhibited proliferation, migration, and viability, while BRD-K12189280 was identified as a promising candidate compound.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that PCLAF is significantly upregulated in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma and promotes malignant cellular phenotypes. The study highlights a novel link between PCLAF expression and a hyperproliferative but functionally restricted Cycling T-cell state, suggesting a connection between tumor progression and immune microenvironment remodeling.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results identify PCLAF as a promising prognostic biomarker and therapeutic target in HCC, while BRD-K12189280 emerges as a potential candidate compound for future drug development. The study provides insights into the interplay between tumor progression and the immune microenvironment that may inform future therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, bias, confounding factors, or a detailed assessment of the evidence strength. The study relies on abstract-only evidence, which restricts the ability to evaluate the full methodological rigor and generalizability of the findings.

梅毒螺旋体阳性献血中发现的隐匿性 乙型肝炎病毒 混合感染的高 患病率:对降低 HBV 风险的影响

High Prevalence of Occult Hepatitis B Virus Co-Infection Identified in Treponema Pallidum-Positive Blood Donations: Implications for HBV Risk Reduction
作者： Xianlin Ye, Xiaoxuan Xu, Jinfeng Zeng, He Xie, Jujun Sun, Baoren He, Limin Chen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于梅毒 发病率 的上升以及认识到性接触是 HBV 传播 的主要途径,需要开展这项研究来调查梅毒阳性献血者中隐匿性 发病率 (HBV) 感染的 发病率,并评估梅毒螺旋体 (TP) 检测对评估 OBI 相关血液供应风险的替代价值。
设计与对象
这项研究分析了总共 64,871 人的献血样本,重点关注了 252 例抗 TP ELISA 阳性献血者,这些献血者经 TPPA 检测进一步证实,其中 138 例确诊梅毒阳性但 NAT 阴性献血者纳入分析。
核心方法
确认梅毒阳性的献血者接受了额外的 HBV 合并感染检测,包括针对 HBsAg、抗 HBs、HBeAg、抗 HBe 和抗 HBc 的 ECLI,然后进行 HBV DNA 定量的 qPCR、针对 S 和 BCP/PC 区域的嵌套 PCR 以及通过 DNA 测序进行分子表征。
主要结果
138例梅毒阳性献血者中,78例(56.5%)抗-HBc阳性,88例(63.7%)抗-HBs阳性,7例(5.1%)诊断为OBI合并感染;序列分析显示3例B基因型和1例C基因型,以及S区和BCP/PC区的数个突变。
解释与创新
作者解释说,梅毒阳性献血者中未检测到的 OBI 较高的 患病率 支持梅毒筛查作为血液供应中 HBV 相关风险替代标记的潜力,而 HBV 基因组 中的 突变 可能导致低病毒载量和 HBsAg 检测失败。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,对献血者进行梅毒筛查可以作为一项额外的保障措施,排除与 OBI 合并感染的献血者,从而提高血液安全并降低 HBV 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究仅限于对已确认的梅毒阳性捐赠者的单中心分析,并且 OBI 合并感染的样本量较小(n = 7),这可能限制了普遍性。

High Prevalence of Occult Hepatitis B Virus Co-Infection Identified in Treponema Pallidum-Positive Blood Donations: Implications for HBV Risk Reduction

Authors: Xianlin Ye, Xiaoxuan Xu, Jinfeng Zeng, He Xie, Jujun Sun, Baoren He, Limin Chen
Original abstract (English)
Over the past decade, the incidence of infectious syphilis has been on the rise in the general Chinese population. Consequently, Treponema Pallidum (TP) testing has been proposed as a surrogate marker for sexually transmitted pathogens and for monitoring risky sexual behaviors among blood donors globally. In addition, sexual contact with individuals chronically infected with hepatitis B virus (HBV) is recognized as one of the primary routes of HBV transmission. Blood donors may acquire HBV infection through sexual contact with chronically infected partners, particularly with occult hepatitis B infections (OBIs), which are characterized by intermittent and extremely low viral loads. Therefore, the prevalence of OBIs among syphilis-positive blood donations and the corresponding risks to blood safety require further investigation. This study aimed to investigate the prevalence of OBIs among syphilis-positive blood donors and assess the surrogate value of TP testing for evaluating OBI-related risks to blood supply. After routine screening using serological assays and nucleic acid testing (NAT), blood donation samples with positive anti-TP enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) results were collected and further confirmed by the Treponema Pallidum Particle Agglutination Assay (TPPA). For blood donations confirmed positive for syphilis, further tests were performed to characterize whether the donations had HBV co-infection, including electrochemiluminescence immunoassay (ECLI) for the detection of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), anti-hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs), hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg), anti-hepatitis B e antibody (anti-HBe), and anti-hepatitis B core antibody (anti-HBc). Additionally, quantitative real-time polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) was used for HBV DNA quantification, and nested PCRs for the S and basal core promoter/precore (BCP/PC) region were conducted in combination with high-volume nucleic acid extraction. Subsequently, molecular characterization of HBV DNA in these co-infected samples was carried out by DNA sequencing to analyze the viral genetic features. Of 252 anti-TP ELISA+ donations screened from 64,871 blood samples, 138 (138/250, 55.2%) donations were confirmed syphilis-positive but NAT−, among which 78 (78/138, 56.5%) were anti-HBc-positive, and 88 (88/138, 63.7%) had anti-HBs. Notably, seven donations (7/138, 5.1%) were diagnosed as OBI co-infections, and available sequence analysis revealed that three cases were genotype B and one case was genotype C. In addition, several mutations in the S region of the HBV genome were identified, including Q101R, K122R, Q129H, T131N, M133T, G145R, and Y161F mutations. Furthermore, nucleotide mutations such as T1719G, A1752T, G1896A, and A1762T/G1764A in the BCP/PC regions were also detected in these OBI donations. These mutations may contribute to the extremely low HBV viral loads and/or failure in HBsAg detection, collectively leading to OBIs. These data indicate that syphilis screening of blood donors has potential to serve as an additional safeguard measure for excluding donations co-infected with OBIs. The high prevalence of undetected OBIs in syphilis-positive blood donors further supports that syphilis screening has the potential to serve as a surrogate marker for HBV-related risks in the blood supply.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to investigate the prevalence of occult hepatitis B virus (HBV) infections among syphilis-positive blood donors and assess the surrogate value of Treponema pallidum (TP) testing for evaluating OBI-related risks to blood supply, given the rising incidence of syphilis and the recognition of sexual contact as a primary route of HBV transmission.
Design and population
This study analyzed blood donation samples from a total of 64,871 individuals, focusing on 252 anti-TP ELISA-positive donations that were further confirmed by TPPA testing, with 138 confirmed syphilis-positive but NAT-negative donations included in the analysis.
Core methods
Blood donations confirmed positive for syphilis underwent additional testing for HBV co-infection, including ECLI for HBsAg, anti-HBs, HBeAg, anti-HBe, and anti-HBc, followed by qPCR for HBV DNA quantification, nested PCRs for the S and BCP/PC regions, and molecular characterization via DNA sequencing.
Main results
Among 138 syphilis-positive donations, 78 (56.5%) were anti-HBc-positive, 88 (63.7%) had anti-HBs, and 7 (5.1%) were diagnosed as OBI co-infections; sequence analysis revealed three cases of genotype B and one case of genotype C, along with several mutations in the S region and BCP/PC regions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the high prevalence of undetected OBIs in syphilis-positive blood donors supports the potential of syphilis screening as a surrogate marker for HBV-related risks in the blood supply, with mutations in the HBV genome potentially contributing to the low viral loads and HBsAg detection failure.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that syphilis screening of blood donors could serve as an additional safeguard measure to exclude donations co-infected with OBIs, thereby enhancing blood safety and reducing HBV transmission risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study was limited to a single-center analysis of confirmed syphilis-positive donations, and the sample size for OBI co-infections was small (n=7), which may limit generalizability.

乙型肝炎 (Hepatitis B) 流行病学调查

Skrining HBSAG untuk Deteksi Dini Infeksi Hepatitis B pada Populasi Dewasa di Kelurahan Semanan
作者： Donatila Mano S, Alexander Halim Santoso
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎仍然是一个公共卫生问题,有可能导致慢性肝病、肝硬化和肝细胞癌。通过基于社区的筛查进行早期发现对于提高病例识别和预防 传播 至关重要。此次社区服务活动旨在对社区进行HBsAg筛查并开展乙型肝炎教育。共有 155 名参与者参与,平均年龄为 46.49 ± 10.29 岁,年龄范围为 19-80 岁。参与者以女性为主,占 109 人(70.3%),男性占 46 人(29.7%)。筛查结果显示,153 名参与者(98.7%)HBsAg 阴性,2 名参与者(1.3%)HBsAg 阳性。基于性别的分布表明,所有阳性病例均发现于女性参与者中。HBsAg 阳性结果比例较低,表明大多数筛查个体没有活动性乙型肝炎感染。尽管如此,阳性病例的发现凸显了定期筛查和健康教育作为早期发现、预防肝病并发症和控制社区内乙型肝炎 传播 策略的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎仍然是一个公共卫生问题,有可能导致慢性肝病、肝硬化和肝细胞癌。
设计与对象
参与者以女性为主,占 109 人(70.3%),男性占 46 人(29.7%)。
核心方法
共有 155 名参与者参与,平均年龄为 46.49 ± 10.29 岁,年龄范围为 19-80 岁。
主要结果
筛查结果显示,153 名参与者(98.7%)HBsAg 阴性,2 名参与者(1.3%)HBsAg 阳性。
解释与创新
HBsAg 阳性结果比例较低,表明大多数筛查个体没有活动性乙型肝炎感染。
科研与公卫意义
尽管如此,阳性病例的发现凸显了定期筛查和健康教育作为早期发现、预防肝病并发症和控制社区内乙型肝炎 传播 策略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Skrining HBSAG untuk Deteksi Dini Infeksi Hepatitis B pada Populasi Dewasa di Kelurahan Semanan

Authors: Donatila Mano S, Alexander Halim Santoso
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B remains a public health problem with the potential to cause chronic liver disease, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Early detection through community-based screening is essential to improve case identification and prevent transmission. This community service activity aimed to conduct HBsAg screening and provide education regarding hepatitis B among the community. A total of 155 participants were involved, with a mean age of 46.49 ± 10.29 years and an age range of 19–80 years. Most participants were female, accounting for 109 individuals (70.3%), while males accounted for 46 individuals (29.7%). Screening results showed that 153 participants (98.7%) were HBsAg negative and 2 participants (1.3%) were HBsAg positive. Gender-based distribution demonstrated that all positive cases were found among female participants. The low proportion of HBsAg-positive results indicates that most screened individuals did not have active hepatitis B infection. Nevertheless, the identification of positive cases highlights the importance of regular screening and health education as strategies for early detection, prevention of liver disease complications, and control of hepatitis B transmission within the community.

抗病毒药物 源自 FUBP1-IN-1 的新型化合物 0411 通过上调 DUSP5 对抗 乙型肝炎病毒 的活性

Antiviral activity of novel chemical compound 0411, derived from FUBP1-IN-1 against hepatitis B virus by upregulation of DUSP5
作者： Shuang Zhao, Haisha Wei, Yiyang Liang, Hui Fan
摘要中文翻译目前的一线药物很少能对慢性乙型肝炎患者实现功能性治愈,这促使我们探索新的抗HBV治疗策略。在这项研究中,我们发现新型化合物 0411(FUBP1 抑制剂 FUBPI-IN-1 的衍生物)在体外和体内均显着抑制 HBV 转录。转录组测序表明,0411 治疗 显着上调双特异性磷酸酶 5 (DUSP5) 基因的表达,该基因是 MAPK 途径的关键调节因子。进一步的功能研究表明,化合物0411的抗病毒药物活性依赖于DUSP5。从机制上讲,HepG2-NTCP 细胞中的 0411 治疗 显着增强了活性组蛋白修饰在 DUSP5 启动子上的沉积。总之,我们的研究结果证明了 0411 作为有效抗 HBV 转录药物的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前一线药物很少能达到慢性乙型肝炎患者的功能性治愈效果,这迫切需要探索新的抗HBV治疗策略。
设计与对象
该研究调查了新型化合物 0411(FUBP1-IN-1 的衍生物)在体外和体内针对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 的 乙型肝炎病毒 活性。
核心方法
转录组测序证明 0411 治疗 显着上调双特异性磷酸酶 5 (DUSP5) 基因的表达,该基因是 MAPK 途径的关键调节因子。
主要结果
新型化学化合物0411在体外和体内均显著抑制了HBV转录,且其抗病毒药物活性依赖于DUSP5。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,0411 通过增强活性组蛋白修饰在 DUSP5 启动子上的沉积,具有作为抗 HBV 转录的有效药物的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该研究通过上调DUSP5来靶向HBV转录,为慢性乙型肝炎患者提供了一种新的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内实验,还需要进一步的临床试验来验证化合物0411在人体中的有效性和安全性。

Antiviral activity of novel chemical compound 0411, derived from FUBP1-IN-1 against hepatitis B virus by upregulation of DUSP5

Authors: Shuang Zhao, Haisha Wei, Yiyang Liang, Hui Fan
Original abstract (English)
Current first-line drugs rarely achieve functional curesfor chronic hepatitis B patients, which urges us to explore novel anti-HBV therapeutic strategies. In this study, we found that the novel chemical compound 0411, a derivative of the FUBP1 inhibitor, FUBPI-IN-1, significantly suppressed HBV transcription both in vitro and in vivo. Transcriptome sequencing demonstrated that 0411 treatment markedly upregulated the expression of the dual-specificity phosphatase 5 (DUSP5) gene, a crucial regulator of the MAPK pathway. Further functional studies illustrated that the antiviral activity of compound 0411 was dependent on DUSP5. Mechanismly, 0411 treatment in HepG2-NTCP cells enhances the deposition of active histone modifications on the promoter of DUSP5 significantly. In conclusion, our findings demonstrate the potential of 0411 as an effective agent against HBV transcription.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Current first-line drugs rarely achieve functional cures for chronic hepatitis B patients, which urges the exploration of novel anti-HBV therapeutic strategies.
Design and population
The study investigated the antiviral activity of the novel chemical compound 0411, a derivative of FUBP1-IN-1, against hepatitis B virus (HBV) both in vitro and in vivo.
Core methods
Transcriptome sequencing was used to demonstrate that 0411 treatment markedly upregulated the expression of the dual-specificity phosphatase 5 (DUSP5) gene, a crucial regulator of the MAPK pathway.
Main results
The novel chemical compound 0411 significantly suppressed HBV transcription both in vitro and in vivo, and its antiviral activity was dependent on DUSP5.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the potential of 0411 as an effective agent against HBV transcription by enhancing the deposition of active histone modifications on the promoter of DUSP5.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides a novel therapeutic strategy for chronic hepatitis B patients by targeting HBV transcription through the upregulation of DUSP5.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo experiments, and further clinical trials are needed to validate the efficacy and safety of compound 0411 in humans.

北克什米尔的一项横断面研究中的 血清流行率 以及乙型和丙型肝炎的相关危险因素

Seroprevalence and associated risk factors of Hepatitis B and C in a cross sectional study from North Kashmir
作者： Shakil U. Rehman, Neesa Majeed, Tanveer Ahmad Mir, Nazima Nazir, Showkat Ahmad Lone, Junaid Ahmad
摘要中文翻译印度地方性 HBV 和 HCV 感染负担较高,全国 HBV 感染 血清流行率 为 0.95%,HCV 感染率为 0.32%。在查谟和克什米尔,乙肝病毒和丙肝病毒的 患病率 被认为远高于全国平均水平。HBV 和 HCV 感染的 发病率 不断增加,需要针对特定​​区域进行筛查,以评估疾病负担、危险因素并计划控制措施。2023年9月至2024年11月在印度北克什米尔的一家三级医院进行了一项横断面研究。采用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)方法筛查乙型肝炎表面抗原和丙型肝炎病毒抗体。采用Logistic回归模型对HBV、HCV感染的危险因素进行统计分析。使用 AUC-ROC 和 F1 评分评估模型。总共对 1081 份和 1087 份样本进行了 HBsAg 和抗 HCV 分析,结果显示 患病率 分别为 3.42% 和 18.30%。在已确定的危险因素中,静脉注射药物(aOR = 18.4,P = 0.000)代表 HCV 传播 最常见的手段。此外,发烧、患者类型、20-40 岁年龄组和计划手术等其他危险因素与 HCV 感染存在统计学显着相关性。该研究强调了与全国 患病率 相比,克什米尔北部的 HBV 和 HCV 感染负担令人震惊。HCV 感染和 HBV/HCV 混合感染的高 患病率,特别是在年轻人中,需要进行紧急、区域特定的筛查和早期 诊断,以防止病毒性肝炎感染的进一步传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
印度地方性 HBV 和 HCV 感染负担较高,全国 HBV 感染 血清流行率 为 0.95%,HCV 感染率为 0.32%。在查谟和克什米尔,乙肝病毒和丙肝病毒的 患病率 被认为远高于全国平均水平。
设计与对象
2023 年 9 月至 2024 年 11 月在印度北克什米尔的一家三级医院进行了一项横断面研究。该研究分别分析了 1081 个和 1087 个样本的 HBsAg 和抗 HCV。
核心方法
采用Logistic回归模型对HBV、HCV感染的危险因素进行统计分析。使用 AUC-ROC 和 F1 评分评估模型。采用酶联免疫吸附试验(ELISA)方法筛查乙型肝炎表面抗原和抗HCV抗体。
主要结果
总共对 1081 份和 1087 份样本进行了 HBsAg 和抗 HCV 分析,结果显示 患病率 分别为 3.42% 和 18.30%。在已确定的危险因素中,静脉注射药物(aOR = 18.4,P = 0.000)代表 HCV 传播 最常见的手段。此外,发烧、患者类型、20-40 岁年龄组和计划手术等其他危险因素与 HCV 感染存在统计学显着相关性。
解释与创新
该研究强调了与全国 患病率 相比,克什米尔北部的 HBV 和 HCV 感染负担令人震惊。HCV 感染和 HBV/HCV 混合感染的高 患病率,特别是在年轻人中,需要进行紧急、区域特定的筛查和早期 诊断,以防止病毒性肝炎感染的进一步传播。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为特定区域的筛查和早期 诊断 策略提供信息,以防止病毒性肝炎感染的进一步传播。HBV 和 HCV 感染的 发病率 不断增加,需要针对特定​​区域进行筛查,以评估疾病负担、危险因素并计划控制措施。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。证据仅是抽象的,并对研究结果提供了适度的评估。

Seroprevalence and associated risk factors of Hepatitis B and C in a cross sectional study from North Kashmir

Authors: Shakil U. Rehman, Neesa Majeed, Tanveer Ahmad Mir, Nazima Nazir, Showkat Ahmad Lone, Junaid Ahmad
Original abstract (English)
India has a high burden of endemic HBV and HCV infections, with national seroprevalence of 0.95% for HBV and 0.32% for HCV. In Jammu and Kashmir, the prevalence of HBV and HCV is believed to be elevated much higher than the national average. The ever-increasing incidence of both HBV and HCV infections demand region-specific screening to assess the burden of disease, risk factors and plan control measures. A cross- sectional study was conducted at a tertiary-care hospital in North Kashmir, India, from September 2023-November 2024.Enzyme linked immunosorbent assay(ELISA) method was used for screening Hepatitis B surface antigen and antibodies against HCV. The risk factors associated with HBV and HCV infections were analyzed statistically by using logistic regression model. The model was evaluated using AUC-ROC and F1 score. A total of 1081 and 1087 samples were analyzed for HBsAg and anti-HCV, which showed prevalence of 3.42% and 18.30%, respectively. Among the identified risk factors, intra venous drug usage (aOR = 18.4, P =0.000) represents the most common means of HCV transmission. Additionally, other risk factors like fever, patient type, age group of 20–40 years, and planned surgery showed statistically significant associations with HCV infection. The study highlights the alarming burden of HBV and HCV infection in North Kashmir compared to the national prevalence. The high prevalence of HCV infection and HBV/HCV co-infection particularly among the young population, calls for urgent, region specific screening and early diagnosis to prevent further spread of viral hepatitis infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
India has a high burden of endemic HBV and HCV infections, with national seroprevalence of 0.95% for HBV and 0.32% for HCV. In Jammu and Kashmir, the prevalence of HBV and HCV is believed to be elevated much higher than the national average.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted at a tertiary-care hospital in North Kashmir, India, from September 2023-November 2024. The study analyzed 1081 and 1087 samples for HBsAg and anti-HCV, respectively.
Core methods
The risk factors associated with HBV and HCV infections were analyzed statistically by using logistic regression model. The model was evaluated using AUC-ROC and F1 score. Enzyme linked immunosorbent assay(ELISA) method was used for screening Hepatitis B surface antigen and antibodies against HCV.
Main results
A total of 1081 and 1087 samples were analyzed for HBsAg and anti-HCV, which showed prevalence of 3.42% and 18.30%, respectively. Among the identified risk factors, intra venous drug usage (aOR = 18.4, P =0.000) represents the most common means of HCV transmission. Additionally, other risk factors like fever, patient type, age group of 20–40 years, and planned surgery showed statistically significant associations with HCV infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the alarming burden of HBV and HCV infection in North Kashmir compared to the national prevalence. The high prevalence of HCV infection and HBV/HCV co-infection particularly among the young population, calls for urgent, region specific screening and early diagnosis to prevent further spread of viral hepatitis infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform region-specific screening and early diagnosis strategies to prevent further spread of viral hepatitis infection. The ever-increasing incidence of both HBV and HCV infections demand region-specific screening to assess the burden of disease, risk factors and plan control measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study. The evidence is abstract-only and provides a moderate assessment of the findings.

鞑靼斯坦共和国医务人员突发事件流行病学特征及献血者及其成分乙型肝炎病毒感染标志物检测

Epidemiological characteristics of emergency situations among medical workers and the detection of markers of HBV‐infection in donors of blood and its components in the Republic of Tatarstan
作者： Zh. G. Eremeeva, M. A. Patyashina, G. N. Khasanova, N. V. Ilyina, I. K. Minullin, Ж. Г. Еремеева, Патяшина Марина Александровна, Г. Н. Хасанова, И. К. Минуллин
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究的目的是从流行病学角度描述医疗紧急情况,并分析区域一级血液和血液成分捐献者的乙型肝炎标志物检测情况。
设计与对象
使用 Excel 进行数据处理的描述性流行病学研究,2016 年。
核心方法
分析了鞑靼斯坦共和国 2015 年至 2024 年医护人员紧急情况的频率、结构和原因,以及血液及其成分献血者 HBsAg 和/或抗 HBc 总量检测频率的信息。
主要结果
From 2015 to 2022, a downward trend in the incidence of chronic HBV infection was observed (9.2 and 4.1 per 100,000 population, respectively), while in 2023–2024, an upward trend was observed (7.1 and 10.8, respectively). Positive HBsAg is detected in the serum of 2–12 blood and blood component donors annually, representing 0.1–0.4 cases per 1,000 donors; anti-HBc total is detected in 64–606 donors, representing 2.3–26.2 cases per 1,000 donors. Of all registered emergency situations, 75–90 % were medical, with nursing staff accounting for the majority of casualties (57.1 %), and needlestick injuries (74.4 %) being the primary cause.
解释与创新
血液和血液成分捐献者中持续存在的 HBV 感染标记物 患病率 以及医疗机构中持续存在的工作场所紧急情况凸显了进一步研究 诊断、治疗 和乙型肝炎预防的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要在医疗机构中继续采取 监测 乙型肝炎病毒感染趋势和工作场所安全措施,以防止 传播 和职业暴露。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个地区(鞑靼斯坦共和国)的描述性流行病学数据,可能无法推广到其他人群。由于区域范围和依赖描述性统计而不进行多变量分析,证据强度中等。

Epidemiological characteristics of emergency situations among medical workers and the detection of markers of HBV‐infection in donors of blood and its components in the Republic of Tatarstan

Authors: Zh. G. Eremeeva, M. A. Patyashina, G. N. Khasanova, N. V. Ilyina, I. K. Minullin, Ж. Г. Еремеева, Патяшина Марина Александровна, Г. Н. Хасанова, И. К. Минуллин
Original abstract (English)
The aim of this study was to epidemiologically characterize medical emergencies and analyze the detection of hepatitis B markers in blood and blood component donors at the regional level. Materials and methods. Descriptive epidemiological study with data processing in Excel, 2016. The frequency, structure, and causes of emergency situations among healthcare workers, as well as information on the frequency of detection of HBsAg and/or anti-HBc totals in donors of blood and its components for 2015–2024 in the Republic of Tatarstan, were analyzed. From 2015 to 2022, a downward trend in the incidence of chronic HBV infection was observed (9.2 and 4.1 per 100,000 population, respectively), while in 2023–2024, an upward trend was observed (7.1 and 10.8, respectively). Positive HBsAg is detected in the serum of 2–12 bloodand blood component donors annually, representing 0.1–0.4 cases per 1,000 donors; anti-HBc total is detected in 64–606 donors, representing 2.3–26.2 cases per 1,000 donors. Of all registered emergency situations, 75–90 % were medical, with nursing staff accounting for the majority of casualties (57.1 %), and needlestick injuries (74.4 %) being the primary cause. The persistent prevalence of HBV infection markers in blood and blood component donors and ongoing workplace emergencies in healthcare organizations highlight the importance of further research into the diagnosis, treatment, and prevention of hepatitis B.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The aim of this study was to epidemiologically characterize medical emergencies and analyze the detection of hepatitis B markers in blood and blood component donors at the regional level.
Design and population
Descriptive epidemiological study with data processing in Excel, 2016.
Core methods
The frequency, structure, and causes of emergency situations among healthcare workers, as well as information on the frequency of detection of HBsAg and/or anti-HBc totals in donors of blood and its components for 2015–2024 in the Republic of Tatarstan, were analyzed.
Main results
From 2015 to 2022, a downward trend in the incidence of chronic HBV infection was observed (9.2 and 4.1 per 100,000 population, respectively), while in 2023–2024, an upward trend was observed (7.1 and 10.8, respectively). Positive HBsAg is detected in the serum of 2–12 blood and blood component donors annually, representing 0.1–0.4 cases per 1,000 donors; anti-HBc total is detected in 64–606 donors, representing 2.3–26.2 cases per 1,000 donors. Of all registered emergency situations, 75–90 % were medical, with nursing staff accounting for the majority of casualties (57.1 %), and needlestick injuries (74.4 %) being the primary cause.
Interpretation and novelty
The persistent prevalence of HBV infection markers in blood and blood component donors and ongoing workplace emergencies in healthcare organizations highlight the importance of further research into the diagnosis, treatment, and prevention of hepatitis B.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for continued surveillance of HBV infection trends and workplace safety measures in healthcare settings to prevent transmission and occupational exposure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to descriptive epidemiological data from a single region (Republic of Tatarstan) and may not be generalizable to other populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the regional scope and reliance on descriptive statistics without multivariate analysis.

替诺福韦艾拉酚胺对 30 岁以上、ALT 正常且可检测到 HBV DNA 的 HBeAg 阳性和阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的疗效和安全性:一项为期 96 周的回顾性两中心研究

Efficacy and safety of tenofovir alafenamide in HBeAg-positive and negative chronic hepatitis B patients aged over 30 years with normal ALT and detectable HBV DNA: a 96-week retrospective two-center study
作者： Yanfang Li, Jing Sun, Xinxin Liu, Haifeng Yu, Youde Liu, Ning Sun
摘要中文翻译背景 2022 年更新的中国 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 管理指南提倡对年龄 > 30 岁的 HBV DNA 阳性患者启动 抗病毒药物 治疗,无论丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 状态如何。本研究旨在通过短期(48 周)和长期(96 周)观察来调查替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 在这一特定人群中的病毒学反应和肾脏安全性。方法 在这项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究中,来自两家三级医院(烟台旗山医院和烟台毓璜顶医院)的 122 名符合纳入标准的 治疗 初治 CHB 患者接受了标准 TAF 单药治疗(25 mg/天)。在基线、第 48 周和第 96 周对病毒学参数(HBV DNA 定量)和肾功能标志物(血清肌酐、估计肾小球滤过率)进行系列评估。结果 TAF 治疗 48 周后,122 名入组患者中有 74.6% 实现了 HBV DNA 转化(HBV DNA 30 年,ALT 正常,活动性病毒血症,整个治疗期间肾脏耐受性良好) 96 周 治疗 期的延长随访正在进行中,以确定长期临床结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 对 30 岁以上、ALT 正常且可检测到 HBV DNA 的 HBeAg 阳性和阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的疗效和安全性,因为 2022 年更新的中国 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 管理指南提倡无论 HBV DNA 阳性患者的 ALT 状态如何,都开始 慢性乙型肝炎 治疗。
设计与对象
这是一项在两家三级医院进行的为期 96 周的回顾性两中心 队列研究 研究,涉及 122 名未接受过 治疗 治疗的患者,年龄超过 30 岁,ALT 正常且可检测到 HBV DNA。
核心方法
该研究涉及标准 TAF 单药治疗(25 毫克/天)以及基线、第 48 周和第 96 周的病毒学参数(HBV DNA 定量)和肾功能标志物(血清肌酐、估计肾小球滤过率)的系列评估。
主要结果
TAF 治疗 治疗 48 周后,122 名入组患者中有 74.6% 实现了 HBV DNA 转化,96 周后,78.7% 实现了 HBV DNA 转化。所有患者在整个 治疗 期间均具有良好的肾脏耐受性。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为证明了 TAF 在实现 HBV DNA 转化方面的功效及其在这一特定 慢性乙型肝炎 患者群体中良好的肾脏安全性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果为 30 岁以上、ALT 正常且可检测到 HBV DNA 的患者的 慢性乙型肝炎 治疗提供了重要信息,支持使用 TAF 作为安全有效的 治疗 选项。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,可能会引入偏倚并限制证据的强度。然而,结果是基于对明确患者群体的病毒学和肾脏结果的直接观察。

Efficacy and safety of tenofovir alafenamide in HBeAg-positive and negative chronic hepatitis B patients aged over 30 years with normal ALT and detectable HBV DNA: a 96-week retrospective two-center study

Authors: Yanfang Li, Jing Sun, Xinxin Liu, Haifeng Yu, Youde Liu, Ning Sun
Original abstract (English)
Background The 2022 update of Chinese Guidelines for Chronic Hepatitis B (CHB) Management advocates initiating antiviral therapy regardless of alanine aminotransferase (ALT) status in HBV DNA-positive patients aged > 30 years. This study aimed to investigate the virological response and renal safety profile of tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) in this specific population through both short-term (48-week) and extended (96-week) observations. Methods In this multicenter retrospective cohort study, 122 treatment-naïve CHB patients meeting inclusion criteria from two tertiary hospitals (Yantai Qishan Hospital and Yantai Yuhuangding Hospital) underwent standard TAF monotherapy (25 mg/day). Serial assessments of virological parameters (HBV DNA quantification) and renal function markers (serum creatinine, estimated glomerular filtration rate) were conducted at baseline, week 48, and week 96. Results After 48 weeks of TAF treatment, 74.6% of the 122 enrolled patients achieved HBV DNA conversion (HBV DNA 30 years with normal ALT and active viremia, accompanied by favorable renal tolerability throughout the 96-week treatment period. Extended follow-up is ongoing to establish long-term clinical outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to evaluate the efficacy and safety of tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) in HBeAg-positive and negative chronic hepatitis B patients aged over 30 years with normal ALT and detectable HBV DNA, as the 2022 update of Chinese Guidelines for Chronic Hepatitis B (CHB) Management advocates initiating antiviral therapy regardless of ALT status in HBV DNA-positive patients.
Design and population
This was a 96-week retrospective two-center cohort study conducted in two tertiary hospitals, involving 122 treatment-naïve chronic hepatitis B patients aged over 30 years with normal ALT and detectable HBV DNA.
Core methods
The study involved standard TAF monotherapy (25 mg/day) and serial assessments of virological parameters (HBV DNA quantification) and renal function markers (serum creatinine, estimated glomerular filtration rate) at baseline, week 48, and week 96.
Main results
After 48 weeks of TAF treatment, 74.6% of the 122 enrolled patients achieved HBV DNA conversion, and after 96 weeks, 78.7% achieved HBV DNA conversion. All patients had favorable renal tolerability throughout the treatment period.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these results as demonstrating the efficacy of TAF in achieving HBV DNA conversion and its favorable renal safety profile in this specific population of patients with chronic hepatitis B.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide important information for the management of chronic hepatitis B in patients aged over 30 years with normal ALT and detectable HBV DNA, supporting the use of TAF as a safe and effective treatment option.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective cohort study, which may introduce bias and limit the strength of the evidence. However, the results are based on direct observations of virological and renal outcomes in a well-defined patient population.

新型 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 细胞因子调节 乙型肝炎病毒 X 蛋白 (HBx) 的动态核和细胞质定位

Novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors regulate dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of Hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx)
作者： Yasuo Ariumi
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒转录、复制和 HBV 相关肝癌发生中发挥关键作用。鉴定了几种新型 HBx 相互作用 宿主 细胞因子:DDX1、DDX3、MOV10 RNA 解旋酶、INI1/hSNF5 染色质重塑因子、Rad18 DNA 修复酶和 CPSF6 裂解因子。DDX1、DDX3 和 MOV10 与 HBx 共定位于细胞质灶或聚集体中。相反,INI1/hSNF5 和 CPSF6 与 HBx 一起重新定位于核斑点、染色质间颗粒簇 (IGC) 和/或 PML 核体中。Rad18 将 HBx 隔离在核仁中。重要的是,DDX3、MOV10 和 INI1/hSNF5 限制 HBV 复制,而 CPSF6 促进 HBV 复制。此外,DDX1 增强 HBx 介导的 NF-κB 转录,而 MOV10 和 Rad18 抑制它。总之,HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子决定 HBx 的动态核和细胞质定位,并调节 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在鉴定与 HBV X 蛋白 (HBx) 相互作用的 宿主 细胞因子,并了解它们在调节 HBx 定位和病毒复制中的作用。乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒转录、复制和 HBV 相关肝癌发生中发挥关键作用。
设计与对象
该研究是在实验室环境中使用人类乙型肝炎病毒感染模型进行的。它涉及通过生化和细胞测定来鉴定与 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子。
核心方法
研究人员使用生化和细胞测定来识别 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子,包括细胞质灶或聚集体和核斑点、染色质间颗粒簇 (IGC) 和/或 PML 核体的共定位研究。他们还评估了这些因素对 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录的影响。
主要结果
DDX1、DDX3 和 MOV10 与 HBx 共定位于细胞质灶或聚集体中。INI1/hSNF5 和 CPSF6 与 HBx 一起重新定位于核斑点、IGC 和/或 PML 核体中。Rad18 将 HBx 隔离在核仁中。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子决定 HBx 的动态核和细胞质定位,并调节 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录。这项研究确定了几种与 HBx 相互作用并对病毒过程具有独特影响的新因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 HBV 复制和 发病机制 的分子机制的见解,这可以为靶向治疗的开发提供信息并提高对 HBV 相关肝癌发生的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,不提供详细的统计分析或实验对照。由于缺乏有关这些因素体内功能影响的全面数据,证据强度中等。

Novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors regulate dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of Hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx)

Authors: Yasuo Ariumi
Original abstract (English)
The hepatitis B virus (HBV) X protein (HBx) is a key player in viral transcription, replication, and HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis. Several novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors: DDX1, DDX3, MOV10 RNA helicases, INI1/hSNF5 chromatin-remodeling factor, Rad18 DNA repair enzyme, and CPSF6 cleavage factor were identified. DDX1, DDX3, and MOV10 colocalized with HBx in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates. In contrast, INI1/hSNF5 and CPSF6 relocalized with HBx in nuclear speckles, interchromatin granule clusters (IGCs), and/or PML-nuclear bodies. Rad18 sequestered HBx in the nucleoli. Importantly, DDX3, MOV10, and INI1/hSNF5 restricted HBV replication, whereas CPSF6 facilitated HBV replication. Furthermore, DDX1 enhanced HBx-mediated NF-κB transcription, whereas MOV10 and Rad18 inhibited it. Altogether, HBx-interacting host factors determine dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of HBx and regulate HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify host cellular factors that interact with HBV X protein (HBx) and understand their role in regulating HBx localization and viral replication. The hepatitis B virus (HBV) X protein (HBx) is a key player in viral transcription, replication, and HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a laboratory setting using human HBV infection models. It involved the identification of host factors interacting with HBx through biochemical and cellular assays.
Core methods
The researchers used biochemical and cellular assays to identify HBx-interacting host factors, including colocalization studies in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates and nuclear speckles, interchromatin granule clusters (IGCs), and/or PML-nuclear bodies. They also assessed the impact of these factors on HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription.
Main results
DDX1, DDX3, and MOV10 colocalized with HBx in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates. INI1/hSNF5 and CPSF6 relocalized with HBx in nuclear speckles, IGCs, and/or PML-nuclear bodies. Rad18 sequestered HBx in the nucleoli.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HBx-interacting host factors determine the dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of HBx and regulate HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription. This study identified several novel factors that interact with HBx and have distinct effects on viral processes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of HBV replication and pathogenesis, which could inform the development of targeted therapies and improve understanding of HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence and does not provide detailed statistical analyses or experimental controls. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of comprehensive data on the functional impact of these factors in vivo.

乙型肝炎 血清学 的两项诊断挑战:低 HBsAg S/CO 值和孤立的抗 HBc 阳性

Two Diagnostic Challenges in Hepatitis B Serology: Low HBsAg S/CO Values and Isolated Anti-HBc Positivity
作者： Şerife Yılmaz Gürbüz, Oğuzhan Yağdı, Erhan Başar
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 信号截止值 (S/CO) 和孤立的抗 HBc 阳性代表了 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 血清学 中的两个重大诊断挑战。这项回顾性研究于 2021 年至 2025 年间在卡拉比克进行,旨在评估这些特征与 HBV DNA 阳性之间的相关性,以确定验证性检测的最佳 S/CO 阈值,并阐明分离的抗 HBc 反应性的临床相关性。在 17,356 名进行抗 HBc 筛查的患者中,有 506 名患者 (2.9%) 被鉴定为孤立的抗 HBc 阳性,其中 3.2% 可检测到 HBV DNA。50 岁以上患者和胃肠病科患者的阳性率显着较高。另外,对 213 份 HBsAg S/CO 值在 1.0 至 10.0 之间的样本进行了 HBV DNA 确认检测。ROC 分析得出出色的曲线下面积 0.937,S/CO 阈值≥3.04,实现 HBV DNA 检测的 100% 灵敏度。所有 S/CO 值在 1.0 至 3.0 之间的样本均为 HBV DNA 阴性。在本研究人群中,S/CO 值低于 3.04 的样本中未检测到血清 HBV DNA 阳性,这表明 HBV DNA 检测可能有助于避免在此范围内不必要的确认性检查。老年患者应优先考虑抗 HBc 筛查。使用 HBV DNA 检测的基于 S/CO 的验证算法可以提高诊断准确性,并减少常规乙型肝炎筛查项目中不必要的临床干预。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 信号截止值 (S/CO) 和孤立的抗 HBc 阳性代表了 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 血清学 中的两个重大诊断挑战。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究于 2021 年至 2025 年间在卡拉比克进行,评估了 17,356 名接受抗 HBc 筛查的患者和 213 份 HBsAg S/CO 值在 1.0 至 10.0 之间的样本。
核心方法
该研究利用 HBV DNA 确认测试和 ROC 分析来确定确认测试的最佳 S/CO 阈值,并阐明分离的抗 HBc 反应性的临床相关性。
主要结果
在 506 名患者 (2.9%) 中发现了孤立的抗 HBc 阳性,其中 3.2% 可检测到 HBV DNA,其中 50 岁以上患者的阳性率明显更高。ROC 分析得出曲线下面积为 0.937,S/CO 阈值≥3.04,灵敏度达到 100%,而所有 S/CO 值在 1.0 和 3.0 之间的样本均为 HBV DNA 阴性。
解释与创新
作者建议,HBV DNA 检测可能有助于避免对 S/CO 值低于 3.04 的样本进行不必要的确认性检查,并且应优先对老年患者进行抗 HBc 筛查。
科研与公卫意义
使用 HBV DNA 检测的基于 S/CO 的验证算法可以提高诊断准确性并减少常规乙型肝炎筛查项目中不必要的临床干预。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告具体的设计局限性,但由于回顾性设计和对仅摘要数据的依赖,证据强度被评估为中等。

Two Diagnostic Challenges in Hepatitis B Serology: Low HBsAg S/CO Values and Isolated Anti-HBc Positivity

Authors: Şerife Yılmaz Gürbüz, Oğuzhan Yağdı, Erhan Başar
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) signal-to-cutoff (S/CO) values and isolated anti-HBc positivity represent two significant diagnostic challenges in hepatitis B virus (HBV) serology. This retrospective study, conducted in Karabük between 2021 and 2025, aimed to evaluate the correlation between these profiles and HBV DNA positivity to determine the optimal S/CO thresholds for confirmatory testing and clarify the clinical relevance of isolated anti-HBc reactivity. Among 17,356 patients screened for anti-HBc, isolated anti-HBc positivity was identified in 506 patients (2.9%), of whom 3.2% had detectable HBV DNA. Positivity was significantly higher in patients older than 50 years and in the Gastroenterology department. Separately, 213 specimens with HBsAg S/CO values between 1.0 and 10.0 underwent HBV DNA confirmation testing. ROC analysis yielded an excellent area under the curve of 0.937, with an S/CO threshold of ≥3.04, achieving 100% sensitivity for HBV DNA detection. All specimens with S/CO values between 1.0 and 3.0 were HBV DNA-negative. In this study population, no serum HBV DNA positivity was detected among samples with S/CO values below 3.04, suggesting that HBV DNA testing may help avoid unnecessary confirmatory work-up in this range. Anti-HBc screening should be prioritized in older patients. S/CO-based confirmatory algorithm using HBV DNA testing may improve diagnostic accuracy and reduce unnecessary clinical interventions in routine hepatitis B screening programs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) signal-to-cutoff (S/CO) values and isolated anti-HBc positivity represent two significant diagnostic challenges in hepatitis B virus (HBV) serology.
Design and population
This retrospective study, conducted in Karabük between 2021 and 2025, evaluated 17,356 patients screened for anti-HBc and 213 specimens with HBsAg S/CO values between 1.0 and 10.0.
Core methods
The study utilized HBV DNA confirmation testing and ROC analysis to determine the optimal S/CO thresholds for confirmatory testing and clarify the clinical relevance of isolated anti-HBc reactivity.
Main results
Isolated anti-HBc positivity was identified in 506 patients (2.9%), of whom 3.2% had detectable HBV DNA, with positivity significantly higher in patients older than 50 years. ROC analysis yielded an area under the curve of 0.937 with an S/CO threshold of ≥3.04 achieving 100% sensitivity, while all specimens with S/CO values between 1.0 and 3.0 were HBV DNA-negative.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that HBV DNA testing may help avoid unnecessary confirmatory work-up for samples with S/CO values below 3.04 and that anti-HBc screening should be prioritized in older patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
An S/CO-based confirmatory algorithm using HBV DNA testing may improve diagnostic accuracy and reduce unnecessary clinical interventions in routine hepatitis B screening programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, but the evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the retrospective design and reliance on abstract-only data.

细胞外载脂蛋白E促进乙型肝炎病毒感染

Promotion of hepatitis B virus infection by extracellular apolipoprotein E
作者： Emad Elgendy, Sachin Kumar Tripathi, Guangxiang Luo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major burden to global health, affecting more than 250 million people worldwide. While HBV-associated apolipoprotein E (apoE) and its expression in hepatocytes are known to be critical for efficient HBV infection, the importance of extracellular apoE in HBV infection has not been examined.
设计与对象
本研究是使用 HepG2 细胞和 HepG2 NTCP 细胞进行的体外研究,以确定细胞外 apoE 在 HBV 感染中的作用。该研究利用了apoE无效的HBV、来自apoE3过表达的HepG2细胞的细胞培养物上清液、来自人apoE3敲入小鼠的血清以及在大肠杆菌中表达的纯化重组apoE。
核心方法
该研究采用免疫共沉淀实验来评估 apoE 向 HBV 病毒粒子的转移。通过在感染前将细胞外 apoE3 脂蛋白与 apoE 缺失的 HBV 混合来进行感染测定,并测量 HBV 对细胞的附着以评估作用机制。
主要结果
当与 apoE 缺失的 HBV 混合时,ApoE 可有效转移至 HBV 病毒体。细胞外apoE3脂蛋白以剂量依赖性方式显着促进HBV感染,并且用含有apoE3的小鼠血清、过表达apoE3的HepG2细胞的上清液和脂化重组apoE3观察到这种增强作用。此外,细胞外 apoE3 脂蛋白剂量依赖性地增强 HBV 对 HepG2 NTCP 细胞的附着。
解释与创新
作者对这些发现进行了解释,证明细胞外 apoE3 通过可交换转移至 HBV 包膜并介导 HBV 细胞附着,在 HBV 感染中发挥关键作用。该研究揭示了细胞外 apoE3 在促进 HBV 感染中的先前未被认识的作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,慢性乙型肝炎患者血浆中不同密度的apoE3脂蛋白可能在HBV感染和体内发病机制中发挥关键作用。这凸显了细胞外 apoE 作为影响病毒进入和疾病进展的因素的潜力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性或超出体外背景的证据强度。证据来自摘要,这限制了对潜在偏见或混杂因素的评估。

Promotion of hepatitis B virus infection by extracellular apolipoprotein E

Authors: Emad Elgendy, Sachin Kumar Tripathi, Guangxiang Luo
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major burden to global health, affecting more than 250 million people worldwide. HBV and host interactions determine the HBV chronicity. Our previous studies have found that human apolipoprotein E (apoE) is incorporated in infectious HBV virions and plays important roles in HBV infection and morphogenesis. ApoE mediates HBV cell attachment through its interaction with cell surface receptors such as the low-density lipoprotein receptor (LDLR) family members and heparan sulfate proteoglycans (HSPGs). More importantly, both HBV-associated apoE and its expression in hepatocytes are critical for efficient HBV infection. However, the importance of extracellular apoE in HBV infection has not been examined. In the present study, we have determined the role of extracellular apoE in HBV infection in vitro . ApoE is an exchangeable apolipoprotein and was found to be efficiently transferred to HBV virions when mixed with an apoE-null HBV, as suggested by the results obtained from coimmunoprecipitation experiments. More significantly, extracellular apoE3 lipoproteins significantly promoted HBV infection in a dose-dependent manner when mixed with apoE-null HBV prior to infection. The cell culture supernatants obtained from apoE3-overexpressing HepG2 cells, sera derived from human apoE3-knockin mice, and a purified recombinant apoE expressed in E. coli were all able to significantly promote HBV infection. Additionally, extracellular apoE3 lipoproteins could dose-dependently enhance HBV attachment to HepG2 NTCP cells. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that extracellular apoE3 plays a key role in HBV infection by exchangeable transfer to the HBV envelope and mediation of HBV cell attachment. IMPORTANCE Human apolipoprotein E (apoE) is implicated in the infection of different viruses as an entry-promoting factor. Our previous studies have demonstrated that apoE is enriched in the envelopes of hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV)and promotes their infection and morphogenesis in vitro . In the present study, we have uncovered a previously unrecognized role of extracellular apoE3 in the promotion of HBV infection. Extracellular apoE3 was efficiently transferred to the HBV envelope when incubated with an apoE-deficient HBV. Strikingly, apoE-null HBV infection was proportionally enhanced by the increasing amounts of apoE3-containing mouse sera, as well as the supernatants of apoE3-overexpressing HepG2 cells. Likewise, a lipidated recombinant apoE3 significantly promoted HBV infection in a dose-dependent manner. Additionally, extracellular apoE3 promoted regular HBV infection. Moreover, extracellular apoE3 enhanced HBV cell attachment. These findings suggest that apoE3 lipoproteins with various densities in the plasma of chronic hepatitis B patients may play a key role in HBV infection and pathogenesis in vivo .
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major burden to global health, affecting more than 250 million people worldwide. While HBV-associated apolipoprotein E (apoE) and its expression in hepatocytes are known to be critical for efficient HBV infection, the importance of extracellular apoE in HBV infection has not been examined.
Design and population
The present study was an in vitro investigation using HepG2 cells and HepG2 NTCP cells to determine the role of extracellular apoE in HBV infection. The study utilized apoE-null HBV, cell culture supernatants from apoE3-overexpressing HepG2 cells, sera from human apoE3-knockin mice, and purified recombinant apoE expressed in E.
Core methods
The study employed coimmunoprecipitation experiments to assess the transfer of apoE to HBV virions. Infection assays were conducted by mixing extracellular apoE3 lipoproteins with apoE-null HBV prior to infection, and HBV attachment to cells was measured to evaluate the mechanism of action.
Main results
ApoE was efficiently transferred to HBV virions when mixed with an apoE-null HBV. Extracellular apoE3 lipoproteins significantly promoted HBV infection in a dose-dependent manner, and this enhancement was observed with apoE3-containing mouse sera, supernatants of apoE3-overexpressing HepG2 cells, and lipidated recombinant apoE3. Additionally, extracellular apoE3 lipoproteins dose-dependently enhanced HBV attachment to HepG2 NTCP cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings to demonstrate that extracellular apoE3 plays a key role in HBV infection by exchangeable transfer to the HBV envelope and mediation of HBV cell attachment. The study uncovers a previously unrecognized role of extracellular apoE3 in the promotion of HBV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that apoE3 lipoproteins with various densities in the plasma of chronic hepatitis B patients may play a key role in HBV infection and pathogenesis in vivo. This highlights the potential of extracellular apoE as a factor influencing viral entry and disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or the strength of the evidence beyond the in vitro context. The evidence is derived from an abstract, which restricts the assessment of potential biases or confounding factors.

患有 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态和妊娠糖尿病的女性早产风险预测模型的开发和验证

Development and validation of a risk prediction model for preterm birth in women concurrent with Hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus
作者： Zaidi Gui, Xiaoxiao Fan, Tianzi Zang, Hongyan Xu, Qi Wu, Lijun Chen, Jinbing Bai, Yanqun Liu
摘要中文翻译早产是一种主要的不良新生儿结局,与母亲 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态相关。与单独的 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态相比,乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态与妊娠糖尿病同时出现与早产风险增加相关。本研究旨在开发并外部验证一种临床适用的多变量预测模型,该模型结合了临床风险因素和母体血液生物标志物,以估计同时患有 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态和妊娠糖尿病的女性的早产风险。使用 2015 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月在中国浙江省妇女医院分娩的 882 名 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者和妊娠糖尿病孕妇的临床数据和母血生物标志物进行回顾性分析。数据预处理后,参与者以 7:3 的比例随机分配到训练组和验证组。使用最小绝对收缩和选择算子(LASSO)进行变量选择,随后进行单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析,以评估临床变量和母血生物标志物与同时患有 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态和妊娠糖尿病的妇女早产的关联。开发了列线图以方便临床应用。分别使用受试者工作特征曲线分析和校准图评估模型辨别和校准,同时通过决策曲线分析评估临床效用。本研究共纳入 847 名患有 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态和妊娠期糖尿病的女性,其中 187 名(22%)经历过早产。多变量逻辑回归分析确定孕前体重指数、舒张压、收缩压、丙氨酸转氨酶、前置胎盘、白细胞计数和母血生物标志物(包括总蛋白、总胆汁酸、总甲状腺素、胎膜破裂模式和凝血酶原时间)作为早产的独立预测因素。这些变量被纳入列线图,显示出良好的校准和区分能力。训练集中的曲线下面积为 0.854 (95% CI: 0.823–0.881),验证集中的曲线下面积为 0.754 (95% CI: 0.697–0.806)。决策曲线分析表明列线图为预测早产提供了良好的临床实用性。该模型表现出良好的判别能力和校准能力,能够准确预测同时患有 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态和妊娠期糖尿病的女性的早产风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
早产是与母亲 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态相关的主要不良新生儿结局,与单独携带者状态相比,这种携带者状态与妊娠糖尿病同时发生会增加早产风险。本研究旨在开发并外部验证一种临床适用的多变量预测模型,该模型结合了临床风险因素和母血生物标志物,以估计同时患有 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态和妊娠糖尿病的女性的早产风险。
设计与对象
使用 2015 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月在中国浙江省妇女医院分娩的 882 名 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者和妊娠糖尿病孕妇的临床数据和母血生物标志物进行回顾性分析。数据预处理后,参与者以 7:3 的比例随机分配到训练组和验证组。该研究共有 847 名女性参与,其中 187 名(22%)经历过早产。
核心方法
使用最小绝对收缩和选择算子(LASSO)进行变量选择,并进行单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析,以评估临床变量和母体血液生物标志物与早产的关联。使用受试者工作特征曲线分析和校准图评估模型辨别和校准,同时通过决策曲线分析评估临床效用。开发了列线图以方便临床应用。
主要结果
多变量逻辑回归分析确定孕前体重指数、舒张压、收缩压、丙氨酸转氨酶、前置胎盘、白细胞计数和母血生物标志物(包括总蛋白、总胆汁酸、总甲状腺素、胎膜破裂模式和凝血酶原时间)作为早产的独立预测因素。训练集中的曲线下面积为 0.854 (95% CI: 0.823–0.881),验证集中的曲线下面积为 0.754 (95% CI: 0.697–0.806)。决策曲线分析表明列线图为预测早产提供了良好的临床实用性。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明该模型具有良好的判别能力和校准能力,能够准确预测同时患有 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态和妊娠期糖尿病的女性的早产风险。该研究提出了一种新颖的列线图,结合了特定的临床因素和血液生物标志物,以解决与这些疾病同时发生相关的风险增加问题。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果可以为临床 监测 和风险评估提供信息,提供一种工具来估计同时患有 乙型肝炎病毒 携带者状态和妊娠期糖尿病的孕妇的早产概率。该列线图可以帮助临床医生识别高危患者,以进行有针对性的监测和潜在的干预策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制、潜在偏差或超出分析回顾性的混杂因素。由于外部验证和报告的绩效指标,证据强度被评估为中等,尽管对单个中心数据的依赖可能会影响普遍性。

Development and validation of a risk prediction model for preterm birth in women concurrent with Hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus

Authors: Zaidi Gui, Xiaoxiao Fan, Tianzi Zang, Hongyan Xu, Qi Wu, Lijun Chen, Jinbing Bai, Yanqun Liu
Original abstract (English)
Preterm birth is a major adverse neonatal outcome that has been associated with maternal hepatitis B virus carrier status. Co-occurrence of hepatitis B virus carrier status carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus is associated with an increased risk of preterm birth compared with hepatitis B virus carrier status alone. This study aimed to develop and externally validate a clinically applicable multivariable prediction model incorporating both clinical risk factors and maternal blood biomarkers to estimate preterm birth risk among women with concurrent hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus. A retrospective analysis was performed using clinical data and maternal blood biomarkers from 882 pregnant women with hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus who delivered at Women’s Hospital, in Zhejiang Province, China, from January 2015 to December 2024. Following data preprocessing, participants were randomly allocated to training and validation sets at a 7:3 ratio. Variable selection was performed using the least absolute shrinkage and selection operator (LASSO), and univariate and multivariable logistic regression analyses were subsequently conducted to assess the associations of clinical variables and maternal blood biomarkers with preterm birth in women with concurrent hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus. A nomogram was developed to facilitate clinical application. Model discrimination and calibration were evaluated using receiver operating characteristic curve analysis and calibration plots, respectively, while clinical utility was assessed through decision curve analysis. A total of 847 women with hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus were included in this study, of whom 187 (22%) experienced preterm birth. Multivariable logistic regression analysis identified pre-pregnancy body mass index, diastolic blood pressure, systolic blood pressure, alanine aminotransferase, placenta previa, white blood cell count, and maternal blood biomarkers—including total protein, total bile acid, total thyroxine, mode of membrane rupture, and prothrombin time—as independent predictors of preterm birth. These variables were incorporated into a nomogram, which demonstrated good calibration and discrimination. The area under the curve was 0.854 (95% CI: 0.823–0.881) in the training set and 0.754 (95% CI: 0.697–0.806) in the validation set. Decision curve analysis indicated that the nomogram provided favorable clinical utility for predicting preterm birth. The model demonstrated good discriminative ability and calibration, enabling accurate prediction of preterm birth risk in women with concurrent hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Preterm birth is a major adverse neonatal outcome associated with maternal hepatitis B virus carrier status, and the co-occurrence of this carrier status with gestational diabetes mellitus increases preterm birth risk compared with carrier status alone. This study aimed to develop and externally validate a clinically applicable multivariable prediction model incorporating clinical risk factors and maternal blood biomarkers to estimate preterm birth risk in women with concurrent hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was performed using clinical data and maternal blood biomarkers from 882 pregnant women with hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus who delivered at Women’s Hospital in Zhejiang Province, China, from January 2015 to December 2024. Following data preprocessing, participants were randomly allocated to training and validation sets at a 7:3 ratio. A total of 847 women were included in the study, of whom 187 (22%) experienced preterm birth.
Core methods
Variable selection was performed using the least absolute shrinkage and selection operator (LASSO), and univariate and multivariable logistic regression analyses were conducted to assess associations of clinical variables and maternal blood biomarkers with preterm birth. Model discrimination and calibration were evaluated using receiver operating characteristic curve analysis and calibration plots, while clinical utility was assessed through decision curve analysis. A nomogram was developed to facilitate clinical application.
Main results
Multivariable logistic regression analysis identified pre-pregnancy body mass index, diastolic blood pressure, systolic blood pressure, alanine aminotransferase, placenta previa, white blood cell count, and maternal blood biomarkers—including total protein, total bile acid, total thyroxine, mode of membrane rupture, and prothrombin time—as independent predictors of preterm birth. The area under the curve was 0.854 (95% CI: 0.823–0.881) in the training set and 0.754 (95% CI: 0.697–0.806) in the validation set. Decision curve analysis indicated that the nomogram provided favorable clinical utility for predicting preterm birth.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that the model has good discriminative ability and calibration, enabling accurate prediction of preterm birth risk in women with concurrent hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus. The study presents a novel nomogram incorporating specific clinical factors and blood biomarkers to address the increased risk associated with the co-occurrence of these conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform clinical surveillance and risk assessment by providing a tool to estimate preterm birth probability in pregnant women with both hepatitis B virus carrier status and gestational diabetes mellitus. This nomogram may assist clinicians in identifying high-risk patients for targeted monitoring and potential intervention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, potential biases, or confounding factors beyond the retrospective nature of the analysis. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the external validation and reported performance metrics, though the reliance on data from a single center may affect generalisability.
