聚乙二醇化干扰素对有/无表面抗原清除的慢性乙型肝炎患者T细胞受体指纹的影响

Effects of pegylated interferon on T cell receptor fingerprints in chronic hepatitis B patients with/without surface antigen clearance
作者： Kun-Yan Hao, Lei Tang, Guo-Bing Chen, Wei Guo, Zhan-Hui Wang, Li-Peng Mao, Yue-Cheng Yu
摘要中文翻译背景 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然是一个关键的全球健康挑战,很少有患者能够自发清除乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg),而乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 是 功能性治愈 的关键指标。T细胞在HBsAg清除中发挥重要作用。目的 比较核苷(酸)类似物(NA)添加聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b(PegIFNα-2b)后有或没有 HBsAg 清除的患者之间的 T 细胞受体(TCR)指纹。方法 在这项观察性研究中,从 6 名可比较的 CHB 患者中分离出外周血单核细胞 (PBMC),这些患者在 PegIFNα-2b 联合 NA 治疗 48 周后达到 HBsAg 清除(C 组)或未清除(NC 组)。C 组和 NC 组患者的年龄、性别、基线 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA、HBsAg、乙型肝炎包膜抗原 (HBeAg)、总胆红素、丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 和肝硬化状态相当。所有患者在 PegIFNα-2b 治疗前均已接受至少一年的 NA。通过RNA测序和生物信息分析对两组之间的TCR谱图(指纹)进行筛选和比较。结果 6名无肝硬化男性患者纳入本研究,每组3例。从每个 PBMC 样品中提取 200 ng RNA,用于标准 RNA 测序,以构建互补 DNA 文库。HBsAg、HBeAg、HBV DNA 的基线水平 (0.05)。结果发现,与Group-NC相比,Group-C在α链和β链上都具有显着更高的TCR多样性,包括更独特的克隆型(分别为P = 0.017,P = 0.038),更多的库重叠(分别为P = 0.002,P = 0.002),更广泛的互补决定区3长度分布和更高的Chao1指数(P = 0.016,P =分别为 0.048)。结论 HBsAg 清除的患者具有更加多样化和稳健的 T 细胞反应,这可能与 HBsAg 清除状态相关。这些免疫特征可以作为 CHB 患者中 PegIFNα-2b 治疗 反应的潜在参考指标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 仍然是一个关键的全球健康挑战,很少有患者能够自发清除乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg),而乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 是 功能性治愈 的关键指标。该研究旨在比较在核苷(酸)类似物上添加聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 后有或没有 HBsAg 清除的患者之间的 T 细胞受体指纹。
设计与对象
这项观察性研究招募了六名没有肝硬化的男性患者,经过 48 周的联合治疗后,将其平均分为一组实现 HBsAg 清除,另一组未实现 HBsAg 清除。两组患者的年龄、性别、基线 乙型肝炎病毒 DNA、HBsAg、乙型肝炎包膜抗原、总胆红素、丙氨酸转氨酶和肝硬化状态相当,并且在聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 治疗前均接受过至少一年的核苷(酸)类似物治疗。
核心方法
分离参与者的外周血单核细胞,从每个样本中提取200 ng RNA进行标准RNA测序,构建互补DNA文库。使用 RNA 测序和生物信息分析对两组 T 细胞受体谱进行筛选和比较。
主要结果
与非清除组相比,清除组的α链和β链的T细胞受体多样性均显着更高,包括更多独特的克隆型(分别为P = 0.017,P = 0.038)和更多的库重叠(分别为P = 0.002,P = 0.002)。清除组还表现出更广泛的互补决定区3长度分布和更高的Chao1指数(分别为P = 0.016、P = 0.048)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HBsAg 清除的患者具有更多样化和更强的 T 细胞反应,这可能与 HBsAg 清除状态相关。这一发现凸显了 T 细胞受体多样性在 功能性治愈 中的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些免疫特征可以作为 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 治疗 反应的潜在参考指标。这些发现可以为监测或预测 功能性治愈 的策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
由于六名患者的样本量非常小以及观察性研究设计,证据强度较低。所提供的证据并未报告潜在的混杂因素或研究结果对更广泛人群的普遍性。

Effects of pegylated interferon on T cell receptor fingerprints in chronic hepatitis B patients with/without surface antigen clearance

Authors: Kun-Yan Hao, Lei Tang, Guo-Bing Chen, Wei Guo, Zhan-Hui Wang, Li-Peng Mao, Yue-Cheng Yu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a critical global health challenge, with few patients achieving spontaneous clearance of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), which is a key indicator of functional cure. T cell plays an important role in HBsAg clearance. AIM To compare T cell receptor (TCR) fingerprints between patients with or without HBsAg clearance after pegylated interferon alpha-2b (PegIFNα-2b) added on nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs). METHODS In this observational study, peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) were isolated from six comparable CHB patients who achieved HBsAg clearance (Group-C) or not (Group-NC) after 48 weeks of PegIFNα-2b added to NA therapy. Patients in Group-C and Group-NC had comparable age, gender, baseline hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA, HBsAg, hepatitis B envelop antigen (HBeAg), total bilirubin, alanine aminotransferase (ALT) and cirrhosis status. All patients had received at least one year of NA before PegIFNα-2b therapy. TCR repertoire profiles (fingerprints) were screened and compared by RNA sequencing and bioinformation analysis between the two groups. RESULTS Six male patients without cirrhosis were enrolled in this study, with three patients in each group. 200 ng of RNA from each PBMC sample was extracted for standard RNA sequencing for construction of a complementary DNA library. Baseline levels of HBsAg, HBeAg, HBV DNA ( 0.05). It was found that compared to Group-NC, Group-C had significantly higher TCR diversity in both α and β chain, including more unique clonotypes (P = 0.017, P = 0.038 respectively), more repertoire overlap (P = 0.002, P = 0.002 respectively), a broader complementarity-determining region3 length distribution, and higher Chao1 index (P = 0.016, P = 0.048 respectively). CONCLUSION Patients with HBsAg clearance had a more diverse and robust T cell response, which may correlate with HBsAg clearance status. These immune signatures may serve as potential reference indicators for PegIFNα-2b treatment response in CHB patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B remains a critical global health challenge with few patients achieving spontaneous clearance of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), a key indicator of functional cure. The study aimed to compare T cell receptor fingerprints between patients with or without HBsAg clearance after pegylated interferon alpha-2b added on nucleos(t)ide analogues.
Design and population
This observational study enrolled six male patients without cirrhosis, divided equally into a group achieving HBsAg clearance and a group that did not after 48 weeks of combined therapy. Patients in both groups had comparable age, gender, baseline hepatitis B virus DNA, HBsAg, hepatitis B envelop antigen, total bilirubin, alanine aminotransferase and cirrhosis status, and all had received at least one year of nucleos(t)ide analogues before pegylated interferon alpha-2b therapy.
Core methods
Peripheral blood mononuclear cells were isolated from participants, and 200 ng of RNA from each sample was extracted for standard RNA sequencing to construct a complementary DNA library. T cell receptor repertoire profiles were screened and compared between the two groups using RNA sequencing and bioinformation analysis.
Main results
Compared to the non-clearance group, the clearance group had significantly higher T cell receptor diversity in both alpha and beta chains, including more unique clonotypes (P = 0.017, P = 0.038 respectively) and more repertoire overlap (P = 0.002, P = 0.002 respectively). The clearance group also exhibited a broader complementarity-determining region3 length distribution and higher Chao1 index (P = 0.016, P = 0.048 respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that patients with HBsAg clearance had a more diverse and robust T cell response, which may correlate with HBsAg clearance status. This finding highlights the potential role of T cell receptor diversity in functional cure.
Scientific and public-health significance
These immune signatures may serve as potential reference indicators for pegylated interferon alpha-2b treatment response in chronic hepatitis B patients. The findings could inform strategies for monitoring or predicting functional cure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is low due to the very small sample size of six patients and the observational study design. The supplied evidence does not report on potential confounding factors or the generalisability of the findings to broader populations.

特殊情况下肠道炎症的治疗:结核病、VIH 感染、乙型病毒性肝炎和重症患者

Terapias en enfermedades inflamatorias intestinales en situaciones especiales: tuberculosis, infección por VIH, virus hepatitis B y paciente crítico
作者： Edith Pérez de Arce Oñate
摘要中文翻译简介:肠道炎症 (EII) 是一种特殊的临床情况。免疫抑制的作用是预防机会性感染和潜伏性感染的重新激活,这取决于公约协议中的治疗决定。目的:修订 EII 中特殊情况的证据:结核病 (TBC)、VIH 感染、乙型肝炎 (VHB) 和重症监护。方法:在 PubMed 系统中修改 basada 叙事,以 ECCO、GETECCU、ACG 和 GETAID 为基础,优先考虑最近几年的公共事务。结果:TBC 潜伏期和生物生物学启动前的必要条件,以及抗 TNF 药物已重新激活 20 次。El VIH aumenta el riesgo de desarrollar EII (OR 2,68) y confiere un fenotipo menos agresivo,pero Mayor riesgo de infecciones opportunistas;生物制剂可以保护 VIH 和病毒阴性。VHB 需要 tamizaje 血清学普遍和分布 抗病毒药物 强制 HBsAg 阳性。EII 的Paciente crítico 要求对所需的条件进行多学科评估,支持强化营养并考虑采取补救措施和禁忌症的治疗方法。结论:EII 的具体情况需要个性化决策、主要证据和跨学科基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在回顾由于免疫抑制和合并症带来的临床挑战,在特殊情况下治疗炎症性肠病 (IBD) 的现有证据,包括 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染。
设计与对象
这是一篇基于 PubMed 系统检索和 ECCO、GETECCU、ACG 和 GETAID 指南的叙述性综述,优先考虑过去五年的出版物。
核心方法
该综述涉及对 PubMed 和临床指南的系统检索,以综合特殊情况下 IBD 管理的证据,重点是 HBV 筛查和 抗病毒药物 预防。
主要结果
必须对 HBV 进行普遍血清学筛查,并且 HBsAg 阳性的患者需要进行 抗病毒药物 预防,以防止再激活。
解释与创新
作者强调,在包括 HBV 感染在内的特殊情况下管理 IBD 需要基于最佳现有证据和多学科方法的个体化决策。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了 IBD 患者进行 HBV 筛查和预防以防止病毒再激活和改善预后的重要性,从而为临床实践提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该综述是叙述性的,并且依赖于现有的指南,其中可能不包括最新的或高质量的研究。

Terapias en enfermedades inflamatorias intestinales en situaciones especiales: tuberculosis, infección por VIH, virus hepatitis B y paciente crítico

Authors: Edith Pérez de Arce Oñate
Original abstract (English)
Introducción: La enfermedad inflamatoria intestinal (EII) en situaciones especiales constituye un desafío clínico. La inmunosupresión sostenida predispone a infecciones oportunistas y a la reactivación de infecciones latentes, mientras que determinadas comorbilidades condicionan decisiones terapéuticas que escapan a los protocolos convencionales. Objetivo: Revisar la evidencia disponible sobre el manejo de cuatro situaciones especiales en la EII: tuberculosis (TBC), infección por VIH, hepatitis B (VHB) y paciente crítico. Métodos: Revisión narrativa basada en búsqueda sistemática en PubMed, guías ECCO, GETECCU, ACG y GETAID, priorizando publicaciones de los últimos cinco años. Resultados: El tamizaje de TBC latente es obligatorio antes de iniciar cualquier biológico, con riesgo de reactivación hasta 20 veces mayor con anti-TNF. El VIH aumenta el riesgo de desarrollar EII (OR 2,68) y confiere un fenotipo menos agresivo, pero mayor riesgo de infecciones oportunistas; los biológicos son seguros con VIH con carga viral negativa. El VHB requiere tamizaje serológico universal y profilaxis antiviral obligatoria en pacientes HBsAg positivos. El paciente crítico con EII demanda manejo multidisciplinario con evaluación precoz del riesgo quirúrgico, soporte nutricional intensivo y consideración de terapias de rescate según disponibilidad y contraindicaciones. Conclusiones: El manejo de la EII en situaciones especiales requiere decisiones individualizadas, fundamentadas en la mejor evidencia disponible y en un abordaje multidisciplinario.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to review the evidence available for managing inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) in special situations, including hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, due to the clinical challenges posed by immunosuppression and comorbidities.
Design and population
This was a narrative review based on a systematic search in PubMed and guidelines from ECCO, GETECCU, ACG, and GETAID, prioritizing publications from the last five years.
Core methods
The review involved a systematic search of PubMed and clinical guidelines to synthesize evidence on IBD management in special situations, with a focus on HBV screening and antiviral prophylaxis.
Main results
Universal serological screening for HBV is mandatory, and antiviral prophylaxis is required in patients who are HBsAg positive to prevent reactivation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors emphasize that managing IBD in special situations, including HBV infection, requires individualized decisions based on the best available evidence and a multidisciplinary approach.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings inform clinical practice by highlighting the importance of HBV screening and prophylaxis in IBD patients to prevent viral reactivation and improve outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the review is narrative and relies on existing guidelines, which may not include the most recent or high-quality studies.

COVID-19 大流行是否影响了 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的 HBsAg 血清清除率?

Has the COVID-19 Pandemic Affected HBsAg Seroclearance Rates in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients?
作者： Necati Mumcu, Sinem Taştan, Buket Balcı Sandıkçı, Fatma Cevahir, Mustafa Gökhan Gözel, Mustafa Altındiş, Sinem Tastan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在记录在 COVID-19 大流行期间观察到的乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 血清清除率的增加。
设计与对象
这项研究纳入了 188 名年龄在 18 岁及以上的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者,他们于 2010 年至 2023 年间在土耳其的三家医院进行了随访。
核心方法
该研究通过与 2020 年之前相比,分析了大流行期间出现 HBsAg 血清清除的患者的数据,确定了 HBsAg 血清清除率、血清转化率和血清学反应率。
主要结果
共有 26 名患者 (13.8%) 出现 HBsAg 血清清除,其中 22 名 (84.6%) 发生在 2020 年或之后,4 名 (15.4%) 发生在 2020 年之前。HBsAg 血清清除的年 发病率 率为 2014 年 0.78%,2017 年 0.6%,2020 年 1.15%。2019年、2020年1.71%、2021年2.23%、2022年7.4%、2023年1.2%。
解释与创新
作者将这一发现解释为,与大流行前相比,大流行期间 HBsAg 血清清除率有所增加。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,COVID-19 大流行环境或相关因素对 慢性乙型肝炎 自然史的潜在影响,特别是在 HBsAg 血清清除方面。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告具体的研究设计局限性,但由于数据仅抽象且缺乏详细的统计方法,证据强度被评估为低。

Has the COVID-19 Pandemic Affected HBsAg Seroclearance Rates in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients?

Authors: Necati Mumcu, Sinem Taştan, Buket Balcı Sandıkçı, Fatma Cevahir, Mustafa Gökhan Gözel, Mustafa Altındiş, Sinem Tastan
Original abstract (English)
Objectives:The aim of this study is to document the increase in hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance that we observed during the coronavirus disease-2019 (COVID-19) pandemic.Materials and Methods: Patients with chronic hepatitis B aged 18 years and older who were followed between 2010 and 2023 at three hospitals in Türkiye were included in this study.Rates of HBsAg seroclearance, seroconversion, and serological response among the patients were determined.Data from patients other than those who developed HBsAg seroclearance before the COVID-19 pandemic (before 2020) were analyzed.Results: A total of 188 patients were included in the study.In total, 26 (13.8%) patients developed HBsAg seroclearance, 28 (15%) developed HBsAg seroconversion, and 21 (11.2%)developed a serological response.Of the patients who developed HBsAg seroclearance, 22 (84.6%)occurred in 2020 or later, and 4 (15.4%)occurred before 2020.The annual incidence rates of HBsAg seroclearance were 0.78% in 2014, 0.6% in 2017, 1.15% in 2019, 1.71% in 2020, 2.23% in 2021, 7.4% in 2022, and 1.2% in 2023.95% of the patients were vaccinated with Pfizer-BioNTech and/or Sinovac-CoronaVac vaccines, and 36.4% had a COVID-19 infection. Conclusion:In this study, the annual incidence rate of HBsAg seroclearance in the pre-pandemic period was consistent with the ÖZ
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to document an observed increase in hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
This study included 188 patients with chronic hepatitis B aged 18 years and older who were followed between 2010 and 2023 at three hospitals in Türkiye.
Core methods
The study determined rates of HBsAg seroclearance, seroconversion, and serological response, analyzing data from patients who developed HBsAg seroclearance during the pandemic compared to before 2020.
Main results
A total of 26 (13.8%) patients developed HBsAg seroclearance, with 22 (84.6%) occurring in 2020 or later and 4 (15.4%) before 2020. Annual incidence rates of HBsAg seroclearance were 0.78% in 2014, 0.6% in 2017, 1.15% in 2019, 1.71% in 2020, 2.23% in 2021, 7.4% in 2022, and 1.2% in 2023.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating an increase in HBsAg seroclearance rates during the pandemic period compared to pre-pandemic years.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest a potential impact of the COVID-19 pandemic environment or associated factors on the natural history of chronic hepatitis B, specifically regarding HBsAg seroclearance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific study design limitations, but the evidence strength is assessed as low due to the abstract-only nature of the data and the lack of detailed statistical methodology.

尼日利亚埃基蒂南医疗机构接受产前护理的孕妇的乙型肝炎病毒、丙型肝炎病毒和艾滋病毒感染情况

HBV, HCV and HIV Infections among Pregnant Women Attending Antenatal Care at the Health Facilities in Ekiti South, Nigeria
作者： B. T. Osuolale, I. A. Simon-Oke, O. J. Afolabi, B. O. Oladejo, Osuolale B.T.
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 是可能导致母婴传播的血源性感染,并且仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区产前护理的重要问题。这项横断面研究确定了尼日利亚埃基蒂南参议院区选定医疗机构的孕妇中 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 的 血清流行率。共有 710 名孕妇从 6 个地方政府区域招募:Ekiti South West、Ikere、Ise、Emure、Ekiti East 和 Gbonyin。使用结构化调查问卷收集有关知识、意识和态度的社会人口统计数据和信息。无菌采集静脉血样本并筛查乙型肝炎表面抗原、抗HCV抗体和HIV抗体。使用描述性统计和卡方检验来评估 患病率 和 p < 0.05 的关联。HBV 的总体 血清流行率 为 2.5% (18/710),HIV 为 1.5% (11/710),HCV 为 0%。未发现合并感染。年龄组与 HIV 感染显着相关(p < 0.001),而地点、婚姻状况、居住地、胎龄、教育和职业与 HIV 感染状况没有显着相关。HBsAg 阳性与任何评估的社会人口统计学变量均无显着相关性。研究结果表明,研究人群中乙型肝炎病毒流行程度处于中等水平,并支持持续的产前筛查、与护理、健康教育、乙型肝炎病毒 传播 和预防母婴 传播 服务的联系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 是可能导致母婴传播的血源性感染,并且仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区产前护理的重要问题。本研究旨在确定尼日利亚埃基蒂南孕妇中这些感染的 血清流行率。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究招募了来自尼日利亚埃基蒂南参议院区六个地方政府辖区的 710 名孕妇,以确定 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 的 血清流行率。
核心方法
使用结构化调查问卷收集有关知识、意识和态度的社会人口学数据和信息,并对静脉血样本进行乙型肝炎表面抗原、抗 HCV 抗体和 HIV 抗体筛查。
主要结果
HBV 总体 血清流行率 为 2.5% (18/710),HIV 为 1.5% (11/710),HCV 为 0%,未检测到合并感染。年龄组与 HIV 感染显着相关 (p < 0.001),而其他社会人口统计学变量与 HIV 状态或 HBsAg 阳性没有显着相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,研究人群中乙型肝炎病毒流行程度处于中等水平,支持持续产前筛查和预防母婴 传播 服务的需要。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了持续产前筛查和健康教育对于预防该地区母婴乙型肝炎的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计限制了建立因果关系的能力,并且样本可能无法完全代表该地区的所有孕妇。由于研究设计和样本量,证据强度中等。

HBV, HCV and HIV Infections among Pregnant Women Attending Antenatal Care at the Health Facilities in Ekiti South, Nigeria

Authors: B. T. Osuolale, I. A. Simon-Oke, O. J. Afolabi, B. O. Oladejo, Osuolale B.T.
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) are blood-borne infections with the potential for mother-to-child transmission and remain important concerns in antenatal care in sub-Saharan Africa. This cross-sectional study determined the seroprevalence of HBV, HCV, and HIV among pregnant women attending selected health facilities in Ekiti South Senatorial District, Nigeria. A total of 710 pregnant women were recruited from six Local Government Areas: Ekiti South West, Ikere, Ise, Emure, Ekiti East, and Gbonyin. Sociodemographic data and information on knowledge, awareness, and attitudes were collected using a structured questionnaire. Venous blood specimens were obtained aseptically and screened for hepatitis B surface antigen, anti-HCV antibodies, and HIV antibodies. Descriptive statistics and chi-square tests were used to assess prevalence and associations at p < 0.05. The overall seroprevalence was 2.5% (18/710) for HBV, 1.5% (11/710) for HIV, and 0% for HCV. No co-infection was detected. Age group was significantly associated with HIV infection (p < 0.001), whereas location, marital status, residence, gestational age, education, and occupation were not significantly associated with HIV status. HBsAg positivity was not significantly associated with any assessed sociodemographic variable. The findings indicate an intermediate level of HBV endemicity in the study population and support sustained antenatal screening, linkage to care, health education, HBV vaccination, and prevention of mother-to-child transmission services.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) are blood-borne infections with the potential for mother-to-child transmission and remain important concerns in antenatal care in sub-Saharan Africa. This study aimed to determine the seroprevalence of these infections among pregnant women in Ekiti South, Nigeria.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study recruited 710 pregnant women from six Local Government Areas in Ekiti South Senatorial District, Nigeria, to determine the seroprevalence of HBV, HCV, and HIV.
Core methods
Sociodemographic data and information on knowledge, awareness, and attitudes were collected using a structured questionnaire, and venous blood specimens were screened for hepatitis B surface antigen, anti-HCV antibodies, and HIV antibodies.
Main results
The overall seroprevalence was 2.5% (18/710) for HBV, 1.5% (11/710) for HIV, and 0% for HCV, with no co-infections detected. Age group was significantly associated with HIV infection (p < 0.001), whereas other sociodemographic variables were not significantly associated with HIV status or HBsAg positivity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate an intermediate level of HBV endemicity in the study population, supporting the need for sustained antenatal screening and prevention of mother-to-child transmission services.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the importance of continued antenatal screening and health education to prevent mother-to-child transmission of HBV in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design limits the ability to establish causality, and the sample may not be fully representative of all pregnant women in the region. The evidence strength is moderate due to the study design and sample size.

优化乙型肝炎筛查:ELISA 与快速检测的比较

Optimizing Hepatitis B screening: A comparison of ELISA and rapid tests
作者： Vero Hanitriniaina Harijaona Vololontsoa, Anselme Rakotoarivelo, Andry Rasamindrakotroka, Ny Onja Niaina Raharijaona, E. Verenako
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎仍然是一个主要的全球公共卫生问题,特别是在马达加斯加等高流行地区,该地区的 患病率 超过 8%。诊断 主要依赖于 ELISA 免疫酶技术,该技术因其可靠性而受到认可,但在资源有限的实验室中并未广泛使用。快速诊断测试(RDT)提供了一种实用的替代方案,但其在这方面的性能尚未得到评估。这项前瞻性描述性和分析性研究比较了 Virucheck® 快速检测和 Wantai Bio Pharm ELISA 在检测 乙型肝炎病毒 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 方面的性能。该样本由来自镰状细胞病患者和 JRA 大学医院随访受试者的 74 份血清样本组成。结果显示,TDR 的 血清流行率 为 6.75%,而 ELISA 的 血清流行率 为 9.45%。TDR的敏感性为71.41%,特异性为100%,而ELISA证实了其优越性,敏感性超过95%,特异性接近98%。除了技术表现之外,该研究还强调了年轻人口占主导地位,反映了母婴 传播 的重要性,以及与危险性行为相关的年轻人中值得注意的 传播。这些发现强调了早期和有针对性的筛查以及强化的 疫苗接种 策略的紧迫性。总之,马达加斯加优化乙型肝炎筛查依赖于快速检测(适合基于社区的筛查)和 ELISA 的互补使用,这对于诊断确认和临床随访至关重要。这一方法代表了在乙型肝炎高流行的情况下减少 传播、提高 传播 覆盖率和加强乙型肝炎防治工作的战略途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎仍然是一个主要的全球公共卫生问题,特别是在马达加斯加等高流行地区,该地区的 患病率 超过 8%。快速诊断测试提供了 ELISA 的实用替代方案,但其在这方面的性能尚未得到评估。
设计与对象
This prospective descriptive and analytical study compared the performance of the Virucheck® rapid test and the Wantai Bio Pharm ELISA for the detection of 乙型肝炎病毒 surface antigen.该样本由来自镰状细胞病患者和 JRA 大学医院随访受试者的 74 份血清样本组成。
核心方法
该研究将 Virucheck® 快速检测与 Wantai Bio Pharm ELISA 检测 乙型肝炎病毒 表面抗原进行了比较。计算包括敏感性和特异性在内的性能指标,以评估两种方法的诊断准确性。
主要结果
结果显示,快速诊断测试的 血清流行率 为 6.75%,而 ELISA 的 血清流行率 为 9.45%。快速诊断检测的敏感性为71.41%,特异性为100%,而ELISA证实了其优越性,敏感性超过95%,特异性接近98%。
解释与创新
该研究强调了以年轻人为主的人群,反映了母婴 传播 的重要性,以及与危险性行为相关的年轻人中值得注意的 传播。马达加斯加优化乙型肝炎筛查依赖于社区筛查快速检测和诊断确认 ELISA 的互补使用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了早期和有针对性的筛查以及强化的 疫苗接种 策略的紧迫性。这一方法代表了在乙型肝炎高流行的情况下减少 传播、提高 传播 覆盖率和加强乙型肝炎防治工作的战略途径。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制、偏差或混杂因素。由于数据仅具有抽象性质且 74 名参与者的样本量较小,因此证据强度被评估为中等。

Optimizing Hepatitis B screening: A comparison of ELISA and rapid tests

Authors: Vero Hanitriniaina Harijaona Vololontsoa, Anselme Rakotoarivelo, Andry Rasamindrakotroka, Ny Onja Niaina Raharijaona, E. Verenako
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B remains a major global public health problem, particularly in areas of high endemicity such as Madagascar, where prevalence exceeds 8%. Diagnosis relies primarily on the ELISA immunoenzymatic technique, which is recognized for its reliability but is not widely available in resource-limited laboratories. Rapid diagnostic tests (RDTs) offer a practical alternative, but their performance in this context has yet to be evaluated. This prospective descriptive and analytical study compared the performance of the Virucheck® rapid test and the Wantai Bio Pharm ELISA for the detection of hepatitis B virus surface antigen (HBsAg). The sample consisted of 74 serum samples from patients with sickle cell disease and subjects followed at the JRA University Hospital. The results showed a seroprevalence of 6.75% by TDR versus 9.45% by ELISA. The TDR had a sensitivity of 71.41% and a specificity of 100%, while the ELISA confirmed its superiority with sensitivities exceeding 95% and specificities close to 98%. Beyond technical performance, the study highlighted a predominantly young population, reflecting the significance of mother-to-child transmission, as well as a notable seroprevalence among young adults linked to risky sexual behaviors. These findings underscore the urgency of early and targeted screening, combined with a strengthened vaccination strategy. In conclusion, optimizing hepatitis B screening in Madagascar relies on the complementary use of rapid tests—suitable for community-based screening—and ELISA, which is essential for diagnostic confirmation and clinical follow-up. This approach represents a strategic pathway to reduce transmission, improve vaccination coverage, and strengthen the fight against hepatitis B in a context of high endemicity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B remains a major global public health problem, particularly in areas of high endemicity such as Madagascar, where prevalence exceeds 8%. Rapid diagnostic tests offer a practical alternative to ELISA, but their performance in this context has yet to be evaluated.
Design and population
This prospective descriptive and analytical study compared the performance of the Virucheck® rapid test and the Wantai Bio Pharm ELISA for the detection of hepatitis B virus surface antigen. The sample consisted of 74 serum samples from patients with sickle cell disease and subjects followed at the JRA University Hospital.
Core methods
The study compared the Virucheck® rapid test against the Wantai Bio Pharm ELISA for detecting hepatitis B virus surface antigen. Performance metrics including sensitivity and specificity were calculated to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of both methods.
Main results
The results showed a seroprevalence of 6.75% by rapid diagnostic test versus 9.45% by ELISA. The rapid diagnostic test had a sensitivity of 71.41% and a specificity of 100%, while the ELISA confirmed its superiority with sensitivities exceeding 95% and specificities close to 98%.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlighted a predominantly young population, reflecting the significance of mother-to-child transmission, as well as a notable seroprevalence among young adults linked to risky sexual behaviors. Optimizing hepatitis B screening in Madagascar relies on the complementary use of rapid tests for community-based screening and ELISA for diagnostic confirmation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the urgency of early and targeted screening, combined with a strengthened vaccination strategy. This approach represents a strategic pathway to reduce transmission, improve vaccination coverage, and strengthen the fight against hepatitis B in a context of high endemicity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, bias, or confounding factors. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the data and the small sample size of 74 participants.

埃塞俄比亚西北部贡德尔大学医学与健康科学专业学生的乙型肝炎预防实践及相关因素

Hepatitis B prevention practices and associated factors among medical and health sciences students at university of Gondar, Northwest Ethiopia
作者： Askal Admasie Dessie, Habtu Adane Aytolgn, Misganaw GuadieTiruneh, Amsalu Feleke Demssie
摘要中文翻译因此,本研究的目的是评估埃塞俄比亚西北部贡德尔大学医学和健康科学专业学生的乙型肝炎预防实践和相关因素。2025 年 3 月至 8 月,对贡德尔大学医学与健康科学专业的学生进行了一项基于机构的混合方法横断面研究。研究表明,医学与健康科学专业的学生对乙型肝炎预防实践的知识、态度和实践水平较低。最后,衷心感谢数据收集者和研究参与者为本论文的完成提供了宝贵的时间和数据。资助 这项研究没有得到任何公共、私人或非营利资助机构的支持。伦理批准和参与同意本研究是根据机构审查委员会的伦理标准和赫尔辛基宣言的原则进行的。贡德尔大学公共卫生研究所伦理审查委员会批准了本研究,并获得伦理审查,伦理审查号和日期分别为IPH3273/2025和2025年5月。参与者参与研究是自愿的,如果愿意,他们可以随时退出。摘要 据估计,埃塞俄比亚有超过 500 万人感染 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒,只有 41.54% 的人对 乙型肝炎病毒 感染预防有良好的做法。国内乙肝感染预防实践中存在研究和方法上的差距。采用分层抽样技术。使用自填问卷来收集数据。此外,还使用关键知情人访谈指南来收集医学院教师和学生的定性数据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
More than 5 million people in Ethiopia are living with chronic Hepatitis B virus, yet only 41.54% of individuals had good practice towards prevention, and there are research gaps on prevention practices in the country.
设计与对象
2025 年 3 月至 8 月,采用分层抽样的方式,对贡德尔大学 666 名医学与健康科学专业的学生进行了一项基于机构的混合方法横断面研究。
核心方法
通过自填问卷和对教师和学生的关键信息访谈收集数据,并使用双变量和多变量逻辑回归和归纳主题分析进行分析。
主要结果
51.8% (95% CI: 48.0, 55.6) of participants had good HBV prevention practice; being 20–24 years old (AOR 2.54), a Physiotherapy student (AOR 5.41), and having a favorable attitude (AOR 2.63) were significantly associated with good practice.
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明学生对乙型肝炎预防的知识、态度和实践较低,定性数据强调了认识不足、个人防护装备短缺以及对传统医学的依赖。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,大学和卫生部应提供实践培训,解决个人防护装备短缺问题,确保乙肝病毒筛查和 疫苗接种,并开展文化敏感的宣传活动。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制或超出研究横截面性质的证据强度的详细评估。

Hepatitis B prevention practices and associated factors among medical and health sciences students at university of Gondar, Northwest Ethiopia

Authors: Askal Admasie Dessie, Habtu Adane Aytolgn, Misganaw GuadieTiruneh, Amsalu Feleke Demssie
Original abstract (English)
Therefore, the aim of this study was to assess hepatitis B prevention practices and associated factors among medical and health sciences students at the University of Gondar, Northwest Ethiopia. An institution-based mixed-methods cross-sectional study was conducted among Medical and Health Sciences students at the University of Gondar from March to August 2025. The study revealed that knowledge, attitude and practice regarding HBV prevention practice is low among medical and health science students. Finally, my heartfelt thanks go to data collectors and study participants for providing valuable time and data for the accomplishment of this thesis. Funding This study was not supported by any public, private, or non-profit funding bodies. Ethical approval and consent to participate This study was conducted in accordance with the ethical standards of institutional review board and the principles of Declaration of Helsinki. The ethical review committee of institute of public health of University of Gondar approved the study and ethical clearance was obtained with ethical review number and date IPH3273/2025 and May 2025 respectively. Participants’ involvement in the study was voluntary and they could withdraw at any time if they want. Abstract It is estimated that more than 5 million people in Ethiopia are living with chronic Hepatitis B virus and only 41.54% of individuals had good practice towards hepatitis B virus infection prevention. There are research and methodological gap on the HBV infection prevention practices in the country. Stratified sampling technique was used. A self-administered questionnaire was used to collect the data. Furthermore, key informants interview guides were used to collect qualitative data among instructors and students at college of medicine a...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
More than 5 million people in Ethiopia are living with chronic Hepatitis B virus, yet only 41.54% of individuals had good practice towards prevention, and there are research gaps on prevention practices in the country.
Design and population
An institution-based mixed-methods cross-sectional study was conducted among 666 Medical and Health Sciences students at the University of Gondar from March to August 2025 using stratified sampling.
Core methods
Data were collected via a self-administered questionnaire and key informant interviews with instructors and students, analyzed using bi-variable and multivariable logistic regression and inductive thematic analysis.
Main results
51.8% (95% CI: 48.0, 55.6) of participants had good HBV prevention practice; being 20–24 years old (AOR 2.54), a Physiotherapy student (AOR 5.41), and having a favorable attitude (AOR 2.63) were significantly associated with good practice.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating low knowledge, attitude and practice regarding HBV prevention among students, with qualitative data highlighting inadequate awareness, PPE shortages, and a reliance on traditional medicine.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the University and Ministry of Health should provide practical training, address PPE shortages, ensure HBV screening and vaccination, and create culturally sensitive awareness campaigns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations or a detailed assessment of evidence strength beyond the cross-sectional nature of the study.

核苷(酸)类似物治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的纵向肾功能变化和试点线粒体表观遗传学分析

Longitudinal Renal Function Changes and Pilot Mitochondrial Epigenetic Analysis in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients Treated with Nucleos(t)ide Analogues
作者： Güliz Uyar Güleç, Sevin Kırdar, Ceylan Ak, Mehtap Kılıç Eren, Hüseyin Örün
摘要中文翻译目的:长期使用核苷(酸)类似物(NA)与线粒体功能障碍和潜在不良反应有关。本研究旨在评估已治疗和未接受 治疗 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的肾毒性,并探索 DNA 甲基转移酶 1 (DNMT1) 表达作为潜在的表观遗传机制。材料和方法:接受替诺福韦二吡呋酯治疗的成人 CHB 患者富马酸盐(TDF)或恩替卡韦(ETV)≥12个月包括“治疗”以下组,而未经治疗的患者形成“治疗-naïve”组。回顾性分析基线和第3、6和12个月时的肾脏参数。使用线性混合效应模型评估肾小球滤过率(GFR)的纵向变化。外周血中DNMT1表达评估单核细胞作为初步分析。结果:总共分析了 158 名患者;91 例接受了治疗,其中 67 例未接受 治疗 治疗。治疗组的中位 GFR 值从基线时的 102.3 显着下降至第 12 个月时的 102.1 (p=0.012)。在纵向分析中,每个额外的时间点均与平均 GFR 下降 1.65 mL/min/1.73m 2 相关(95% 置信区间:-3.13 至-0.16;p=0.030)。年龄与 GFR 降低独立相关(β=-0.75 每年;p<0.001)。在 TDF 和 ETV 之间未观察到肾脏结果存在显着差异。在初步表观遗传学分析 (n=25) 中,治疗组和未接受 治疗 治疗的患者之间的 DNMT1 表达具有可比性(中位数 1.1789 与 ÖZAmaç: Nükleos(t)id 类似物 (NA) uzun süreli kullanımı、mitokondriyal işlev bozukluğu 和 olasıyan etkilerle ilişkilendirilmiştir、tedavi altındaki 和 tedavi-naiv kronik 乙型肝炎 (KHB) hastalarında。nefrotoksisitenin değerlendirilmesi ve potansiyel epigenetik mekanizma olarak DNA metiltransferaz 1 (DNMT1) ekspresyonunun araştırılması amaçlanmıştır.Gereç ve Yöntemler:替诺福韦二索普西延胡索 (TDF) 维雅恩替卡韦 (ETV) tedavisini ≥12 ay boyunca alan Yetişkin KHB hastaları“tedavi altındaki”grubu oluştururken,tedavi görmemiş hastalar ise“tedavinaiv”grubu oluşturdu.Başlangıçta ve 3.,6. aylarda ölçülen böbrek parametreleri Retrospektif olarak analiz edildi.Glomerüler filtrasyon hızındaki (GFR) uzunlamasına değişiklikler,doğrusal karışık etki modellemesi kullanılarak değerlendirildi.Pilot çalışma olarak,periferik kan mononükleer hücrelerinde DNMT1 ekspresyonu değerlendirildi.Bulgular:
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
长期使用核苷(酸)类似物与线粒体功能障碍和潜在的不良反应有关。本研究旨在评估经治疗和未接受过 治疗 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的肾毒性,并探索 DNA 甲基转移酶 1 表达作为潜在的表观遗传机制。
设计与对象
接受富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯或恩替卡韦治疗至少 12 个月的成人 慢性乙型肝炎 患者构成 治疗 治疗不足组,而未经治疗的患者构成 治疗 初治组。总共对 158 名患者进行了分析,其中包括 91 名接受过治疗的患者和 67 名未接受过 治疗 治疗的患者。
核心方法
回顾性分析基线以及第 3、6 和 12 个月的肾脏参数,并使用线性混合效应模型评估肾小球滤过率的纵向变化。作为初步分析,评估了外周血单核细胞中 DNA 甲基转移酶 1 的表达。
主要结果
治疗组的中位肾小球滤过率值从基线时的 102.3 显着下降至第 12 个月时的 102.1。纵向分析显示,每个额外的时间点都与平均 GFR 下降 1.65 mL/min/1.73m2 相关,并且年龄与 GFR 下降独立相关。富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯和恩替卡韦之间的肾脏结局没有观察到显着差异,并且组间 DNA 甲基转移酶 1 表达具有可比性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为,随着时间的推移,治疗患者的肾功能显着下降,其中年龄被确定为独立因素。该研究表明,虽然存在肾毒性,但所研究的两种特定核苷(酸)类似物之间没有显着差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为接受长期核苷(酸)类似物治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的肾功能监测提供了谨慎的信息。结果表明,无论使用何种特定药物,都需要对肾脏健康保持警惕。
局限与证据强度
由于回顾性设计和可用数据仅抽象的性质,证据强度中等。试点表观遗传学分析仅限于 25 名患者的小样本量。

Longitudinal Renal Function Changes and Pilot Mitochondrial Epigenetic Analysis in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients Treated with Nucleos(t)ide Analogues

Authors: Güliz Uyar Güleç, Sevin Kırdar, Ceylan Ak, Mehtap Kılıç Eren, Hüseyin Örün
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Long-term use of nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) has been associated with mitochondrial dysfunction and potential adverse effects.This study aimed to evaluate nephrotoxicity in treated and treatment-naïve chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients and to explore DNA methyltransferase 1 (DNMT1) expression as a potential epigenetic mechanism.Materials and Methods: Adult CHB patients receiving tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) or entecavir (ETV) for ≥12 months comprised the "under-treatment'" group, while untreated patients formed the "treatment-naïve" group.Renal parameters at baseline and months 3, 6, and 12 were analyzed retrospectively.Longitudinal changes in glomerular filtration rate (GFR) were assessed using linear mixed-effects modeling.DNMT1 expression in peripheral blood mononuclear cells was evaluated as a pilot analysis.Results: A total of 158 patients were analyzed; 91 were treated and 67 were treatment-naïve.Median GFR values declined significantly from 102.3 at the baseline to 102.1 at the 12 th month in the treated group (p=0.012).In longitudinal analysis, each additional time point was associated with a mean GFR decline of 1.65 mL/min/1.73m 2 (95% confidence interval: -3.13 to -0.16; p=0.030).Age was independently associated with a reduction in GFR (β=-0.75 per year; p<0.001).No significant differences in renal outcomes were observed between TDF and ETV.In the pilot epigenetic analysis (n=25), DNMT1 expression was comparable between treated and treatment-naïve patients (median 1.1789 vs. ÖZAmaç: Nükleos(t)id analoglarının (NA) uzun süreli kullanımı, mitokondriyal işlev bozukluğu ve olası yan etkilerle ilişkilendirilmiştir. Bu çalışmada, tedavi altındaki ve tedavi-naiv kronik hepatit B (KHB) hastalarında nefrotoksisitenin değerlendirilmesi ve potansiyel bir epigenetik mekanizma olarak DNA metiltransferaz 1 (DNMT1) ekspresyonunun araştırılması amaçlanmıştır.Gereç ve Yöntemler: Tenofovir disoproksil fumarat (TDF) veya entekavir (ETV) tedavisini ≥12 ay boyunca alan yetişkin KHB hastaları "tedavi altındaki" grubu oluştururken, tedavi görmemiş hastalar ise "tedavinaiv" grubu oluşturdu.Başlangıçta ve 3., 6. aylarda ölçülen böbrek parametreleri retrospektif olarak analiz edildi.Glomerüler filtrasyon hızındaki (GFR) uzunlamasına değişiklikler, doğrusal karışık etki modellemesi kullanılarak değerlendirildi.Pilot çalışma olarak, periferik kan mononükleer hücrelerinde DNMT1 ekspresyonu değerlendirildi.Bulgular:
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Long-term use of nucleos(t)ide analogues has been associated with mitochondrial dysfunction and potential adverse effects. This study aimed to evaluate nephrotoxicity in treated and treatment-naïve chronic hepatitis B patients and to explore DNA methyltransferase 1 expression as a potential epigenetic mechanism.
Design and population
Adult chronic hepatitis B patients receiving tenofovir disoproxil fumarate or entecavir for at least 12 months comprised the under-treatment group, while untreated patients formed the treatment-naïve group. A total of 158 patients were analyzed, including 91 treated and 67 treatment-naïve individuals.
Core methods
Renal parameters at baseline and months 3, 6, and 12 were analyzed retrospectively, and longitudinal changes in glomerular filtration rate were assessed using linear mixed-effects modeling. DNA methyltransferase 1 expression in peripheral blood mononuclear cells was evaluated as a pilot analysis.
Main results
Median glomerular filtration rate values declined significantly from 102.3 at baseline to 102.1 at the 12th month in the treated group. Longitudinal analysis showed each additional time point was associated with a mean GFR decline of 1.65 mL/min/1.73m2, and age was independently associated with a reduction in GFR. No significant differences in renal outcomes were observed between tenofovir disoproxil fumarate and entecavir, and DNA methyltransferase 1 expression was comparable between groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating a significant decline in renal function over time in treated patients, with age identified as an independent factor. The study suggests that while nephrotoxicity is present, it does not differ significantly between the two specific nucleos(t)ide analogues studied.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings cautiously inform the monitoring of renal function in chronic hepatitis B patients undergoing long-term nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy. The results suggest that vigilance regarding kidney health is warranted regardless of the specific agent used.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the retrospective design and the abstract-only nature of the available data. The pilot epigenetic analysis was limited to a small sample size of 25 patients.

金/银/铂注入二氧化硅对乙型肝炎药物恩替卡韦作用的理论研究

Effects of Silica Infused with Gold/Silver/Platinum on the Hepatitis B Drug Entecavir by Theoretical Investigation
作者： Mohan Munirajulu, Prasath Manivannan, Aksshita Sreerambabu, Vetrivelan Vaithiyanathan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Chronic hepatitis B virus infection remains a deadly global health issue affecting around 275 million individuals despite the availability of effective vaccines. This study investigated the adsorption behavior of the hepatitis B drug Entecavir on silica infused with gold, silver, and platinum nanocomposites to explore potential drug delivery improvements.
设计与对象
所提供的证据并未报告具体的研究设计、背景、日期或人类或动物种群。该研究利用涉及密度泛函理论和分子对接模拟的理论计算方法来分析药物恩替卡韦和注入金属的二氧化硅纳米复合材料之间的相互作用。
核心方法
该研究采用与 B3LYP 泛函相关的密度泛函理论,结合 6-311++g(d,p) 和 LANL2DZ 的组合基组来优化几何结构并计算吸附能。进行自然键轨道和分子静电势分析以评估电子转移和活性位点,同时进行分子对接以评估与蛋白质 3OX8 和 3OXR 的结合相互作用。
主要结果
恩替卡韦通过其 NH2 基团吸附在银、金和铂注入的二氧化硅上,平衡校正能量分别为 -60.13、-39.39 和 -48.33 kcal/mol。分子对接显示,药物-纳米复合物有效结合蛋白3OX8和3OXR,恩替卡韦和铂配合物的结合能分别为-4.87、-6.11、-9.72和-9.35 kcal/mol,形成多个氢键。
解释与创新
作者将电子从纳米复合材料到药物的转移和有利的结合能解释为二氧化硅作为电子供体的作用的确认以及复合物稳定性的增强。新颖之处在于理论证明金属掺杂的二氧化硅纳米复合材料可以为恩替卡韦的有效吸附创造活性位点,并有可能提高其生物活性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,注入金、银或铂的二氧化硅可以作为恩替卡韦的有效纳米载体。这一理论见解可能为未来开发优化的药物递送系统提供信息,以增强 治疗 对 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的功效。
局限与证据强度
证据强度较低,因为该研究完全是理论和计算的,缺乏体外或体内验证。提供的证据没有报告纳米复合材料的实验数据、样本大小或物理合成,这限制了确认计算的相互作用的生物相关性的能力。

Effects of Silica Infused with Gold/Silver/Platinum on the Hepatitis B Drug Entecavir by Theoretical Investigation

Authors: Mohan Munirajulu, Prasath Manivannan, Aksshita Sreerambabu, Vetrivelan Vaithiyanathan
Original abstract (English)

(Eds.), Methods and Protocols (pp. Abstract The new deoxyguanine nucleoside analogue Entecavir (ETV) specifically inhibits the replication of the hepatitis B virus. Around 275 million individuals globally continue to be affected by chronic hepatitis B virus infections, which can be deadly despite the presence of effective vaccines. The medical field has employed drug delivery clusters based on nanocomposite. Therefore, we have investigated the adsorption behaviour of the hepatitis B drug ETV on silica infused with Au/Ag/Pt nano cluster composites (NCCs) using DFT linked to the B3LYP functional with combined basis set of 6-311 + + g(d, p)&LANL2DZ. ETV adsorbs on Ag/Au/Pt...SiO2 via its NH2 group with counterpoise-corrected energies of − 60.13, − 39.39, and − 48.33 kcal/mol, respectively. NBO analysis indicates electron transfer from Au/Ag/ Pt...SiO2 to the NH2 group of ETV, with net charges of 0.082|e|, 0.105|e|, and 0.032|e|, respectively, confirming SiO2 acts as an electron donor. MEP analysis reveals that doped metals in ETV@Ag/Au/Pt...SiO2 create active sites for drug adsorption, with electrophilic regions located on O, F, and N atoms of ETV and nucleophilic zones on C and H atoms, highlighting the potential interaction sites between the drug and NCCs. Molecular docking revealed that ETV@Ag/Au/Pt...SiO2 effectively binds to proteins 3OX8 and 3OXR, with binding energies of − 4.87, − 6.11, − 9.72, and − 9.35 kcal/mol for ETV and ETV@Pt...SiO2, respectively, and mul...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus infection remains a deadly global health issue affecting around 275 million individuals despite the availability of effective vaccines. This study investigated the adsorption behavior of the hepatitis B drug Entecavir on silica infused with gold, silver, and platinum nanocomposites to explore potential drug delivery improvements.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not report a specific study design, setting, dates, or human or animal population. The research utilized a theoretical computational approach involving density functional theory and molecular docking simulations to analyze the interaction between the drug Entecavir and metal-infused silica nanocomposites.
Core methods
The study employed density functional theory linked to the B3LYP functional with a combined basis set of 6-311++g(d,p) and LANL2DZ to optimize geometry and calculate adsorption energies. Natural Bond Orbital and Molecular Electrostatic Potential analyses were conducted to assess electron transfer and active sites, while molecular docking was performed to evaluate binding interactions with proteins 3OX8 and 3OXR.
Main results
Entecavir adsorbed on silver, gold, and platinum-infused silica via its NH2 group with counterpoise-corrected energies of -60.13, -39.39, and -48.33 kcal/mol, respectively. Molecular docking revealed that the drug-nanocomposite complexes effectively bind to proteins 3OX8 and 3OXR, with binding energies of -4.87, -6.11, -9.72, and -9.35 kcal/mol for Entecavir and the platinum complex, respectively, forming multiple hydrogen bonds.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the electron transfer from the nanocomposites to the drug and the favorable binding energies as confirmation of silica's role as an electron donor and the enhanced stability of the complexes. The novelty lies in the theoretical demonstration that metal-doped silica nanocomposites can create active sites for effective Entecavir adsorption and potentially improve its biological activity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that silica infused with gold, silver, or platinum could serve as effective nanocarriers for the delivery of Entecavir. This theoretical insight may inform the future development of optimized drug delivery systems to enhance the treatment efficacy for chronic hepatitis B virus infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is low because the study is entirely theoretical and computational, lacking in vitro or in vivo validation. The supplied evidence does not report experimental data, sample sizes, or physical synthesis of the nanocomposites, which limits the ability to confirm the biological relevance of the calculated interactions.

多组学和人工智能识别肝细胞癌感染相关亚型和预后特征

Multiomics and artificial intelligence identify infection associated subtypes and prognostic signatures in hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Peng Wang, Jiayan Fang, Wenna Zhao, Yijuan Huang

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肝细胞癌 (HCC) 仍然是全球主要的健康负担,其发病率和 死亡率 持续居高不下,与 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV)/丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 感染相关的肿瘤微环境失调会导致肿瘤进展和不良后果。该研究旨在解决许多现有 HCC 预后模型没有系统地将感染相关分子特征与免疫和基因组特征整合在一起的局限性。
设计与对象
本研究基于多 队列研究 转录组、体细胞 突变、免疫浸润以及公共数据库(包括 TCGA-LIHC、ICGC-LIRI-JP 和 GEO-GSE14520)的临床数据,建立了一个综合预测框架。预后风险模型是在 TCGA-LIHC 培训 队列研究 中开发的,并在 ICGC-LIRI-JP 和 GSE14520 队列研究 中进行外部验证。
核心方法
研究人员构建了一个全面的 248 个基因感染相关集,涵盖 HBV/HCV 感染、抗病毒药物 反应和免疫调节,并通过无监督共识聚类确定了两种强大的 HCC 分子亚型。他们使用三种互补的机器学习算法筛选了 12 个核心预后基因,并使用曲线下面积 (AUC) 值、风险比和决策曲线分析来评估模型。
主要结果
该研究确定了两种具有不同感染状态注释、免疫浸润特征、基因组改变特征和临床预后的 HCC 分子亚型。预后风险模型在 TCGA-LIHC、ICGC-LIRI-JP 和 GSE14520 队列研究 中的 1 年 AUC 值分别为 0.82、0.78 和 0.75,并显示出 HCC 的独立预后价值,风险比为 2.87(P < 0.001)。
解释与创新
结果表明感染相关分子特征、肿瘤微环境重塑和 HCC 预后之间存在关联。作者将这些发现解释为表明感染相关特征可能支持回顾性 HCC 风险分层。
科研与公卫意义
与感染相关的特征可能支持回顾性 HCC 风险分层,与传统的临床病理模型相比,为感染相关的 HCC 人群的预测性能提供潜在的改进。在常规临床应用之前需要进行前瞻性验证。
局限与证据强度
由于依赖回顾性 队列研究 以及未来前瞻性研究的需要,证据强度中等。

Multiomics and artificial intelligence identify infection associated subtypes and prognostic signatures in hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Peng Wang, Jiayan Fang, Wenna Zhao, Yijuan Huang

In this study, we established an integrated predictive framework based on multi-cohort transcriptomic, somatic mutation, immune infiltration, and clinical data, together with artificial intelligence (AI)-assisted analysis. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) remains a major global health burden with persistently high morbidity and mortality, and dysregulation of the tumor microenvironment associated with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV)/hepatitis C virus (HCV) infection contributes to tumor progression and adverse outcomes. The study aimed to address the limitation that many existing HCC prognostic models do not systematically integrate infection-associated molecular signatures with immune and genomic features.
Design and population
This study established an integrated predictive framework based on multi-cohort transcriptomic, somatic mutation, immune infiltration, and clinical data from public databases including TCGA-LIHC, ICGC-LIRI-JP, and GEO-GSE14520. The prognostic risk model was developed in the TCGA-LIHC training cohort and externally validated in ICGC-LIRI-JP and GSE14520 cohorts.
Core methods
The researchers constructed a comprehensive 248-gene infection-associated set covering HBV/HCV infection, antiviral responses, and immune regulation, and identified two robust HCC molecular subtypes through unsupervised consensus clustering. They screened 12 core prognostic genes using three complementary machine learning algorithms and evaluated the model using area under the curve (AUC) values, hazard ratios, and decision curve analysis.
Main results
The study identified two HCC molecular subtypes with distinct infection-status annotations, immune infiltration features, genomic alteration profiles, and clinical prognosis. The prognostic risk model achieved 1-year AUC values of 0.82, 0.78, and 0.75 in the TCGA-LIHC, ICGC-LIRI-JP, and GSE14520 cohorts, respectively, and showed independent prognostic value for HCC with a hazard ratio of 2.87 (P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest associations among infection-associated molecular characteristics, tumor microenvironment remodeling, and HCC prognosis. The authors interpret the findings as indicating that the infection-associated signature may support retrospective HCC risk stratification.
Scientific and public-health significance
The infection-associated signature may support retrospective HCC risk stratification, offering potential improvements in predictive performance for infection-associated HCC populations compared to traditional clinicopathological models. Prospective validation is required before routine clinical application.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on retrospective cohorts and the need for future prospective studies.

血清流行率 COVID-19 大流行之前和期间的 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 趋势:北塞浦路斯一家三级医院的五年回顾性数据

Seroprevalence Trends of HBV, HCV, and HIV Before and During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Five-Year Retrospective Data from a Tertiary Hospital in Northern Cyprus
作者： Behich Koyutourk, Melis Kalaycı, Ertan Direnç, Kaya Süer
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:评估北塞浦路斯 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 的五年血清反应性趋势,重点关注 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 和大流行后时期。材料和方法:这项回顾性横断面研究评估了 2020 年至 2024 年间 278,016 例乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg)、抗 HCV 和抗 HIV 血清学检测结果。所有检测均在 ARCHITECT Plus ci4100 系统(美国伊利诺伊州芝加哥雅培实验室)上使用酶联免疫吸附测定技术进行。对不同年份和性别组进行比较。结果还根据 HCV 和 HIV 的信号截止 (S/CO) 比以及 HBsAg 的 IU/mL 进行分类。结果:HBsAg 总体血清反应性为 1.2%,抗 HCV 为 0.6%,抗 HIV 为 0.2%。HBV 血清反应性从 2020 年的 1.5% 显着下降至 2023-2024 年的 1.0% (p=0.001)。HCV 患病率 保持在 1% 以下,较 2020 年无显着增加。0.5% 至 0.7% (p=0.192);大多数阳性样本属于低 S/CO 类别。HIV 患病率 在整个研究期间保持稳定在 0.2% (p=0.043)。结论:HBV血清反应性多年来呈下降趋势;HCV水平仍处于低位,但呈上升趋势;HIV 水平保持稳定且较低。结果表明北塞浦路斯仍然是低流行地区,并强调了持续 监测 和预防在后 COVID-19 时代的重要性。需要谨慎解释结果,因为它们可能无法完全表明感染水平。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估北塞浦路斯 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 的五年血清反应趋势,重点关注 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 和大流行后时期。
设计与对象
这项回顾性横断面研究评估了 2020 年至 2024 年间北塞浦路斯一家三级医院的 278,016 份血清学检测结果。
核心方法
乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg)、抗 HCV 和抗 HIV 检测是在 ARCHITECT Plus ci4100 系统上使用酶联免疫吸附测定技术进行的,计算并比较了不同年份和性别组的年度血清反应性率。
主要结果
HBsAg 的总体血清反应率为 1.2%,抗 HCV 的总体血清反应率为 0.6%,抗 HIV 的总体血清反应率为 0.2%;HBV 血清反应性从 2020 年的 1.5% 显着下降至 2023-2024 年的 1.0% (p=0.001),而 HCV 患病率 无显着增加,从 0.5% 增至 0.7% (p=0.192),HIV 患病率 保持稳定在 0.2% (p=0.043)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明北塞浦路斯仍然是一个低流行地区,并指出乙型肝炎病毒血清反应性呈下降趋势,丙型肝炎病毒水平呈上升趋势,并且艾滋病毒水平稳定在低水平。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了在后 COVID-19 时代持续 监测 和预防这些血源性感染的努力的重要性。
局限与证据强度
作者表示,需要谨慎解释结果,因为它们可能无法完全表明感染水平,并且由于回顾性设计和对单个中心血清学数据的依赖,证据被评估为中等。

Seroprevalence Trends of HBV, HCV, and HIV Before and During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Five-Year Retrospective Data from a Tertiary Hospital in Northern Cyprus

Authors: Behich Koyutourk, Melis Kalaycı, Ertan Direnç, Kaya Süer
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND/AIMS: To assess five-year seroreactivity trends of hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) in Northern Cyprus, with emphasis on the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) and post-pandemic periods. MATERIALS AND METHODS:This retrospective cross-sectional study evaluated 278,016 serological test results for hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), anti-HCV, and anti-HIV between 2020 and 2024.All tests were carried out using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay technology on the ARCHITECT Plus ci4100 system (Abbott Laboratories, Chicago, IL, USA).The annual rate of seroreactivity was calculated and compared across years and sex groups.The results were also categorised based on signal-to-cut-off (S/CO) ratios for HCV and HIV and on IU/mL for HBsAg. RESULTS:Overall seroreactivity rates were 1.2% for HBsAg, 0.6% for anti-HCV, and 0.2% for anti-HIV.HBV seroreactivity declined significantly from 1.5% in 2020 to 1.0% in 2023-2024 (p=0.001).HCV prevalence remained below 1%, increasing non-significantly from 0.5% to 0.7% (p=0.192);most positive samples fell within the low S/CO category.HIV prevalence remained stable at 0.2% across the study period (p=0.043). CONCLUSION:HBV seroreactivity exhibited a declining trend over the years; HCV levels remained low but exhibited a rising trend; HIV levels remained stable and low.The results indicate that Northern Cyprus remains a low-endemic region and highlight the importance of sustained surveillance and prevention in the post-COVID-19 era.The results need to be interpreted cautiously, as they may not entirely indicate the levels of infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess five-year seroreactivity trends of hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) in Northern Cyprus, with emphasis on the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) and post-pandemic periods.
Design and population
This retrospective cross-sectional study evaluated 278,016 serological test results from a tertiary hospital in Northern Cyprus between 2020 and 2024.
Core methods
Tests for hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), anti-HCV, and anti-HIV were performed using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay technology on the ARCHITECT Plus ci4100 system, with annual rates of seroreactivity calculated and compared across years and sex groups.
Main results
Overall seroreactivity rates were 1.2% for HBsAg, 0.6% for anti-HCV, and 0.2% for anti-HIV; HBV seroreactivity declined significantly from 1.5% in 2020 to 1.0% in 2023-2024 (p=0.001), while HCV prevalence increased non-significantly from 0.5% to 0.7% (p=0.192) and HIV prevalence remained stable at 0.2% (p=0.043).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that Northern Cyprus remains a low-endemic region, noting a declining trend in HBV seroreactivity, a rising trend in HCV levels, and stable low levels of HIV.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the importance of sustained surveillance and prevention efforts for these blood-borne infections in the post-COVID-19 era.
Limitations and evidence strength
The authors stated that results need to be interpreted cautiously as they may not entirely indicate the levels of infection, and the evidence is assessed as moderate due to the retrospective design and reliance on serological data from a single center.

石蒜碱与甘草酸超分子自组装成纳米药物,用于安全、持续的抗乙肝治疗

Supramolecular Self-Assembly of Lycorine with Glycyrrhizic Acid into a Nanodrug for Safe and Sustained Anti-HBV Therapy
作者： Hang Sun, Zi-Dong He, Bei-Bei Jia, Bo Yuan, Ling-Chuan Kong, Rui-Xian Xu, Qiong Wang, Wen-Jing Pan, Hao Chen, Shao-Xing Dai
摘要中文翻译为 慢性乙型肝炎 实现 功能性治愈 仍然是一个重大挑战,因为传统疗法难以消除乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 并随后恢复适应性 抗病毒药物 免疫力。Lycorine (LY) 是一种具有广谱 抗病毒药物 活性的化合物,在抑制 HBsAg 分泌方面表现出特别的前景。然而,其临床应用因显着的细胞毒性、诱导细胞凋亡、半衰期短和体内不良反应而受到严重限制。为了克服这些障碍并增强其治疗潜力,我们通过将 LY 与天然抗 HBV 药物甘草酸共组装来设计了一种自组装纳米药物 (LGN)。这种策略不仅可以保护 LY 免于过早降解并使其能够在结肠中持续释放,而且还可以显着减弱 LY 引起的全身毒性。值得注意的是,LGN 有效抑制 HBsAg 分泌 (IC 50 = 2.15 ± 0.09 μM),其选择性指数 (SI > 93) 比单独使用 LY (SI = 39.26) 高出两倍多。此外,LGN 同时抑制 HBV DNA 复制、pgRNA 转录以及病毒蛋白 HBc 和 HBx 的表达,表明与核苷(酸)类似物不同的多靶点、宿主 依赖性机制。治疗 在 HDI 模型中使用这种纳米药物进一步证明了其对 HBV 的治疗潜力且具有明显的安全性。进一步的转录组学研究证实,LGN 诱导独特的 宿主 转录程序,该程序破坏 HBV 生命周期的多个关键阶段,包括病毒进入、共价闭合环状 DNA 维持、基因转录和免疫逃避。总的来说,这些发现为利用 LY 等天然产物进行 抗病毒药物 治疗提供了宝贵的见解,并强调了自组装技术在改善药物安全性和药代动力学特征方面的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为 慢性乙型肝炎 实现 功能性治愈 仍然是一个重大挑战,因为传统疗法难以消除乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 并随后恢复适应性 抗病毒药物 免疫力。
设计与对象
该研究通过将石蒜碱与甘草酸共组装,设计了一种自组装纳米药物(LGN),以克服石蒜碱的局限性,增强其抗乙肝病毒的治疗潜力。
核心方法
该策略涉及将石蒜碱与甘草酸共同组装,以创建一种纳米药物,可保护石蒜碱免于过早降解,使其能够在结肠中持续释放,并减弱全身毒性。
主要结果
LGN 有效抑制 HBsAg 分泌 (IC 50 = 2.15 ± 0.09 μM),选择性指数 (SI > 93) 比单独使用石蒜碱 (SI = 39.26) 高两倍以上,同时抑制 HBV DNA 复制、pgRNA 转录以及病毒蛋白 HBc 和 HBx 的表达。
解释与创新
作者解释说,LGN 诱导了一种独特的 宿主 转录程序,该程序破坏了 HBV 生命周期的多个关键阶段,包括病毒进入、共价闭合环状 DNA 维持、基因转录和免疫逃避,代表了一种与核苷(酸)类似物不同的多靶点、宿主 依赖机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为利用石蒜碱等天然产物进行 抗病毒药物 治疗提供了宝贵的见解,并强调了自组装技术在改善 HBV 治疗 药物安全性和药代动力学特征方面的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究证明了在 HDI 模型中针对 HBV 的治疗潜力和明显的安全性,但证据强度中等,因为它基于仅摘要的报告,没有详细的方法或长期临床数据。

Supramolecular Self-Assembly of Lycorine with Glycyrrhizic Acid into a Nanodrug for Safe and Sustained Anti-HBV Therapy

Authors: Hang Sun, Zi-Dong He, Bei-Bei Jia, Bo Yuan, Ling-Chuan Kong, Rui-Xian Xu, Qiong Wang, Wen-Jing Pan, Hao Chen, Shao-Xing Dai
Original abstract (English)
Achieving a functional cure for chronic hepatitis B remains a major challenge, as conventional therapies struggle to eliminate the hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and subsequently restore adaptive antiviral immunity. Lycorine (LY), a compound with broad-spectrum antiviral activity, has shown particular promise inhibiting HBsAg secretion. However, its clinical application is severely limited by significant cytotoxicity, the induction of apoptosis, a short half-life, and adverse in vivo effects. To overcome these barriers and enhance its therapeutic potential, we designed a self-assembled nanomedicine (LGN) by co-assembling LY with glycyrrhizic acid, a natural anti-HBV agent. This strategy not only protected LY from premature degradation and enabled its sustained release in the colon but also markedly attenuated LY-induced systemic toxicity. Notably, LGN effectively suppressed HBsAg secretion (IC 50 = 2.15 ± 0.09 μM) with a selectivity index (SI > 93) over two-fold higher than that of LY alone (SI = 39.26). Furthermore, LGN concurrently inhibited HBV DNA replication, pgRNA transcription, and the expression of viral protein HBc and HBx, indicating a multitargeted, host-dependent mechanism distinct from nucleos(t)ide analogs. Treatment with this nanomedicine in the HDI model further demonstrated its therapeutic potential against HBV with apparent safety. Further transcriptomic study confirmed that LGN induces a distinct host transcriptional program that disrupts multiple critical stages of the HBV life cycle including viral entry, covalently closed circular DNA maintenance, gene transcription, and immune evasion. Collectively, these findings provide valuable insights into exploiting natural products like LY for antiviral therapy and underscore the potential of self-assembly technology in improving drug safety and pharmacokinetic profiles.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Achieving a functional cure for chronic hepatitis B remains a major challenge, as conventional therapies struggle to eliminate the hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and subsequently restore adaptive antiviral immunity.
Design and population
The study designed a self-assembled nanomedicine (LGN) by co-assembling lycorine with glycyrrhizic acid to overcome the limitations of lycorine and enhance its therapeutic potential against HBV.
Core methods
The strategy involved co-assembling lycorine with glycyrrhizic acid to create a nanomedicine that protected lycorine from premature degradation, enabled sustained release in the colon, and attenuated systemic toxicity.
Main results
LGN effectively suppressed HBsAg secretion (IC 50 = 2.15 ± 0.09 μM) with a selectivity index (SI > 93) over two-fold higher than that of lycorine alone (SI = 39.26), and concurrently inhibited HBV DNA replication, pgRNA transcription, and the expression of viral proteins HBc and HBx.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that LGN induces a distinct host transcriptional program that disrupts multiple critical stages of the HBV life cycle, including viral entry, covalently closed circular DNA maintenance, gene transcription, and immune evasion, representing a multitargeted, host-dependent mechanism distinct from nucleos(t)ide analogs.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide valuable insights into exploiting natural products like lycorine for antiviral therapy and underscore the potential of self-assembly technology in improving drug safety and pharmacokinetic profiles for HBV treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study demonstrates therapeutic potential against HBV with apparent safety in the HDI model, but the evidence strength is moderate as it is based on an abstract-only report without detailed methods or long-term clinical data.

接受 HBV 疫苗 的意愿及相关因素:对城市越南成年人实施 疫苗接种 态度检查量表

Willingness to accept the HBV vaccine and related factors: Administering the vaccination attitudes examination scale to Urban Vietnamese adults
作者： Nghi Hoc Ho, Tung Hoang, Cong Minh Le, Tram Thi Huyen Nguyen
摘要中文翻译越南成人乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率较低,对医疗保健系统构成威胁。为了减轻负面影响,必须确定 疫苗 的犹豫和拒绝程度及其社会人口统计学相关性。我们使用自填问卷和 疫苗接种 态度考试 (VAX) 量表对胡志明市的 926 名成年人(2025 年 1 月至 3 月)进行了横断面调查。进行描述性分析,并使用卡方检验探索变量之间的关联。多项逻辑回归确定了 疫苗 犹豫和拒绝的相关性,统计显着性设置为 p < 0.05。在分析一般 疫苗 意图(无论 疫苗接种 状态如何)时,65.6% 的参与者表示接受,19.3% 犹豫,15.1% 拒绝。总体 Cronbach’s alpha 为 0.833(范围 = 0.812–0.919),McDonald's omega 系数范围为 0.820 至 0.920。唯一性值 (0.115–0.422) 表明,显着的差异是由潜在因素解释的,显着的非标准化载荷为 0.94 到 1.31 (p < 0.001)。发现了一个独特的“知识悖论”:对 HBV 疫苗 的了解不足预示着彻底拒绝(AOR = 2.42),而较高的事实知识与对 疫苗 福利的不信任呈正相关(r = 0.23,p < 0.01),对未来影响的担忧(r = 0.19,p < 0.01)和总 VAX 评分(r = 0.19,p < 0.01)。这表明有限的知识会导致拒绝,而更多的事实知识则与“知情怀疑论”或谨慎犹豫并存。疫苗 拒绝主要与对自然免疫力的偏好有关,而对 疫苗 好处和商业担忧的不信任则与犹豫的增加特别相关。VAX 量表是评估越南城市成年人 疫苗 态度的可靠工具。它有助于识别与 疫苗 相关的关键问题,并支持对公众态度的持续监控,这对于设计有效的 疫苗 计划和增强公众对 疫苗 的信任至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
越南成人乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率较低,对医疗保健系统构成威胁。为了减轻负面影响,必须确定 疫苗 的犹豫和拒绝程度及其社会人口统计学相关性。
设计与对象
我们使用自填问卷和 疫苗接种 态度考试 (VAX) 量表对胡志明市的 926 名成年人(2025 年 1 月至 3 月)进行了横断面调查。
核心方法
进行描述性分析,并使用卡方检验探索变量之间的关联。多项逻辑回归确定了 疫苗 犹豫和拒绝的相关性,统计显着性设置为 p < 0.05。
主要结果
在分析一般 疫苗 意图(无论 疫苗接种 状态如何)时,65.6% 的参与者表示接受,19.3% 犹豫,15.1% 拒绝。疫苗 拒绝主要与对自然免疫力的偏好有关,而对 疫苗 好处和商业担忧的不信任则与犹豫的增加特别相关。
解释与创新
发现了一个独特的“知识悖论”:对 HBV 疫苗 的了解不足预示着彻底拒绝,而较高的事实知识与对 疫苗 福利的不信任、对未来影响的担忧以及 VAX 总分呈正相关。这表明有限的知识会导致拒绝,而更多的事实知识则与“知情的怀疑论”或谨慎的犹豫并存。
科研与公卫意义
VAX 量表是评估越南城市成年人 疫苗 态度的可靠工具。它有助于识别与 疫苗 相关的关键问题,并支持对公众态度的持续监控,这对于设计有效的 疫苗 计划和增强公众对 疫苗 的信任至关重要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告具体的局限性或偏见。总体Cronbach's alpha为0.833(范围= 0.812-0.919),McDonald's omega系数范围为0.820至0.920,表明证据强度较高。

Willingness to accept the HBV vaccine and related factors: Administering the vaccination attitudes examination scale to Urban Vietnamese adults

Authors: Nghi Hoc Ho, Tung Hoang, Cong Minh Le, Tram Thi Huyen Nguyen
Original abstract (English)
Vietnam has low hepatitis B vaccination coverage among adults, posing a threat to the healthcare system. To mitigate negative impacts, it is essential to identify vaccine hesitancy and refusal levels and their sociodemographic correlates. We conducted a cross-sectional survey of 926 adults in Ho Chi Minh City (January–March 2025) using a self-administered questionnaire and the Vaccination Attitudes Examination (VAX) scale. Descriptive analysis was performed, and associations between variables were explored using a chi-square test. Multinomial logistic regression identified correlates of vaccine hesitancy and refusal, statistical significance was set at p < 0.05. When analyzing general vaccine intentions regardless of vaccination status, 65.6% of participants indicated acceptance, 19.3% hesitancy, and 15.1% refusal. The overall Cronbach’s alpha was 0.833 (range = 0.812–0.919), and the McDonald’s omega coefficients ranged from 0.820 to 0.920. The uniqueness values (0.115–0.422) indicated that substantial variances were explained by latent factors, with significant unstandardized loadings of 0.94 to 1.31 ( p < 0.001). A unique “knowledge paradox” was identified: poor knowledge about the HBV vaccine predicted outright refusal (AOR = 2.42), whereas higher factual knowledge was positively correlated with mistrust of vaccine benefits (r = 0.23, p < 0.01), worries about future effects (r = 0.19, p < 0.01), and total VAX scores (r = 0.19, p < 0.01). This suggests that limited knowledge contribute to refusal, while greater factual knowledge coexisting with “informed skepticism” or cautious hesitancy. Vaccine refusal was primarily associated with a preference for natural immunity, while mistrust of vaccine benefits and commercial concerns were specifically linked to increased hesitancy. The VAX scale serves as a reliable instrument for assessing vaccine attitudes among urban Vietnamese adults. It helps identify key vaccine-related concerns and supports continuous monitoring of public attitudes, which is essential for designing effective vaccination programs and enhancing public trust in vaccines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vietnam has low hepatitis B vaccination coverage among adults, posing a threat to the healthcare system. To mitigate negative impacts, it is essential to identify vaccine hesitancy and refusal levels and their sociodemographic correlates.
Design and population
We conducted a cross-sectional survey of 926 adults in Ho Chi Minh City (January–March 2025) using a self-administered questionnaire and the Vaccination Attitudes Examination (VAX) scale.
Core methods
Descriptive analysis was performed, and associations between variables were explored using a chi-square test. Multinomial logistic regression identified correlates of vaccine hesitancy and refusal, statistical significance was set at p < 0.05.
Main results
When analyzing general vaccine intentions regardless of vaccination status, 65.6% of participants indicated acceptance, 19.3% hesitancy, and 15.1% refusal. Vaccine refusal was primarily associated with a preference for natural immunity, while mistrust of vaccine benefits and commercial concerns were specifically linked to increased hesitancy.
Interpretation and novelty
A unique 'knowledge paradox' was identified: poor knowledge about the HBV vaccine predicted outright refusal, whereas higher factual knowledge was positively correlated with mistrust of vaccine benefits, worries about future effects, and total VAX scores. This suggests that limited knowledge contribute to refusal, while greater factual knowledge coexisting with 'informed skepticism' or cautious hesitancy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The VAX scale serves as a reliable instrument for assessing vaccine attitudes among urban Vietnamese adults. It helps identify key vaccine-related concerns and supports continuous monitoring of public attitudes, which is essential for designing effective vaccination programs and enhancing public trust in vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or bias. The overall Cronbach's alpha was 0.833 (range = 0.812–0.919), and the McDonald's omega coefficients ranged from 0.820 to 0.920, indicating high evidence strength.

HIV感染者开始抗逆转录病毒治疗后的多基因风险评分和药物性肝损伤的风险。

Polygenic risk score and risk of drug-induced liver injury after initiating antiretroviral therapy in people living with HIV.
作者： Zinhle Cindi, Katie M. Cardone, Yuki Bradford, Molly A. Hall, Hannah Kim, Eric S. Daar, Roy Gulick, Sharon A Riddler, Marijana Vujkovic, Kyong-Mi Chang
摘要中文翻译简介:药物引起的肝损伤会影响一些在抗逆转录病毒治疗 (ART) 期间感染艾滋病毒 (PWH) 的人。我们检查了 ART 期间多基因风险评分 (PRS) 与肝损伤的关联。方法:我们使用退伍军人事务部百万退伍军人计划 (MVP) 和宾夕法尼亚大学医学生物库 (PMBB) 的汇总统计数据得出 PRS。我们为来自全球推进临床治疗 (ACTG) 网络的 ART 初始研究的 3,619 名 治疗 初始参与者构建了非洲 (AFR) 和欧洲 (EUR) 血统的 PRS。肝损伤定义为 48 周内发生 3 级或更高级别的丙氨酸氨基转移酶 (ALT)。通过多变量回归模型评估关联性。结果:与较高基线 ALT 值相关的临床变量包括乙型或丙型肝炎病毒感染、BMI 和年龄增加以及男性。在对 1,897 名 EUR 参与者(28 例,1869 名对照)和 1722 名 AFR 参与者(21 例,1701 名对照)进行的肝损伤分析中,HBV 与 EUR 和 AFR 参与者的肝损伤相关,HCV 与 EUR 参与者的肝损伤相关(均 p < 0.001)。PRSALT 与 EUR 或 AFR 参与者的肝损伤事件并不独立相关。结论:未观察到基线 PRSALT 与开始 ART 后的肝损伤之间存在关联。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
药物性肝损伤会影响一些艾滋病毒感染者 (PWH) 在抗逆转录病毒治疗 (ART) 期间的情况。我们检查了 ART 期间多基因风险评分 (PRS) 与肝损伤的关联。
设计与对象
我们为来自全球推进临床治疗 (ACTG) 网络的 ART 初始研究的 3,619 名 治疗 初始参与者构建了非洲 (AFR) 和欧洲 (EUR) 血统的 PRS。
核心方法
我们使用退伍军人事务部百万退伍军人计划 (MVP) 和宾夕法尼亚大学医学生物库 (PMBB) 的汇总统计数据得出 PRS。肝损伤定义为 48 周内发生 3 级或更高级别的丙氨酸氨基转移酶 (ALT)。通过多变量回归模型评估关联性。
主要结果
与较高基线 ALT 值相关的临床变量包括乙型或丙型肝炎病毒感染、BMI 和年龄增加以及男性。在对 1,897 名 EUR 参与者(28 例,1869 名对照)和 1722 名 AFR 参与者(21 例,1701 名对照)进行的肝损伤分析中,HBV 与 EUR 和 AFR 参与者的肝损伤相关,HCV 与 EUR 参与者的肝损伤相关(均 p < 0.001)。PRSALT 与 EUR 或 AFR 参与者的肝损伤事件并不独立相关。
解释与创新
未观察到基线 PRSALT 与开始 ART 后的肝损伤之间存在关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,ALT 的遗传风险评分可能无法预测开始 ART 的 PWH 中的药物性肝损伤,这凸显了 HBV 和 HCV 感染等临床因素在监测肝脏健康方面的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到肝损伤病例数量较少的限制,尤其是 AFR 组,这可能会降低统计功效。由于观察设计和结果样本量有限,证据强度中等。

Polygenic risk score and risk of drug-induced liver injury after initiating antiretroviral therapy in people living with HIV.

Authors: Zinhle Cindi, Katie M. Cardone, Yuki Bradford, Molly A. Hall, Hannah Kim, Eric S. Daar, Roy Gulick, Sharon A Riddler, Marijana Vujkovic, Kyong-Mi Chang
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Drug-induced liver injury affects some people living with HIV (PWH) during antiretroviral therapy (ART). We examined associations of polygenic risk scores (PRS) with liver injury during ART. METHODS: We derived PRS using summary statistics from the Veterans Affairs Million Veteran Program (MVP) and the Penn Medicine BioBank (PMBB). We constructed PRS for individuals of African (AFR) and European (EUR) ancestry in 3,619 treatment-naïve participants from ART-naive studies of the Advancing Clinical Therapeutics Globally (ACTG) network. Liver injury was defined as incident grade 3 or greater alanine aminotransferase (ALT) within 48 weeks. Associations were evaluated by multivariable regression models. RESULTS: Clinical variables associated with higher baseline ALT values included hepatitis B or C virus infection, increasing BMI and age, and male sex. In analyses of liver injury among 1,897 EUR participants (28 cases, 1869 controls) and 1722 AFR participants (21 cases, 1701 controls), HBV was associated with liver injury in both EUR and AFR participants, and HCV in EUR participants (p < 0.001 for each). PRSALT was not independently associated with incident liver injury in either EUR or AFR participants. CONCLUSION: No association was observed between baseline PRSALT and subsequent liver injury following initiation of ART.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Drug-induced liver injury affects some people living with HIV (PWH) during antiretroviral therapy (ART). We examined associations of polygenic risk scores (PRS) with liver injury during ART.
Design and population
We constructed PRS for individuals of African (AFR) and European (EUR) ancestry in 3,619 treatment-naïve participants from ART-naive studies of the Advancing Clinical Therapeutics Globally (ACTG) network.
Core methods
We derived PRS using summary statistics from the Veterans Affairs Million Veteran Program (MVP) and the Penn Medicine BioBank (PMBB). Liver injury was defined as incident grade 3 or greater alanine aminotransferase (ALT) within 48 weeks. Associations were evaluated by multivariable regression models.
Main results
Clinical variables associated with higher baseline ALT values included hepatitis B or C virus infection, increasing BMI and age, and male sex. In analyses of liver injury among 1,897 EUR participants (28 cases, 1869 controls) and 1722 AFR participants (21 cases, 1701 controls), HBV was associated with liver injury in both EUR and AFR participants, and HCV in EUR participants (p < 0.001 for each). PRSALT was not independently associated with incident liver injury in either EUR or AFR participants.
Interpretation and novelty
No association was observed between baseline PRSALT and subsequent liver injury following initiation of ART.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that genetic risk scores for ALT may not predict drug-induced liver injury in PWH starting ART, highlighting the importance of clinical factors like HBV and HCV infection in monitoring liver health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by the small number of liver injury cases, particularly in the AFR group, which may reduce statistical power. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and limited sample size for the outcome.

通过 spRandom-seq 对福尔马林固定石蜡包埋组织进行空间总 RNA 测序。

Spatial Total RNA Sequencing of Formalin-Fixed Paraffin-Embedded Tissue by spRandom-seq.
作者： Yuan Liao, Junbing Chen, Shunji Zhang, Yuexiao Lyu, Bin Zhu, Haide Chen, Yuyi Zhu, Ling Dong, Qian Liang, Jiong Liu
摘要中文翻译传染病和癌症的分子 发病机制 是由纳米级的 宿主 和组织微环境中的微生物 RNA 转录本精心策划的。然而,在空间上解析复杂临床组织(如福尔马林固定石蜡包埋(FFPE)标本)内的全面转录景观仍然面临着巨大的挑战。在这里,我们提出了 spRandom-seq,一种基于随机引物的空间总 RNA 测序技术,旨在空间解析来自 宿主、细菌甚至 FFPE 组织中纳米级病毒的完整转录组。利用随机引物设计,我们的技术不仅促进了小鼠大脑和嗅球中特定 lncRNA 和选择性剪接事件的发现,而且还描​​绘了临床 FFPE 切片中明显的空间异质性 - 跨越乳腺癌的不同肿瘤区域和肺炎克雷伯菌感染组织的微生物感染部位。重要的是,对 宿主 和 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 阳性肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的 FFPE 样本中的病毒 RNA 进行综合分析表明,补体和凝血途径在广泛的 HBV 感染肿瘤区域中被特异性激活,这也表现出拷贝数变异 (CNV) 负担增加。由于其与现有空间转录组学平台的兼容性和最小的操作复杂性,spRandom-seq 代表了临床病理学应用和感染诊断的实用且可扩展的方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了复杂临床组织(如福尔马林固定石蜡包埋(FFPE)标本)中空间解析综合转录景观的挑战,以了解传染病和癌症中的分子 发病机制。
设计与对象
该研究提出了 spRandom-seq,这是一种空间总 RNA 测序技术,适用于小鼠大脑、嗅球、乳腺癌、肺炎克雷伯菌感染组织和 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 阳性肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的 FFPE 组织。
核心方法
spRandom-seq 使用基于随机引物的设计来空间解析 FFPE 组织中 宿主、细菌和纳米级病毒的完整转录组,从而能够发现 lncRNA、选择性剪接事件和空间异质性分析。
主要结果
在 HBV 阳性 HCC 样本中,宿主 和病毒 RNA 的综合分析揭示了 HBV 感染肿瘤区域补体和凝血途径的特异性激活,并增加了拷贝数变异 (CNV) 的负担。
解释与创新
作者将 spRandom-seq 解释为一种实用且可扩展的临床病理学和感染诊断方法,利用其与现有平台的兼容性和最小的操作复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现能够对 HCC 中的 HBV 感染进行详细的空间转录组分析,有助于了解病毒 发病机制 以及在临床环境中的潜在诊断应用。
局限与证据强度
证据仅来自对方法严谨性和验证的抽象、有限的评估;然而,该技术已证明适用于多种组织类型,表明证据强度中等。

Spatial Total RNA Sequencing of Formalin-Fixed Paraffin-Embedded Tissue by spRandom-seq.

Authors: Yuan Liao, Junbing Chen, Shunji Zhang, Yuexiao Lyu, Bin Zhu, Haide Chen, Yuyi Zhu, Ling Dong, Qian Liang, Jiong Liu
Original abstract (English)
The molecular pathogenesis of infectious diseases and cancer is orchestrated by nanoscale of host and microbial RNA transcripts within the tissue microenvironment. Nevertheless, spatially resolving the comprehensive transcriptional landscape within complex clinical tissues, like formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded (FFPE) specimens, still poses a formidable challenge. Here, we present spRandom-seq, a random primer-based spatial total RNA sequencing technology designed to spatially resolve complete transcriptomes from host, bacteria, and even nanoscale viruses in FFPE tissues. Capitalizing on the random primer design, our technology not only facilitated the discovery of specific lncRNAs and alternative splicing events in mouse brain and olfactory bulb, but also delineated pronounced spatial heterogeneity in clinical FFPE sections-across distinct tumor regions in breast cancer and microbial infection sites in Klebsiella pneumoniae-infected tissues. Importantly, integrated analysis of host and viral RNAs in FFPE samples from hepatitis B virus (HBV)‐positive hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) demonstrated that complement and coagulation pathways were specifically activated across expansive HBV‐infected tumor areas, which also exhibited an increased burden of copy number variations (CNVs). Owing to its compatibility with existing spatial transcriptomics platforms and minimal operational complexity, spRandom-seq represents a practical and scalable approach for clinical pathology applications and infection diagnostics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the challenge of spatially resolving comprehensive transcriptional landscapes in complex clinical tissues like formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded (FFPE) specimens to understand molecular pathogenesis in infectious diseases and cancer.
Design and population
The research presents spRandom-seq, a spatial total RNA sequencing technology applied to FFPE tissues from mouse brain, olfactory bulb, breast cancer, Klebsiella pneumoniae-infected tissues, and hepatitis B virus (HBV)-positive hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).
Core methods
spRandom-seq uses a random primer-based design to spatially resolve complete transcriptomes from host, bacteria, and nanoscale viruses in FFPE tissues, enabling discovery of lncRNAs, alternative splicing events, and spatial heterogeneity analysis.
Main results
In HBV-positive HCC samples, integrated analysis of host and viral RNAs revealed specific activation of complement and coagulation pathways across HBV-infected tumor areas, with an increased burden of copy number variations (CNVs).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret spRandom-seq as a practical and scalable approach for clinical pathology and infection diagnostics, leveraging its compatibility with existing platforms and minimal operational complexity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings enable detailed spatial transcriptomic analysis of HBV infection in HCC, informing understanding of viral pathogenesis and potential diagnostic applications in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is derived solely from the abstract, limiting assessment of methodological rigor and validation; however, the technology's demonstrated applicability to multiple tissue types suggests moderate evidence strength.

与代谢紊乱相关的肝细胞癌的肿瘤发生,重点关注 Hippo 通路。

Tumorigenesis of Hepatocellular Carcinoma Related to Metabolic Disorders With a Focus on the Hippo Pathway.
作者： Haruto Nishida, Keiko Matsuura, Tsutomu Daa
摘要中文翻译Hippo 通路与肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的肿瘤发生有关。我们的目的是利用免疫组织化学染色确定Hippo通路相关蛋白在HCC中的表达,并比较具有不同潜在临床背景疾病的患者之间的数据。我们收集了 40 例切除的 HCC 福尔马林固定石蜡包埋样本,并根据既往病史将其分为 4 组 [分别为 17 例饮酒样本 (HCC-A),以及 6、8 和 9 例 乙型肝炎病毒 感染样本 (HCC-B)、丙型肝炎病毒感染 (HCC-C) 和代谢性疾病样本 (HCC-M)]。使用免疫组织化学,我们研究了 Hippo 通路相关蛋白 YAP1、CADM1 和 SOX9。高血压是肝癌患者中最常见的疾病(72.5%),其次是糖尿病(47.5%)。关于背景肝脏疾病,与 HCC-B (16.7%) 和 HCC-C (0%) 组相比,HCC-A (35.3%) 和 HCC-M (88.9%) 组观察到明显的脂肪性肝炎 (SH)。HCC-M 组(88.9%)还观察到代谢功能障碍相关性 SH(MASH)的显着发现,而肝硬化主要在 HCC-C 组(100%)中观察到。27 名患者处于 Hippo 不活跃状态,13 名患者处于活跃状态。SH 在 Hippo 不活动的 HCC-M 组 (100%) 中比 HCC-A 组 (50.0%) 更占主导地位。Hippo 不活跃组的平均肿瘤大小明显大于 Hippo 活跃组。如果 Hippo 通路失活,Hippo 通路相关蛋白可以作为 MASH 和 MASH 相关 HCC 的潜在治疗靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定 Hippo 通路相关蛋白在肝细胞癌 (HCC) 中的表达,并比较具有不同潜在临床背景疾病的患者之间的数据,因为 Hippo 通路与 HCC 肿瘤发生有关。

我们收集了 40 例切除的 HCC 福尔马林固定石蜡包埋样本,并根据既往病史将其分为 4 组:17 例饮酒样本(HCC-A)、6 例 乙型肝炎病毒 感染样本(HCC-B)、8 例丙型肝炎病毒感染样本(HCC-C)和 9 例代谢性疾病样本(HCC-M)。

使用免疫组织化学,我们研究了收集的 HCC 样本中的 Hippo 通路相关蛋白 YAP1、CADM1 和 SOX9。

高血压是肝癌患者中最常见的疾病(72.5%),其次是糖尿病(47.5%)。与 HCC-B (16.7%) 和 HCC-C (0%) 组相比,HCC-A (35.3%) 和 HCC-M (88.9%) 组观察到明显的脂肪性肝炎 (SH)。HCC-M 组(88.9%)还观察到代谢功能障碍相关性 SH(MASH)的显着发现,而肝硬化主要在 HCC-C 组(100%)中观察到。
